## 550009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かつて、この地には<line-break>魔物が住みついておった。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>すべてを石に変えてしまう<line-break>恐ろしい魔物じゃ。<end>"
EN: "Long ago, a monster made its lair in this land. It was a fearsome creature that turned everything to stone.<end>"
KO: "옛날 이 땅에는 마물이 살고 있었다. 모든 것을 돌로 바꿔 버리는 무서운 마물이었지.<end>"

## 550010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこへ１人の勇者が現れ、<line-break>この神殿に魔物を封じた。<line-break>その勇者が使った剣があれじゃ。　　　<line-break>地中深く刺さったその剣は、<line-break>いかなる怪力の持ち主にも<line-break>引き抜くことができん。<end>"
EN: "Then a hero appeared and sealed the monster within this temple. That is the sword the hero wielded. Its blade lies buried deep in the earth, and no one, however mighty, can pull it free.<end>"
KO: "그곳에 한 용사가 나타나 이 신전에 마물을 봉인했다. 그 용사가 사용한 검이 바로 저것이다. 땅속 깊이 박힌 그 검은 아무리 괴력을 가진 자라도 뽑을 수 없다.<end>"

## 550011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この世を救う使命を負った勇者だけが、<line-break>あの剣を引き抜くことができるという…<line-break>古くからの言い伝えじゃ。<end>"
EN: "It is said that only the Hero charged with saving the world can draw that sword. So the old legend goes.<end>"
KO: "이 세상을 구할 사명을 짊어진 용사만이 그 검을 뽑을 수 있다는… 오래된 전승이란다.<end>"

## 550012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%2今までに何人があの剣に挑んだかねぇ。<line-break>でも成功したためしは一度もないよ。<end>あの娘は領主様の１人娘ノエルさ。<line-break>身体は小さいけど、剣の腕は確かだよ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%2Who knows how many people have tried to pull out that sword? Not one has ever succeeded.<end>That girl is Noel, the lord's only daughter. She may be small, but she's a fine swordswoman.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%2지금까지 몇 명이나 저 검에 도전했는지 몰라. 하지만 성공한 사람은 한 번도 없었어.<end>저 아이는 영주님의 외동딸 노엘이야. 몸은 작아도 검 솜씨는 확실하지.<end>"

## 550013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ときどき、こうやって村の力自慢や<line-break>通りがかりの冒険者が挑戦するのさ。<line-break>それをひやかすのが、わしの道楽でね。<end>"
EN: "Every so often, one of the village strongmen or a passing adventurer gives it a try. Heckling them is a little hobby of mine.<end>"
KO: "가끔 이렇게 마을의 힘센 사람이나 지나가던 모험가가 도전하지. 그걸 구경하며 놀리는 게 내 취미라네.<end>"

## 550014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3ノエル様は、足におケガをなされた　　<line-break>お父上の代わりに、ときどき、付近の<line-break>怪物退治などにも活躍されている。<line-break>剣の腕もたつ、なかなか利発な<line-break>お嬢さんだ。しかし、惜しいことだ。<line-break>男だったら、竜騎士にもなれたものを。<end>ここの領主様は、<line-break>かつてこの地を荒らした石化獣を　　<line-break>倒した勇者の子孫さ。<line-break>つまり、ノエル様は<line-break>あの剣の持ち主の子孫というわけだ。<line-break>きっと、引き抜くことができるよ。<end>ノエル様からは<line-break>かねがね、英雄の気を感じていたのだ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3Lady Noel sometimes takes her injured father's place and helps slay monsters in the surrounding area. Her father injured his foot, and she is intelligent and highly skilled with a sword. A pity, though. Had she been a man, she might even have become a Dragon Knight.<end>The lord here is descended from the hero who defeated the Petrifying Beast that once ravaged this land. In other words, Lady Noel is a descendant of that sword's owner. I'm sure she can pull it out.<end>I have long sensed the spirit of a hero in Lady Noel.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3노엘 님은 다리를 다치신 아버님을 대신해 가끔 근처 괴물 퇴치에서도 활약하고 계셔. 검술 솜씨도 뛰어난 꽤 영리한 아가씨지. 하지만 아까워. 남자였다면 용기사도 될 수 있었을 텐데.<end>이곳 영주님은 예전에 이 땅을 황폐하게 만든 석화수를 쓰러뜨린 용자의 후손이야. 그러니까 노엘 님은 저 검 주인의 후손이라는 거지. 분명 뽑아낼 수 있을 거야.<end>노엘 님에게서는 오래전부터 영웅의 기운을 느끼고 있었어.<end>"

## 550015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな地図にも載っていないような<line-break>珍しい村で、こんな珍しい風習があったなんて。<line-break>実際に世界を旅してないとわからなかったなあ。<line-break>あ、私は数が減って、珍しい存在になった<line-break>コーンスの旅人です。<line-break>う～ん、自分の目で確かめないとなあ。<end>"
EN: "Who would have thought a rare little village, one that isn't even on the map, would have such an unusual custom? I'd never have known without actually traveling the world. Oh, I'm a traveler of the Konsu, who have grown so few that we're a rare sight ourselves. Hmm. You really do have to see things with your own eyes.<end>"
KO: "이런 지도에도 실리지 않은 희귀한 마을에 이런 특이한 풍습이 있었다니. 직접 세계를 돌아다니지 않았다면 몰랐겠군. 아, 나는 수가 줄어 희귀한 존재가 된 콘스 여행자야. 음, 역시 직접 눈으로 확인해야겠어.<end>"

## 550018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この人たち、みんな同じ刺青してる！<line-break>どういうこと？<end>"

KO: "이 사람들, 전부 같은 문신을 하고 있어! 어떻게 된 거야?<end>"

## 550019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうね、実際に見て、確かめなくちゃ。<line-break>オッシ先生もそう言ってたしね。<line-break><line-break>行きましょ、<value:$28>。<line-break>あいつらが何してるか、確かめて<line-break>悪いことなら、くいとめなきゃ。<end>"
EN: "Right. We have to see for ourselves and make sure. Master Ossi said the same thing. Come on, <value:$28>. Let's find out what they're doing, and if it's something bad, we have to stop it.<end>"
KO: "그래, 직접 보고 확인해야지. 오시 선생님도 그렇게 말했으니까. 가자, <value:$28>. 녀석들이 뭘 하는지 확인해서 나쁜 짓이면 막아야 해.<end>"

## 550020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見てよ！　この人たち、<line-break>みんな、同じ刺青してる…。<line-break><line-break>天空神ノトゥーンの紋章…？<line-break>いや、ちょっと違う。<line-break>どこか、まがまがしい…。<end>"
EN: "Look! All these people have the same tattoo... The emblem of Notun, the Sky God? No, it's a little different. There's something sinister about it.<end>"
KO: "봐! 이 사람들 모두 같은 문신을 하고 있어… 천공신 노툰의 문장…? 아니, 조금 달라. 어딘가 불길해…<end>"

## 550021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん、行ってみようよ。<line-break>すっごく、気になるしさ。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, let's go see. I'm dying to know what this is about.<end>"
KO: "응, 가 보자. 엄청 신경 쓰이기도 하고.<end>"

## 550022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>13…みな同じ刺青がある。　<line-break>…天空神の紋章に似た模様。<line-break>そうか、これが…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>15ねえ、<value:$28>、<line-break>みんな同じ刺青があるよ。<line-break><line-break>ほら、テラネにもある天空神の<line-break>紋章に似てるけど…<line-break>なんか、気味が悪いね…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>14なんと！　　　　　<line-break>どいつもこいつも<line-break>同じ刺青をしておる。<end><if><value:$01><equal>19ねぇ、見て、見て、見てぇ！<line-break>みんな同じ目玉の　　　　　<line-break>刺青してるよぉ！<end>姉ちゃん、大発見だ！<line-break>こいつら、みんな同じ刺青してる。<line-break><line-break>なんだろう、この模様…。<line-break>目玉みたいだ！<end>"

Don't blame me if you regret it after you're dead.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Aye. Let us go and see.<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Yeah. Want to go take a look?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Yeah, Sis. Let's go see.<end>Yeah. Let's go see.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>13…그만둬. 너 같은 풋내기가 끼어들 일이 아니다. …라고 해도 갈 생각이겠지? 죽고 나서 후회해도 난 모른다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14그렇구나. 가 보자.<end><if><value:$01><equal>19그래. 가 보자?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10그래, 누나. 가 보자.<end>그래. 가 보자.<end>"

## 550025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど、ね。　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "So that's it.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요.<end>"

## 550026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そろって、同じ刺青してやがる。<line-break>しかも物騒な模様だぜ、これは。<end>"
EN: "Every one of them has the same tattoo. And that's one nasty-looking design.<end>"
KO: "모두 똑같은 문신을 했군. 게다가 꽤 흉흉한 문양이야.<end>"

## 550027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…施文院だ。<end>"
EN: "The Inscription Order.<end>"
KO: "…시문원이다.<end>"

## 550028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "説明はまたの機会だ。<line-break>連中を追っかけようぜ。<line-break><line-break>石化獣…とかいうのが<line-break>この村には封印されているそうだな。<line-break>急がないとヤバいかもしれない。<end>"
EN: "I'll explain another time. Let's go after them. I hear some creature called the Petrifying Beast is sealed in this village. We could be in trouble if we don't hurry.<end>"
KO: "설명은 다음 기회에 하지. 놈들을 쫓자. 석화수…라는 게 이 마을에 봉인돼 있다더군. 서두르지 않으면 위험할지도 몰라.<end>"

## 550029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "＜無＞<end>"
EN: "<end>"
KO: "＜없음＞<end>"

## 550031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、みんな同じ刺青だわ。<line-break>ちょっと、個性がないかも。きゃっ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, they all have the same tattoo. Not much individuality there. Eek!<end>"
KO: "아, 다들 같은 문신이네. 조금 개성이 없을지도. 꺅!<end>"

## 550032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この危険そうな奴らを追いかけるって…<line-break>どうしよっかな…。<line-break><line-break>ついていこうっと！　でもでも<line-break>こんな危険に飛び込もうとするなんて、<line-break>ちょっと、おバカさんかも！　きゃっ！<end>"
EN: "Follow those dangerous-looking people? What should I do...? I'll tag along! But anyone who'd dive headfirst into danger like this might be a bit of an idiot! Eek!<end>"
KO: "이 위험해 보이는 녀석들을 쫓아간다고… 어떡하지…. 따라가야지! 하지만 이런 위험 속으로 뛰어들려고 하다니 조금 바보일지도! 꺄악!<end>"

## 550033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見ろよ！　<value:$28>！<line-break>こいつらみんな同じ刺青してる！<line-break><line-break>へへっ、わかったぜ！<line-break>ってことは、つまり…。<end>"
EN: "Look, <value:$28>! These guys all have the same tattoo! Heh, I've got it! Which means...<end>"
KO: "봐, <value:$28>! 이 녀석들 전부 같은 문신을 하고 있어! 헤헤, 알겠다! 그러니까 이건….<end>"

## 550034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はやってるのかな？<end>"
EN: "Think it's the latest fashion?<end>"
KO: "유행하는 건가?<end>"

## 550035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よーし、行こうぜ。　　<line-break><value:$28>！<line-break><line-break>へへっ、<line-break>とめたって俺１人でも<line-break>行くつもりだったしな。<end>"
EN: "All right, let's go, <value:$28>! Heh. Even if you tried to stop me, I was planning to go alone.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 가자, <value:$28>! 헤헤, 말려도 나 혼자 갈 생각이었어.<end>"

## 550042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ～！<line-break>みんな同じ刺青してる。　　<line-break><line-break>でも、<line-break>あんまりきれいじゃないね。<line-break>ま、モノがモノだからね。<end>"
EN: "Oh! They all have the same tattoo. It's not very pretty, though. Well, given what it is, I suppose that's no surprise.<end>"
KO: "아~! 다들 같은 문신을 했네. 그런데 별로 예쁘진 않다. 뭐, 물건이 물건이니까.<end>"

## 550043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こういうのってさ、<line-break>か～なり、危険なんだよ？<end>"
EN: "You know this sort of thing is very dangerous, right?<end>"
KO: "이런 건 말이지, 꽤나 위험하다고?<end>"

## 550044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "危険だからこそ行く価値があるんだよ。<line-break>それじゃ、追跡、かいし～！<end>"
EN: "The danger is exactly what makes it worthwhile. And now, the chase begins!<end>"
KO: "위험하니까 갈 가치가 있는 거야. 그럼 추적, 시작~!<end>"

## 550062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんなの、なんなの、なんなのぉ！？<line-break>いやっ！　最悪、最悪よ、最悪よぉ～！<end>"
EN: "What is this? What is this? What is this?! No! This is the worst! The worst, the worst!<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 뭐야, 뭐냐고오!? 싫어! 최악, 최악이야, 최악이라고오~!<end>"

## 550063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そろって悪趣味な刺青してるわ！！<line-break>目玉よ、目玉！　め・だ・ま！<end>"
EN: "They've all got the same hideous tattoo! It's an eyeball! An eye-ball!<end>"
KO: "다 같이 취향 나쁜 문신을 하고 있잖아!! 눈알이야, 눈알! 누·운·알!<end>"

## 550064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…え？　<line-break>天空神ノトゥーンの紋章に似てる？<line-break><line-break>…そ、そんなこと、高貴なエルフの<line-break>アタクシには、ちゃんとわかっててよ！<line-break>ア、アタクシが言いたいのはぁ、別！<end>"
EN: "What? It resembles the emblem of Notun, the Sky God? O-of course I knew that! I am a noble Elf! I-I was making an entirely different point!<end>"
KO: "…뭐? 천공신 노툰의 문장과 닮았다고? 그, 그런 건 고귀한 엘프인 아타쿠시는 당연히 알고 있어! 내, 내가 말하려던 건 그게 아니라구!<end>"

## 550065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうよ、アタクシはあいつらの正体を<line-break>突き止めないとって言いたかったの！<line-break><line-break>も、もう！　アタクシのマネしないで！<line-break>ボケッとボサッとしてないで、<line-break>さっさと急いで追いかけるのよぉ！<end>"
EN: "Yes! I was about to say we must uncover who they really are! H-how dare you copy me! Stop standing there gawking and hurry after them!<end>"
KO: "그래, 난 녀석들의 정체를 밝혀내야 한다고 말하고 싶었던 거야! 아, 정말! 나 따라 하지 마! 멍하니 있지 말고 빨리 쫓아가란 말이야!<end>"

## 550066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、面妖な！<line-break>全員、同じ刺青…。<end>"
EN: "H-how strange! Every one of them bears the same tattoo...<end>"
KO: "괴, 괴이하군! 전부 같은 문신….<end>"

## 550067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兄弟子の意見が正しいでござる。　<end>"
EN: "My senior is right.<end>"
KO: "사형의 의견이 옳습니다.<end>"

## 550068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この機会を逃したら<line-break>あの奇怪な奴らの正体はわからぬ！<end>"
EN: "If we let this chance slip away, we shall never learn who those bizarre people are!<end>"
KO: "이 기회를 놓치면 저 기괴한 녀석들의 정체를 알 수 없다!<end>"

## 550069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、面目もござらぬ。<end>"
EN: "I-I am ashamed.<end>"
KO: "면, 면목이 없소이다.<end>"

## 550070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（みんな同じ刺青がある…）<end>"
EN: "(They all have the same tattoo...)<end>"
KO: "(모두 같은 문신이 있어…)<end>"

## 550071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（奴らを追いかけなくちゃ！）<end>"
EN: "(I have to follow them!)<end>"
KO: "(녀석들을 쫓아가야 해!)<end>"

## 550072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエルがやったぞ！<end>"
EN: "Noel did it!<end>"
KO: "노엘이 해냈다!<end>"

## 550073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勇者だ！　勇者の誕生だ！<end>"
EN: "A Hero! A Hero is born!<end>"
KO: "용사다! 용사의 탄생이다!<end>"

## 550074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つ、ついに<line-break>剣が抜けるときが訪れたか…。<end>"
EN: "C-can the time have finally come for the sword to be drawn...?<end>"
KO: "드, 드디어 검을 뽑을 때가 온 건가….<end>"

## 550075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "目にもとまらぬ<line-break>すばやさ、とはこのことだな。<line-break>だが、いくらすばやくても<line-break>攻撃がよめるんだよ。天使さん。<line-break>お前ら人形は単調だからな。<end>"
EN: "So this is what they call speed too fast for the eye to follow. But no matter how fast you are, I can read your attacks, angel. You dolls are too predictable.<end>"
KO: "눈에 보이지 않을 정도의 빠르기란 이런 거군. 하지만 아무리 빨라도 공격이 읽혀, 천사 씨. 너희 인형은 단조로우니까.<end>"

## 550076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "石化獣じゃ！<line-break>石化獣が復活しおった！<end>"
EN: "The Petrifying Beast! The Petrifying Beast has returned!<end>"
KO: "석화수다! 석화수가 부활했다!<end>"

## 550077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "た、助けてくれ！<end>"
EN: "H-help me!<end>"
KO: "사, 살려 줘!<end>"

## 550078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きゃ～っ！<end>"
EN: "Aaaah!<end>"
KO: "꺄~!<end>"

## 550079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な…！<line-break>遅かったか！<end>"
EN: "What...?! Too late?!<end>"
KO: "뭐…! 늦었나!<end>"

## 550080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴を追うぞ！<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "We're going after it, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "놈을 쫓자! <value:$28>!<end>"

## 550081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ソウルリープガキカナイ…。　　　<line-break>…無限ノ、ソウル…<line-break>他ノ対象ニモ予定ノ効果ハ現レナイ。<line-break>…シカシ障害ハコレニテ除去終了。<line-break>任務続行…。<end>"
EN: "SOUL LEAP INEFFECTIVE... INFINITE SOUL... INTENDED EFFECT ALSO FAILS ON OTHER TARGETS. HOWEVER, OBSTACLE REMOVAL COMPLETE. CONTINUING MISSION...<end>"
KO: "…소울 리프가 듣지 않는다…. …무한의 소울… 다른 대상에게도 예정된 효과는 나타나지 않는다. …그러나 장애물은 이것으로 제거 완료. 임무 속행….<end>"

## 550082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "追うわよ、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "We're going after that thing, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "쫓아가자, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 550083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の心配なんてしてないで<line-break>さっさと奴を追うの！<line-break>もう！　ほんっとにグズなんだからぁ！<end>"
EN: "Stop worrying about me and go after it already! Honestly, you're so slow!<end>"
KO: "내 걱정은 그만하고 빨리 녀석을 쫓아가! 정말! 답답해서 못 봐 주겠네!<end>"

## 550084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>22アイリーン、それはないよ。　　　　<line-break>少し、ひどいと思うなぁ。<line-break><value:$28>は君のこと<line-break>心配してくれてるんだよ？<line-break>やさしさ、理解してあげなきゃ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>15ねえ、アイリーン、それはないよ。　<line-break>少し、ひどいと思う。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>は君のこと<line-break>心配してくれてるんだよ？<line-break>やさしさ、理解してあげなきゃ。<end>アイリーン、ひどいよ。　　　　　<line-break>ルルアンタ、悲しい！<line-break>　<line-break>ねえ、<value:$28>は<line-break>アイリーンのこと<line-break>心配してくれてるんだよぉ？。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>22Irene, that's uncalled for. It's a little cruel. <value:$28> is worried about you, you know. You should appreciate that kindness.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Irene, that's uncalled for. It's a little cruel. <value:$28> is worried about you, you know. You should appreciate that kindness.<end>Irene, that's mean. Lulluantha is sad! <value:$28> is worried about you, you know?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>22아이린, 그건 아니야. 좀 심하다고 생각해. <value:$28>은 네 걱정을 해 주고 있잖아? 그 다정함을 이해해 줘야 해.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15있지, 아이린, 그건 아니야. 좀 심하다고 생각해. <value:$28>은 네 걱정을 해 주고 있잖아? 그 다정함을 이해해 줘야 해.<end>아이린, 너무해. 루루안타, 슬퍼! 있지, <value:$28>은 아이린 걱정을 해 주고 있는 거라구우?<end>"

## 550085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>22<value:$28>のこと<line-break>考えてるのはわかるけどさ。<line-break>きみ、彼の悪い保護者になってるよ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>15<value:$28>のこと考えてるの<line-break>わかるけど。君、<value:$28>の<line-break>悪い保護者になってるよ。　　　　　<end><value:$28>のこと考えてるの<line-break>わかるけど、もっと、<line-break>アイリーンも理解してあげなきゃ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>22I know you're thinking of <value:$28>, but you're being a bad guardian to him.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15I know you're thinking of <value:$28>, but you're being a bad guardian to <value:$28>.<end>I know you're thinking of <value:$28>, but you need to try harder to understand Irene too.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>22<value:$28>를 생각하는 건 알겠지만 말이야. 너, 그 애한테 나쁜 보호자가 되고 있어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15<value:$28>를 생각하는 건 알겠지만. 너, <value:$28>한테 나쁜 보호자가 되고 있어.<end><value:$28>를 생각하는 건 알겠지만, 아이린도 좀 더 이해해 줘야 해.<end>"

## 550086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>22ぼくたちは、大丈夫だよ。<line-break>ちょっと、意識が遠くなったけど。<line-break>　<line-break>さ、アイリーンの言うとおり<line-break>あいつを追わなきゃ、だよ！<line-break><value:$28>。<end><if><value:$01><equal>15僕らは、大丈夫だよ。<line-break>ちょっと、意識が遠くなったけど。<line-break>　<line-break>ねえ、アイリーンの言うとおり<line-break>あいつを追わなくちゃ。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>ルルアンタたちは、大丈夫だよぉ。<line-break>ちょっと、クラッとなったけど。<line-break>　<line-break>さ、アイリーンの言うとおり<line-break>あいつを追わなきゃ！<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>22We're all right. We nearly blacked out, that's all. Now, Irene's right: we have to go after that thing, <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15We're all right. We nearly blacked out, that's all. Irene's right: we have to go after that thing, <value:$28>.<end>Lulluantha and the others are all right! We just got a little dizzy. Irene's right: we have to go after that thing, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>22우린 괜찮아. 잠깐 의식이 멀어지긴 했지만. 자, 아이린 말대로 녀석을 쫓아가야 해! <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15우린 괜찮아. 잠깐 의식이 멀어지긴 했지만. 있지, 아이린 말대로 녀석을 쫓아가야 해. <value:$28>.<end>루루안타 일행은 괜찮아아. 잠깐 어질했을 뿐이야. 자, 아이린 말대로 녀석을 쫓아가야 해! <value:$28>.<end>"

## 550087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーン、過保護な母親みたいね！<line-break><value:$28>はマザコンの子供！<line-break>　<line-break>人間って個の自立が足りなくてよ。<line-break>甘えて、依存して、馴れ合って<line-break>それで友情とか絆とか言っちゃって！<end>"
EN: "Irene, you're like an overprotective mother! And <value:$28> has a mother complex! Humans have no sense of individual autonomy. You lean on each other, grow dependent, get far too familiar, and then call it friendship or a bond!<end>"
KO: "아이린, 과보호하는 엄마 같네! <value:$28>는 마마보이 꼬맹이! 인간은 개체의 자립이 부족해. 응석 부리고, 의존하고, 끼리끼리 어울리면서 그걸 우정이니 유대니 하잖아!<end>"

## 550088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "チャラチャラ親子ごっこしてないで<line-break>さ、行くわよ。<end>"
EN: "Enough of this silly game of parent and child. Come on, we're leaving.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 가벼운 가족놀이나 하지 말고, 가자.<end>"

## 550092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーン、それはないよ。　　　　　<line-break>少し、ひどいと思う。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>はキミのこと<line-break>心配してくれてるんだぞ？<line-break>やさしさ、理解してあげなきゃ。<end>"
EN: "Irene, that's uncalled for. It's a little cruel. <value:$28> is worried about you, you know. You should appreciate that kindness.<end>"
KO: "아이린, 그건 아니야. 조금 심하다고 생각해. <value:$28>는 널 걱정해 주는 거잖아? 그 상냥함을 알아줘야지.<end>"

## 550093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>のこと考えてるの<line-break>わかるけど。キミ、<value:$28>の<line-break>悪い保護者になってるよ。　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "I know you're thinking of <value:$28>, but you're being a bad guardian to <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 생각하는 건 알겠는데. 너, <value:$28>의 나쁜 보호자가 되고 있어.<end>"

## 550094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハハ、ボクたちは、大丈夫さ。<line-break>…ちょっと、意識が遠くなったけど。<end>"
EN: "Ha ha, we're all right. We nearly blacked out, that's all.<end>"
KO: "하하, 우리는 괜찮아. …조금 의식이 아득해졌지만.<end>"

## 550095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…やっぱ、ボクより先に<line-break>アイリーンのほうを心配する、よね。<end>"
EN: "You worried about Irene before me... I knew you would.<end>"
KO: "…역시 나보다 아이린부터 걱정하는구나.<end>"

## 550096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "아, 하하, 아이린 말대로 서둘러 저 녀석을 쫓아가야 해.<end>"

## 550100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう！　情けないんだからぁ！<line-break>さ、早く追わなきゃ！<end>"
EN: "Honestly, you're hopeless! Come on, we have to hurry after it!<end>"
KO: "정말! 한심하다니까! 자, 빨리 쫓아가야 해!<end>"

## 550101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺がこれで<line-break>黙っていると思ったか？<line-break>　<line-break>追うぞ！<line-break>逃がさん…。<end>"
EN: "You thought I'd let this go? We're going after it. It won't get away.<end>"
KO: "…내가 이대로 가만있을 줄 알았나? 쫓는다! 놓치지 않겠다….<end>"

## 550102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんじゃ？　なんじゃ？<line-break>一瞬、死んだ婆さんが見えたぞ！　　<end>"
EN: "What? What? For a moment, I saw the old woman who died!<end>"
KO: "뭐냐? 뭐냐? 순간 죽은 마누라가 보였다고!<end>"

## 550103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、追うぞ！<line-break>とにかく、危険な奴じゃということは<line-break>わかったからの！<end>"
EN: "We're going after it, <value:$28>! Whatever else it may be, we know it's dangerous!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 쫓아가자! 아무튼 위험한 놈이라는 건 알았으니까!<end>"

## 550104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>17ムキー！　ド下等生物の分際で<line-break>いい根性していてよぉぉ！　ムキー！<line-break>　<line-break>高貴で優雅なエルフのこのアタクシが<line-break>天誅加えてさしあげてよぉぉぉ！<line-break>地面にめり込むほど土下座させてやる！<end>ちっくしょう！<line-break>この俺を本気で怒らせやがったな！<line-break>　<line-break>涙もハナミズも枯れ果てるまで<line-break>泣くほど、後悔させてやるぜ！！<line-break>行くぜ、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>17Rrraah! Such insolence from a base creature! Rrraah! I, a noble and graceful Elf, shall deliver divine punishment! I'll make you grovel until you're buried in the ground!<end>Damn it! Now you've really made me angry! I'll make you regret it until you've cried yourself dry, tears and snot alike! Let's go, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>17끼익! 하등생물 주제에 배짱 하나는 좋구나아아! 끼익! 고귀하고 우아한 엘프인 이 몸이 천벌을 내려 주겠어어어! 땅에 처박힐 정도로 엎드려 빌게 해 주마!<end>젠장! 이 몸을 진짜로 화나게 했겠다! 눈물도 콧물도 마를 때까지 울 만큼 후회하게 해 주마!! 간다, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 550110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっは、ボ、ボクなら大丈夫だよ。<line-break>一瞬、死んだお母さんが見えたけどね。<end>"
EN: "Ah ha, I-I'm all right. Though for a moment I saw my dead mother.<end>"
KO: "아하, 나, 나는 괜찮아. 잠깐 죽은 엄마가 보이긴 했지만.<end>"

## 550111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうだね！<line-break>早く奴らを追わなきゃ！<end>"
EN: "R-right! We have to hurry after them!<end>"
KO: "그, 그래! 빨리 놈들을 쫓아가야 해!<end>"

## 550112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死んだ本当のお母さんが見えた…。<line-break>やさしくて、なつかしい…。<end>"
EN: "I saw my real mother, the one who died. She was so kind... just as I remembered.<end>"
KO: "돌아가신 진짜 어머니가 보였어…. 다정하고, 그리워….<end>"

## 550113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いけない、ちょっと考えちゃった。<line-break>考えると、悲しいだけなのに。<end>"
EN: "No, I shouldn't dwell on it. Thinking about it only makes me sad.<end>"
KO: "…안 돼, 잠깐 생각해 버렸어. 생각해 봐야 슬퍼질 뿐인데.<end>"

## 550114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "빨리 그 사람들을 쫓아가자! 자, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 550123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん、ルルアンタ、大丈夫だよぉ！<line-break>一瞬、フリントさんが見えたけどね…<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>の方から<line-break>何かおっきなものがルルアンタを包んで<line-break>ルルアンタをここに連れ戻したんだよ。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, Lulluantha's all right! I saw Flint for a moment, though... Then something big came from over by <value:$28>, wrapped around Lulluantha, and brought Lulluantha back here.<end>"
KO: "응, 루루안타 괜찮아! 순간 플린트 씨가 보였지만…. <value:$28> 쪽에서 뭔가 커다란 것이 루루안타를 감싸고 여기로 데려왔어.<end>"

## 550124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それより、さっきの人<line-break>追いかけなきゃ！　村のみんなが…！<end>"
EN: "Never mind that―we have to go after that person! Everyone in the village...!<end>"
KO: "그보다 아까 그 사람을 쫓아가야 해! 마을 사람들이…!<end>"

## 550125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ソウルリープだってぇ？　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Soul Leap, you say?<end>"
KO: "소울 리프라고?<end>"

## 550126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>がいなかったら<line-break>あの世行きだったよ。ふぅ…。　　<end>"
EN: "If <value:$28> hadn't been here, I'd be dead. Whew...<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>이 없었으면 저세상 갈 뻔했어. 후우….<end>"

## 550127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "術者のレベルが魔人レベルだったら<line-break>どのみち、あの世行きだったけど。<end>"
EN: "Though if the caster had been on the level of a demon, I'd have died anyway.<end>"
KO: "술자의 수준이 마인급이었다면 어차피 저승행이었지만.<end>"

## 550128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0<value:$28>、カッコよかったなあ<line-break>って思っただけだよ。ふふ。<line-break>さ、奴を追いかけよう！<line-break>村のみんなが心配だよ。<line-break>急がなきゃ！<end><value:$28>、きれいだったなあ<line-break>って思っただけだよ。ふふ。<line-break>さ、奴を追いかけよう！<line-break>村のみんなが心配だよ。<line-break>急がなきゃ！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0I was just thinking how impressive you looked, <value:$28>. Hee hee. Come on, let's go after that one! I'm worried about everyone in the village. We have to hurry!<end>I was just thinking how beautiful you looked, <value:$28>. Hee hee. Come on, let's go after that one! I'm worried about everyone in the village. We have to hurry!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0<value:$28>, 멋졌다고 생각했을 뿐이야. 후후. 자, 녀석을 쫓아가자! 마을 사람들이 걱정돼. 서둘러야 해!<end><value:$28>, 예뻤다고 생각했을 뿐이야. 후후. 자, 녀석을 쫓아가자! 마을 사람들이 걱정돼. 서둘러야 해!<end>"

## 550129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、<value:$28><line-break>大丈夫？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>, are you all right?<end>"
KO: "저기, <value:$28> 괜찮아?<end>"

## 550130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだね。<line-break>奴らを追わなくちゃ。<end>"
EN: "Right. We have to go after them.<end>"
KO: "그래. 놈들을 쫓아야 해.<end>"

## 550131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "拙者のことならば心配無用！<line-break>ま、一瞬、母上の姿が見えましたが。<end>"
EN: "There is no need to worry about me! Though for a moment, I did see my mother.<end>"
KO: "소생 걱정은 무용! 뭐, 순간 어머님의 모습이 보이긴 했지만.<end>"

## 550132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、拙者の母上は、<line-break>いまだ健在でござるよ。<end>"
EN: "No, my mother is still very much alive.<end>"
KO: "아니, 소인의 어머님은 아직 건재하십니다.<end>"

## 550133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "拙者の母上は強力かつ大災害なお方。<end>"
EN: "My mother is mighty―and a walking catastrophe.<end>"
KO: "제 어머니는 강력하고도 대재앙 같은 분이십니다.<end>"

## 550134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが目に浮かぶということは、<line-break>大変、恐ろしいことなのでござるぞ！<end>"
EN: "So seeing her before my eyes is a most terrifying thing!<end>"
KO: "그게 눈앞에 떠오른다는 건 아주 무서운 일이옵니다!<end>"

## 550135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それよりも兄弟子！<line-break>奴を追わねば、村の人たちが！　　　<end>"
EN: "More importantly, senior disciple! We must pursue that one, or the villagers will...!<end>"
KO: "그보다 사형! 녀석을 쫓지 않으면 마을 사람들이!<end>"

## 550136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（奴を追わなきゃ…。）<end>"
EN: "(I have to go after that one...)<end>"
KO: "(놈을 쫓아가야 해….)<end>"

## 550137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "石化獣じゃ！<line-break>石化獣が復活しおった！<end>"
EN: "The Petrifying Beast! The Petrifying Beast has returned!<end>"
KO: "석화수다! 석화수가 부활했다!<end>"

## 550138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "た、助けてくれ！<end>"
EN: "H-help me!<end>"
KO: "사, 살려 줘!<end>"

## 550139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きゃ～っ！<end>"
EN: "Aaaah!<end>"
KO: "꺄~!<end>"

## 550140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どういうことだよ！<line-break>みんな石になっちまってる！<end>"
EN: "What happened?! Everyone's turned to stone!<end>"
KO: "어떻게 된 거야! 모두 돌이 되어 버렸잖아!<end>"

## 550141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ヴァン？<end>"
EN: "Van...?<end>"
KO: "…반?<end>"

## 550142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "違う！　俺じゃない！<line-break>俺は冗談を言ってねえ！<end>"
EN: "No! It wasn't me! I'm not joking!<end>"
KO: "아니야! 내가 아니야! 난 농담한 게 아니라고!<end>"

## 550143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、さすが師匠！<end>"
EN: "Th-that's my master!<end>"
KO: "여, 역시 스승님!<end>"

## 550144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2う、うそ…<line-break>みんな…石になってる…！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…これだけの人数が石化している？<end>…いや、ただの石化ではない。　　<line-break>これは…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>4きゃっ、みんな、石になってる。<line-break>少し、ここに来たの、後悔してるかも！<end><if><value:$01><equal>14なんということじゃ！<line-break>村人を石に変えるとは！　おのれ…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17な、なんなのよぉ！　石、石…！<line-break>み～んな石になってるじゃない！<end><if><value:$01><equal>19ひ、ひどい！<line-break>みんな…石になってるよぉ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>22みんな魂を抜かれて石にされてるよ。<line-break>さっきの男？　それともあの怪物？<end><if><value:$01><equal>10どういうことだよ！<line-break>みんな石にされちまってる！<end>ね、ねえ、<value:$28>…<line-break>みんな…石になってるよ…！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2N-no... Everyone's turned to stone?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13This many people have been petrified?<end>No. This is no ordinary petrification. This is...<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Eek, everyone's turned to stone. I might be starting to regret coming here!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14What horror! To turn the villagers to stone... Curse you!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17W-what is this?! Stone, stone... Everyone's turned to stone!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19H-how awful! Everyone's turned to stone!<end><if><value:$01><equal>22Their souls were torn out, and that turned them to stone. Was it that man? Or the monster?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10What the hell happened? Everyone's been turned to stone!<end>H-hey, <value:$28>... Everyone's turned to stone...!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2거, 거짓말… 다들… 돌이 됐어…!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…이 많은 사람들이 석화했다고?<end>…아니, 단순한 석화가 아니다. 이건…<end><if><value:$01><equal>4꺅, 다들 돌이 됐어. 여기 온 거 조금 후회될지도!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14이 무슨 짓인가! 마을 사람들을 돌로 만들다니! 이놈…<end><if><value:$01><equal>17뭐, 뭐야아! 돌, 돌…! 전부 돌이 됐잖아!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19너, 너무해! 다들… 돌이 됐어어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>22다들 소울을 빼앗기고 돌이 됐어. 아까 그 남자? 아니면 그 괴물?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10이게 무슨 일이야! 다들 돌이 돼 버렸잖아!<end>저, 저기, <value:$28>… 다들… 돌이 됐어…!<end>"

## 550145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ソウルリープ！　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break><line-break>この状況…さっきの奴よりも<line-break>強力な使い手がいるってことか…。<line-break>石化獣って名前はダテじゃないんだな。<end>"
EN: "Soul Leap! Does this mean there's a user even more powerful than the last one...? So the name Petrifying Beast isn't just for show.<end>"
KO: "…소울 리프! 이 상황… 아까 녀석보다 더 강한 사용자가 있다는 건가…. 석화수라는 이름이 괜히 붙은 게 아니군.<end>"

## 550146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、気をつけろ！<line-break>魂抜かれて、石にされるぞ！<end>"
EN: "Watch out, <value:$28>! You'll have your soul ripped out and be turned to stone!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 조심해! 영혼을 빼앗겨 돌이 될 거야!<end>"

## 550147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "み、み、みんな…<line-break>石になってるよ！？<end>"
EN: "E-e-everyone... They've turned to stone?!<end>"
KO: "다, 다, 다들… 돌이 돼 있잖아!?<end>"

## 550148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんと！　みんな石になっておる！<line-break>いったい、何奴が！？<end>"
EN: "Good heavens! Everyone has turned to stone! Who could have done this?!<end>"
KO: "뭐라고! 모두 돌이 되어 버렸잖아! 대체 누가!?<end>"

## 550149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…少なくとも、自分の意志から<line-break>石になったわけではござらんな。<end>"
EN: "At the very least, they did not turn to stone of their own accord.<end>"
KO: "…적어도 자기 의지로 돌이 된 건 아닌 모양이군.<end>"

## 550150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "申しわけござらぬ。<line-break>不謹慎でござった。<end>"
EN: "My apologies. That was in poor taste.<end>"
KO: "송구하옵니다. 경솔했습니다.<end>"

## 550183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちっくしょう！　あと少しで<line-break>あの怪ブツもおだブツだったのに！<line-break>…ププッ、今日も俺はさえてるぜ！<end>"
EN: "Damn! We almost put that beast to rest! Pfft... I'm sharp today!<end>"
KO: "제기랄! 조금만 더 했으면 저 괴물도 골로 갔을 텐데! …푸풉, 오늘도 내 말솜씨는 끝내주는군!<end>"

## 550184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァン…、<line-break>こういうときに、<line-break>そういう冗談やめようよ。<line-break>それよりも、<line-break>ねえ、<value:$28><line-break>あの黒づくめも…。<end>"
EN: "Van... not now. Quit joking around. More importantly, <value:$28>, what about the one in black...?<end>"
KO: "반…, 이런 때 그런 농담은 그만하자. 그것보다, <value:$28>, 저 검은 옷의 사람도….<end>"

## 550185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホントだ。<end>"
EN: "You're right.<end>"
KO: "정말이야.<end>"

## 550186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "し、師匠、すばらしい！<line-break>背筋がゾクッとくる寒さでござる！<end>"
EN: "M-Master, magnificent! The chill went straight down my spine!<end>"
KO: "스, 스승님, 훌륭하십니다! 등골이 오싹해지는 추위입니다!<end>"

## 550187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へへっ、お前も修行を怠るなよ！<line-break>…で、あの黒づくめはどこ行ったんだ？<end>"
EN: "Heh, don't neglect your training! So... where did the one in black go?<end>"
KO: "헤헷, 너도 수련을 게을리하지 마! …근데 그 검은 옷은 어디로 갔지?<end>"

## 550188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おいおい、どうしたんだ？<line-break>へへっ、あれしきで、へこたれんなよ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, what's wrong? Heh, don't let a little thing like that get you down.<end>"
KO: "어이, 왜 그래? 헤헷, 그 정도로 기죽지 마.<end>"

## 550189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちっ、ホントなら<line-break>大ウケなのによ！<end>"
EN: "Damn. That should've had everyone howling!<end>"
KO: "쳇, 진짜라면 대박인데!<end>"

## 550190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おーい、<value:$28>。<line-break>黒づくめもいねえぞ。どうすんだ？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>! The one in black is gone too. What do we do?<end>"
KO: "어이, <value:$28>. 검은 옷 녀석도 없는데 어쩔 거야?<end>"

## 550191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2あと少しだったのに…。<line-break>それに、あの、黒づくめも<line-break>逃がしちゃったみたいね…。<line-break><value:$28><line-break>だいじょうぶ？<line-break>ケガはない？<end><if><value:$01><equal>6怪物…逃げちゃった。ううん。<line-break>あのままじゃ、こっちが危なかった…<line-break>見逃してもらったって言うべきかな。<line-break>さっきの黒づくめの男もいない…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>12あの、黒づくめの男も　<line-break>逃がしちまったようだ。<line-break>ここをもとに戻す手がかりはなし、か。<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…黒づくめの方も<line-break>逃げたようだ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>10くそっ、逃がすかよ。<line-break>これ以上、誰かを石にさせるもんか！<line-break>姉ちゃん、追おう！<end><if><value:$01><equal>14逃げおったか…。<line-break>ま、わしらに勝てる相手ではない。<line-break>命があっただけでももうけものじゃな。<line-break>そう言えば、あの黒づくめは…？<line-break>あやつ、最後には、<line-break>改心しよった感じじゃったが。<end><if><value:$01><equal>15怪物も<line-break>…それに、あの黒ずくめの男も、<line-break>逃げちゃったね。<line-break>見逃してもらった、とか、<line-break>運がよかった、という方が<line-break>正しい言い方かもね…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17高貴なエルフのアタクシにかかれば<line-break>あんな怪物、軽いわ！　物足りないわ！<line-break>楽勝だわ！<line-break>フフ、あの黒づくめも逃げたようね。<line-break>さ、ド下等生物の<value:$28>、<line-break>この偉大なアタクシを讃えてよくてよ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>18黒づくめもおりませぬ。<line-break>…逃げた様子ですな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>4きゃっ、怪物も逃げたし<line-break>黒づくめもいないわ。<line-break>これって、助かったのかも！<end><if><value:$01><equal>19あの怪物にも、黒づくめにも<line-break>逃げられちゃったね。<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>でも、あの黒づくめの人、<line-break>頭が痛そうにしてたけど…<line-break>大丈夫かなあ。<end>あ～あ、逃げられちゃったよ。<line-break>でも、あんな怪物、<line-break>今のぼくたちには倒せないよぉ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2We were so close... And it looks like the one in black got away too. <value:$28>, are you all right? Are you hurt?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6The monster got away... No. If that had gone on, we'd have been in danger. Maybe I should say it let us go. And the man in black from before is gone too...<end><if><value:$01><equal>12Looks like we let the man in black get away too. So much for finding a way to restore this place.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13It seems the one in black escaped as well.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Damn it, I'm not letting it get away! I won't let anyone else be turned to stone! Sis, let's go after it!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14So it escaped... Well, it was no match for us. It should count itself lucky to be alive. Speaking of which, where is the one in black? The one in black seemed to have had a change of heart by the end.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15The monster... and the man in black both got away. Then again, perhaps it's more accurate to say we were spared. Or just lucky...<end><if><value:$01><equal>17A monster like that is nothing before a noble Elf such as myself! Too easy! Hardly worth my time! Hm-hm, and it seems the one in black fled as well. Now, <value:$28>, you lowest of lowly creatures, you may praise my greatness!<end><if><value:$01><equal>18The one in black is gone as well. It appears that one escaped.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Eek! The monster got away, and the one in black is gone too. Maybe that means we're safe!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19The monster and the one in black both got away, <value:$28>. But the person in black seemed to have a headache... I wonder if everything's all right.<end>Aw, it got away. But there's no way we could defeat a monster like that as we are now.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2조금만 더 하면 됐는데…. 게다가 그 검은 옷도 놓친 것 같네…. <value:$28>, 괜찮아? 다친 데 없어?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6괴물… 도망가 버렸어. 아니, 그대로였으면 우리가 위험했겠지… 봐줬다고 해야 하나. 아까 그 검은 옷의 남자도 없어….<end><if><value:$01><equal>12그 검은 옷의 남자도 놓친 모양이군. 여길 원래대로 돌릴 단서는 없는 건가.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…검은 옷 쪽도 도망간 모양이군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10젠장, 놓칠 것 같냐. 더는 누구도 돌로 만들게 두지 않겠어! 누나, 쫓아가자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14도망갔나…. 뭐, 우리에게 이길 상대는 아니지. 목숨을 건진 것만 해도 다행이다. 그러고 보니 그 검은 옷은…? 그 녀석, 마지막에는 마음을 고쳐먹은 듯했는데.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15괴물도… 그리고 그 검은 옷의 남자도 도망가 버렸네. 봐줬다거나 운이 좋았다고 하는 편이 맞을지도 모르겠어….<end><if><value:$01><equal>17고귀한 엘프인 이 몸이 나서면 저런 괴물쯤은 가벼워요! 시시해요! 식은 죽 먹기예요! 후후, 그 검은 옷도 도망간 모양이네요. 자, 최하등생물 <value:$28>, 이 위대한 이 몸을 찬양해도 좋아요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>18검은 옷도 보이지 않는군요. …도망간 모양입니다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4꺄, 괴물도 도망갔고 검은 옷도 없어. 이거, 우리 살아난 걸지도!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19그 괴물도, 검은 옷도 놓쳐 버렸네. <value:$28>. 그래도 그 검은 옷의 사람, 머리가 아파 보였는데… 괜찮으려나.<end>아아, 놓쳐 버렸어. 그래도 저런 괴물은 지금의 우리로선 쓰러뜨릴 수 없어.<end>"

## 550192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2なぁに？　あの娘が気になるの？<line-break>私はいいけど、フェルムちゃんが<line-break>聞いたら、悲しむわよ？<end><if><value:$01><equal>6あの娘…。<line-break>相当、ショックみたいだよ。<line-break>助けにいって…あげなよ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>12とりあえずは<line-break>あの娘をなんとかしてやんなきゃ、な。<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…放っておけ。<line-break>どうせ、自決する。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>関わるだけ時間のムダだ。<line-break>死にたい奴は<line-break>勝手に死ねばいい。<end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん、なにやってんだよ！<line-break>早く、追わなきゃ…って<line-break>あ…。<line-break>…そうだよな、姉ちゃん。<line-break>ひとり、助かってる人がいたんだ。<line-break>そっちが、先か。<end><if><value:$01><equal>14おお、そうじゃ。<line-break>助かった娘っこが<line-break>おったのじゃったな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>15そうだ、あの娘…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17…って、なによぉぉお！<line-break>そんな下等生物なんて<line-break>放っておいて、アタクシを讃えるのよ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>18…その子供を助けるので？<line-break>兄弟子。<end><if><value:$01><equal>4うふふ、<value:$28>ったら<line-break>あそこの女の子を見つめてる。<line-break>　<line-break>あのノエルってコは<line-break>ショックで自分を失ってるのに<line-break>どうするつもりなのかな？<end><if><value:$01><equal>19あっ、そっか！<line-break>確かにあの人の心配してる<line-break>場合じゃなかった。<line-break>あのお姉ちゃん<line-break>…ノエル…だっけ？<line-break>助けなきゃだった。<end>…黒づくめの男にも逃げられた以上、<line-break>この村の人を救う方法はわからない。<line-break>　<line-break>とりあえず、彼女…ノエルだっけ？<line-break>そのノエルだけでも救わなくちゃだよ。<line-break>ね？　<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2What is it? Interested in that girl? I don't mind, but Ferme would be upset if she heard.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6That girl... She looks badly shaken. Go... help her.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12First things first. We have to do something for that girl.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Leave her. She'll kill herself anyway. Getting involved is a waste of time. If she wants to die, let her.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Sis, what are you doing? We have to hurry after it... Oh. Right, Sis. Someone survived. We should see to her first.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Ah, yes. There was a young lass who survived.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Right, that girl...<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Wait, what?! Forget that lowly creature and praise me!<end><if><value:$01><equal>18Are you going to help that child, senior disciple?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Hee-hee, look at <value:$28>, staring at that girl over there. Noel seems so shocked she's beside herself. I wonder what you're planning to do?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Oh, right! This isn't the time to worry about that person. That girl... Noel, was it? We were supposed to help her.<end>Now that the man in black has escaped too, we have no idea how to save the people of this village. But for now, we have to save her... Noel, wasn't it? At least we have to save Noel. Right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2뭐야? 저 애가 신경 쓰여? 나는 상관없지만 페름이 들으면 슬퍼할걸?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6저 애…. 상당히 충격받은 것 같아. 도우러… 가 줘.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12일단은 저 애를 어떻게든 해 줘야지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…내버려 둬. 어차피 자결할 거다. 관여해 봐야 시간 낭비야. 죽고 싶은 놈은 마음대로 죽으라지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 뭐 하는 거야! 빨리 쫓아가야… 아…. 그렇지, 누나. 한 명 살아남은 사람이 있었어. 저쪽이 먼저구나.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14오오, 그렇군. 살아남은 아가씨가 있었지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15맞아, 저 애….<end><if><value:$01><equal>17…아니 뭐야아아! 그런 하등생물은 내버려 두고 이 몸을 찬양하란 말이야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>18…그 아이를 돕는 것이오? 사형.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4우후후, <value:$28>가 저 여자애를 바라보고 있네. 저 노엘이라는 애는 충격으로 정신을 놓았는데 어쩔 생각일까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19아, 그렇구나! 확실히 그 사람 걱정할 때가 아니었어. 저 언니… 노엘…이라고 했지? 도와야 했어.<end>…검은 옷 남자도 놓쳤으니 이 마을 사람들을 구할 방법은 모르겠어. 일단 그녀… 노엘이라고 했나? 그 노엘만이라도 구해야 해. 그렇지? <value:$28>.<end>"

## 550193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2あ、ちょっと！<line-break><value:$28>！<end><if><value:$01><equal>6…<value:$28>。<end><if><value:$01><equal>12…なるほど<line-break>ノエル…って言ったっけ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…フッ、やれやれ、<line-break>しょうがない奴だ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん<line-break>そのコ、どうだ？<line-break>大丈夫か？<end><if><value:$01><equal>14…ショックじゃったのじゃろうな。<line-break>まるで、ふぬけのようじゃ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>15ショック…だろうな…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17キー！　この高貴なエルフのアタクシを<line-break>置いてくなんて１００年早くてよぉ！！<end><if><value:$01><equal>18…その子もつらかろう。<line-break>自分の村がこの有様では…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>4きゃっ、<value:$28>ったら<line-break>あのコを助けにいくつもりかな？<line-break>かなり、おバカさんかも！<line-break>あのまま、死なせてあげた方が<line-break>あのコにはつらいことが少ないのに！<line-break>もう、ホントにおせっかいさん！<end><if><value:$01><equal>19ねえ、ノエルってお姉ちゃん、<line-break>だいじょうぶそう？<line-break><value:$28>。<end>…でも、立ち直れるかな。<line-break>彼女…。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Hey, wait! <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6<value:$28>...<end><if><value:$01><equal>12I see. Noel, was it?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Hmph. Honestly, you're hopeless.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Sis, how is she? Is she all right?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14She must be in shock. She looks completely out of it.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15She must be in shock...<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Aaargh! You dare leave a noble Elf like me behind? You're a hundred years too early!<end><if><value:$01><equal>18This must be hard for the child as well, with the village in such a state...<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Eek! Is <value:$28> really going to help that girl? What a fool! She'd suffer less if you just let her die! Honestly, what a meddler!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Hey, does Noel look like she'll be all right, <value:$28>?<end>But I wonder if she'll be able to recover...<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2아, 잠깐! <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6…<value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12…그렇군, 노엘…이라고 했던가.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…훗, 이런, 어쩔 수 없는 녀석이군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 그 애 어때? 괜찮아?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14…충격이 컸던 게로구나. 완전히 넋이 나갔어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15충격…이겠지….<end><if><value:$01><equal>17키익! 이 고귀한 엘프인 나를 두고 가다니 100년은 일러!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>18…그 아이도 괴롭겠지. 자기 마을이 이 지경이니….<end><if><value:$01><equal>4꺅, <value:$28>는 저 아이를 도우러 갈 생각인가? 꽤 바보일지도! 그냥 죽게 두는 편이 저 아이에겐 덜 괴로울 텐데! 정말 참견쟁이라니까!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19저기, 노엘이라는 언니, 괜찮아 보여? <value:$28>.<end>…그래도 다시 일어설 수 있을까. 저 애….<end>"

## 550239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…目が覚めるとキミがいた。<line-break>ボクは、眠りのお姫様…。<end>"
EN: "When I woke up, there you were. I'm the sleeping princess...<end>"
KO: "…눈을 뜨니 네가 있었어. 나는 잠자는 공주….<end>"

## 550240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どーして、キミは<line-break>ボクにだけ、そーゆー態度取るかなあ！<end>"
EN: "Why am I the only one you treat like that?!<end>"
KO: "왜 넌 나한테만 그런 태도를 취하는 거야!<end>"

## 550241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あ、あの娘。<line-break>…たぶん、ショックなんだと思うよ。<line-break>　<line-break>ねえ、<value:$28>…。<line-break>行って…あげてよ…。<end>"
EN: "That girl... I think she's in shock. <value:$28>... please, go to her.<end>"
KO: "…아, 그 애. …아마 충격을 받은 것 같아. 저기, <value:$28>…. 가… 줘….<end>"

## 550242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ、お目覚めかい？<line-break>どうやら、やっこさん、俺たちに<line-break>とどめを刺さずに行ったようだ。<line-break>あの、黒づくめのボウヤが<line-break>助けてくれたのかも、な。<end>"
EN: "Hey, awake at last? Looks like our friend left without finishing us off. Maybe that boy in black saved us.<end>"
KO: "여, 정신 들었나? 아무래도 그 녀석, 우리한테 마무리는 안 하고 간 모양이야. 그 검은 옷 꼬마가 구해준 걸지도 모르지.<end>"

## 550244
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は、女の名前なら<line-break>一度聞いたら忘れない。<line-break>　<line-break>ま、それが<line-break>半分の確率でってのが<line-break>つらいところだが、な。<end>"
EN: "Once I hear a woman's name, I never forget it. Trouble is, I'm only right half the time.<end>"
KO: "난 여자 이름이라면 한 번 들으면 잊지 않아. 뭐, 절반 확률이라는 게 문제지만.<end>"

## 550245
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こっちからも助けを出してやらないと<line-break>まずい状況みたいだぜ？　彼女。<end>"
EN: "Looks like she could use some help from us too.<end>"
KO: "이쪽에서도 도움을 보내지 않으면 위험한 상황 같은데? 그녀 말이야.<end>"

## 550246
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イテテテ…お、俺としたことが、<line-break>怪ぶつにやられて、おだぶつ。<line-break>ププププ…。<end>"
EN: "Ow, ow, ow... Me, done in by a monster and gone to meet my maker. Pfffft...<end>"
KO: "아야야… 나, 나도 참, 괴물한테 당해서 죽다니. 푸푸푸….<end>"

## 550247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァン…、こういうときに、<line-break>そういう冗談やめようよ。<line-break>　<line-break>それよりも、<line-break>ねえ、<value:$28><line-break>あの黒づくめも…。<end>"
EN: "Van... don't make jokes like that right now. More importantly, <value:$28>, the one in black is gone too...<end>"
KO: "반…, 이런 때 그런 농담은 그만하자. 그것보다, <value:$28>, 저 검은 옷의 사람도….<end>"

## 550248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホントだ。<end>"
EN: "You're right.<end>"
KO: "정말이야.<end>"

## 550249
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "し、師匠、すばらしい！<line-break>背筋も凍る寒さでござる！<end>"
EN: "M-Master, magnificent! That joke sent a chill down my spine!<end>"
KO: "스, 스승님, 훌륭합니다! 등골이 얼어붙을 만큼 춥습니다!<end>"

## 550250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へへっ、お前も修行を怠るなよ！<line-break>…で、あの黒づくめはどこ行ったんだ？<end>"
EN: "Heh, don't neglect your training! So... where did the one in black go?<end>"
KO: "헤헷, 너도 수련을 게을리하지 마! …근데 그 검은 옷은 어디로 갔지?<end>"

## 550251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おいおい、どうしたんだ？<line-break>へへっ、あれしきで、へこたれんなよ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, what's wrong? Heh, don't let a little thing like that get you down.<end>"
KO: "어이, 왜 그래? 헤헷, 그 정도로 기죽지 마.<end>"

## 550252
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちっ、ホントなら<line-break>大ウケなのによ！<end>"
EN: "Damn. If that were true, it'd be a riot!<end>"
KO: "쳇, 진짜라면 대박인데!<end>"

## 550253
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おーい、<value:$28>。<line-break>黒づくめもいねえぞ。どうすんだ？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. The one in black is gone too. What do we do?<end>"
KO: "어이, <value:$28>. 검은 옷 녀석도 없는데 어쩔 거야?<end>"

## 550254
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>、しっかりして！<line-break><value:$28>！<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…起きろ。<line-break>…いつまで寝ている。<end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん、目を覚ましてくれよ。<line-break>姉ちゃん！！<end><if><value:$01><equal>14こら、しっかりせい。<line-break><value:$28>！<end><if><value:$01><equal>15ねえ、<value:$28>、<line-break>しっかり。しっかりしてよ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17ちょっと！　ここで死んじゃうのぉ？<line-break>下等生物って短命なのはわかるけど<line-break>こんなに簡単に死なないでよぉ！<line-break>許さなくてよぉ！　高貴なエルフである<line-break>アタクシは許さなくてよぉ！<line-break>早く起きるのよぉ！　目を覚まして！<end><if><value:$01><equal>18兄弟子！<line-break>しっかりしてくだされ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>4もしかして<line-break>死んじゃってるかも…<end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>～！<line-break><value:$28>、しっかりしてぇ。<end><value:$28>、しっかりしてよぉ！<line-break><value:$28>てばぁ！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>, wake up! <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Wake up. How long do you intend to sleep?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Sis, please wake up. Sis!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Come now, pull yourself together. <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Come on, <value:$28>. Wake up. Please wake up.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17What do you think you're doing, dying here?! I know inferior life-forms have short lives, but you can't die this easily! I won't allow it! I, a noble Elf, will not allow it! Get up this instant! Open your eyes!<end><if><value:$01><equal>18Senior disciple! Please, pull yourself together!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Maybe... maybe this one's dead...<end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>! <value:$28>, wake up!<end><value:$28>, wake up! Come on, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>, 정신 차려! <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…일어나. …언제까지 잘 거냐.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 눈 좀 떠 봐. 누나!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14이봐, 정신 차리게. <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15저기, <value:$28>, 정신 차려. 정신 차리라고.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17잠깐! 여기서 죽는 거야? 하등생물이 단명하는 건 알지만 이렇게 쉽게 죽지 말라고! 용서 못 해! 고귀한 엘프인 이 몸이 용서 못 한다고! 빨리 일어나! 눈을 떠!<end><if><value:$01><equal>18사형! 정신 차리시게.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4혹시 죽은 걸지도…<end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>~! <value:$28>, 정신 차려어.<end><value:$28>, 정신 차리라고! <value:$28>라니까아!<end>"

## 550255
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2もう、びっくりさせないで！<line-break>死んだかと思ったじゃない。<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…フッ。<line-break>いらぬ心配をかけるな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん、<line-break>…よかった。<end><if><value:$01><equal>14<value:$28>、<line-break>ちゃんと生きておるか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>15…よかった。<line-break>ねえ、<value:$28>、<line-break>僕、心配しちゃたよ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17な、な、な、な、なによ！<line-break>生きてるんなら、<line-break>さっさと起きなさいよ！<line-break>下等生物であるあなたが、<line-break>高貴なエルフであるアタクシに<line-break>心配かけるなんて５００年早くてよぉ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>18兄弟子…<line-break>お気がつかれましたか。<end><if><value:$01><equal>4きゃっ！　生きてたみたい！<end><if><value:$01><equal>19よかったあ！<line-break>大丈夫？<end>よかったぁ！<line-break>このまま目を開けなかったら<line-break>どうしようかと思った。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Don't scare me like that! I thought you were dead.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Hmph. Do not cause needless concern.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Sis... thank goodness.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14<value:$28>, are you properly alive?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Thank goodness. <value:$28>, I was so worried.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17W-w-w-w-what?! If you're alive, then get up already! An inferior life-form like you has no business making a noble Elf such as myself worry. You're five hundred years too young!<end><if><value:$01><equal>18Senior disciple... have you regained consciousness?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Eek! Looks like you're alive!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Thank goodness! Are you all right?<end>Thank goodness! I was afraid you might never open your eyes.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2정말, 놀라게 하지 마! 죽은 줄 알았잖아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…후. 쓸데없는 걱정을 끼치지 마라.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나… 다행이야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14<value:$28>, 제대로 살아 있느냐?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15…다행이다. 저기, <value:$28>, 나 걱정했잖아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17뭐, 뭐, 뭐, 뭐야! 살아 있으면 빨리 일어나요! 하등생물인 당신이 고귀한 엘프인 이 몸을 걱정시키다니 500년은 일러요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>18사형… 정신이 드셨습니까.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4꺅! 살아 있었나 봐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19다행이다아! 괜찮아?<end>다행이다아! 이대로 눈을 안 뜨면 어쩌나 했어.<end>"

## 550256
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2残念だけど、逃げられたようね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…逃げられた。<line-break>怪物にも、あの男にも…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>10怪物にも、あの黒づくめにも<line-break>逃げられちまったよ、姉ちゃん。<line-break>…ちくしょう！<end><if><value:$01><equal>14逃げおったか…。<line-break>ま、わしらに勝てる相手ではない。<line-break>命があっただけでももうけものじゃな。<line-break>そう言えば、あの黒づくめも…？<line-break>あやつ、最後には、<line-break>改心しよった感じじゃったが。<end><if><value:$01><equal>15ひどい目にあったけど<line-break>どうやら助かったみたいだ。<line-break>…見逃してもらったんだね。<line-break>いや、もしかしたら、<line-break>あの黒づくめに助けられたのかも…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17もう怪物もあの黒づくめもいなくてよ。<line-break>まったく、だらしなくてよ、あなた。<line-break>　<line-break>ま、あなた１人、どんな目にあっても<line-break>別にかまわないけれど<line-break>高貴なエルフであるアタクシまで…<end><if><value:$01><equal>18どうやら助かった様子。<line-break>怪物にもとどめを刺されなんだとは、<line-break>あの黒づくめに助けられましたかな？<end><if><value:$01><equal>4私、アカデミーではいじめっ子に、<line-break>家ではお義母さんにいじめられてたから、<line-break>すごく、打たれ強いの！　きゃっ！<line-break>あ、それより、あの怪物も<line-break>黒づくめの人も逃げちゃったのね。　　<line-break>助かっちゃったかも！<end><if><value:$01><equal>19あの怪物にも、黒づくめにも<line-break>逃げられちゃったね。<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>でも、あの黒づくめの人、<line-break>頭が痛そうにしてたけど…<line-break>大丈夫かなあ。<end>あ～あ、逃げられちゃったよ。<line-break>でも、あんな怪物、<line-break>今のぼくたちには倒せないよぉ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Unfortunately, it looks like they got away.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13They escaped. The monster and that man...<end><if><value:$01><equal>10The monster and the one in black both got away, Sis. Damn it!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14So it escaped... Well, it was no foe we could defeat. We are fortunate simply to be alive. Come to think of it, where is the one in black? By the end, that one seemed to have had a change of heart.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15That was awful, but it looks like we survived. We must've been spared. Or maybe the one in black saved us...<end><if><value:$01><equal>17The monster and the one in black are both gone. Honestly, you are utterly hopeless. Not that I care what happens to you, but to drag a noble Elf such as myself into it...<end><if><value:$01><equal>18It appears we survived. Since the monster did not finish us off, might the one in black have saved us?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4I was bullied by bullies at the Academy and by my stepmother at home, so I can take a real beating! Eek! Oh, but never mind that. The monster and the person in black both ran away. Maybe we got lucky!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19The monster and the one in black both got away, <value:$28>. But the person in black seemed to have a headache... I wonder if that person is all right.<end>Oh, they got away. But there's no way we could defeat a monster like that as we are now.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2아쉽지만 도망친 모양이네.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…놓쳤다. 괴물도, 그 남자도….<end><if><value:$01><equal>10괴물도 검은 옷 녀석도 도망쳤어, 누나. …젠장!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14도망쳤나…. 뭐, 우리에게 이길 상대는 아니지. 목숨을 건진 것만으로 다행이군. 그러고 보니 그 검은 옷도…? 그 녀석 마지막에는 마음을 고쳐먹은 듯했는데.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15심한 일을 당했지만 아무래도 살았나 봐. …놓아준 거구나. 아니, 어쩌면 그 검은 옷이 도와준 걸지도….<end><if><value:$01><equal>17이제 괴물도 그 검은 옷도 없어. 정말 한심하네, 당신. 뭐, 당신 혼자 무슨 일을 당하든 상관없지만 고귀한 엘프인 이 몸까지…<end><if><value:$01><equal>18아무래도 살았나 보군. 괴물이 마무리를 짓지 않은 걸 보면 그 검은 옷에게 도움받은 것이오?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4나는 아카데미에서는 괴롭히는 애들한테, 집에서는 새엄마한테 괴롭힘당해서 엄청 맷집이 좋아! 꺅! 아, 그것보다 그 괴물도 검은 옷 사람도 도망쳤네. 살았을지도!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19그 괴물도 검은 옷도 놓쳤네. <value:$28>. 그런데 그 검은 옷 사람, 머리가 아픈 것 같던데… 괜찮을까.<end>아~아, 도망쳐 버렸네. 하지만 저런 괴물은 지금 우리로선 쓰러뜨릴 수 없어어.<end>"

## 550257
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2なぁに？　気がついたら<line-break>すぐ女の子の方に行くわけ？<line-break>　<line-break>べ、別にあたしはいいけど<line-break>フェルムちゃんが悲しむんじゃ<line-break>ないかなーって、思ってさ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…放っておけ。<line-break>どうせ、自決する。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>関わるだけ時間のムダだ。<line-break>死にたい奴は<line-break>勝手に死ねばいい。<end><if><value:$01><equal>10…そっか。<line-break>失敗をくいるより<line-break>今、できること、か…、姉ちゃん。<end><if><value:$01><equal>14おお、そうじゃ。<line-break>助かった娘っこが<line-break>おったのじゃったな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>15あ、そうだ。<line-break>ノエルって言ったっけ。<line-break>あのコを助けなくちゃ…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17何よぉ！　あんな下等生物助けても<line-break>ロクなことはなくてよぉ！　あんなのは<line-break>放っておいて、アタクシの話を聞くの！<end><if><value:$01><equal>18ボルダンには、敗北はこの上ない屈辱。<line-break>…あの人間の貴族の娘にとっても<line-break>そうなのでござろうか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>4あの人は、ずーっとあんな感じかな。　<line-break>たぶん、頭よすぎるのかも。<line-break>きゃっ！<line-break>うふふふ。頭いい人間に、私が<line-break>ひそかに嫉妬と悪感情を持ってるのが、<line-break>よくわかるセリフを言っちゃったかな！<end><if><value:$01><equal>19あっ、そっか！<line-break>確かにあの人の心配してる<line-break>場合じゃなかった。<line-break>あのお姉ちゃん<line-break>…ノエル…だっけ？<line-break>助けなきゃだった。<end>…黒づくめの男にも逃げられた以上、<line-break>この村の人を救う方法はわからない。<line-break>　<line-break>とりあえず、彼女…ノエルだっけ？<line-break>そのノエルだけでも救わなくちゃだよ。<line-break>ね？　<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2What, the moment you wake up, you run straight to another girl? N-not that I care. I just thought Ferme might be upset.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Leave her. She'll kill herself anyway. Getting involved is a waste of time. People who want to die can do as they please.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Right... Instead of dwelling on our failure, we should do what we can now, Sis.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Ah, yes. There was that young lass who survived.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Oh, right. Noel, wasn't it? We have to help her...<end><if><value:$01><equal>17What?! Nothing good will come of helping an inferior life-form like that! Leave her and listen to me!<end><if><value:$01><equal>18To a Boldan, there is no greater humiliation than defeat. Is it the same for that human nobleman's daughter?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Is that person always like that? Maybe it's from being too clever. Eek! Hee hee hee. Did I just say something that makes my secret jealousy and ill will toward clever people painfully obvious?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Oh, right! This really isn't the time to be worrying about that person. That lady... Noel, was it? We have to help her.<end>Now that the man in black has escaped too, we don't know how to save the villagers. But at least we have to save her... Noel, wasn't it? Right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2뭐야? 정신 차리자마자 여자애한테 가는 거야? 벼, 별로 나는 상관없지만 페름이 슬퍼하지 않을까 싶어서.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…내버려 둬. 어차피 자결할 거다. 관여해 봐야 시간 낭비야. 죽고 싶은 놈은 마음대로 죽으라지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10…그렇구나. 실패를 후회하기보다 지금 할 수 있는 일인가… 누나.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14오오, 그렇군. 살아남은 아가씨가 있었지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15아, 맞아. 노엘이라고 했지. 그 애를 도와야 해….<end><if><value:$01><equal>17뭐야! 저런 하등생물을 도와 봐야 좋은 일은 하나도 없어! 저런 건 내버려 두고 이 몸의 이야기를 들어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>18볼단에게 패배는 더없는 굴욕. …저 인간 귀족의 딸에게도 그런 것이오?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4저 사람은 계속 저런 상태일까. 아마 머리가 너무 좋아서 그런지도. 꺅! 우후후. 똑똑한 인간에게 내가 몰래 질투와 악감정을 품고 있다는 걸 너무 잘 보여 주는 말을 해 버렸나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19아, 그렇구나! 확실히 그 사람 걱정할 때가 아니었어. 저 언니… 노엘…이라고 했지? 도와야 했어.<end>…검은 옷 남자도 놓쳤으니 이 마을 사람들을 구할 방법은 모르겠어. 일단 그녀… 노엘이라고 했나? 그 노엘만이라도 구해야 해. 그렇지? <value:$28>.<end>"

## 550258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2…ちょ、ちょっと！<line-break><value:$28>！<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…フッ、やれやれ、<line-break>しょうがない奴だ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>10とにかく、今できることは<line-break>そのコを助けることだよな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>14…ショックじゃったのじゃろうな。<line-break>まるで、ふぬけのようじゃ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>15…ひとりぼっちで生き残って<line-break>つらいだろうな、あのコ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17こらこらこら！　高貴なエルフである<line-break>アタクシをおいていくなんて<line-break>８００年早くてよぉ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>18…その子もつらかろう。<line-break>自分の村がこの有様では…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>4あ、助けにいくんだ。<line-break>うふふ、ホントにおせっかいさん！<end><if><value:$01><equal>19ねえ、ノエルってお姉ちゃん、<line-break>だいじょうぶそう？<line-break><value:$28>。<end>…でも、立ち直れるかな。<line-break>彼女…。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2W-wait! <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Hmph. What a hopeless fool.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Anyway, what we can do now is help that girl.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14The shock must've left her an empty shell.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15It must be awful, being the only one left alive...<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Now see here! You're eight hundred years too young to leave a noble Elf such as myself behind!<end><if><value:$01><equal>18That child must be suffering too, with the village reduced to this...<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Oh, you're going to help her? Hee hee, you really can't help meddling!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Do you think Noel will be all right, <value:$28>?<end>But will she ever recover from this...?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2자, 잠깐! <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…후, 정말이지. 어쩔 수 없는 녀석이군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10아무튼 지금 할 수 있는 건 그 애를 돕는 거겠지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14…충격이 컸던 모양이군. 완전히 넋이 나가 버렸어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15…혼자 살아남아서 힘들겠지, 저 애.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17잠깐잠깐잠깐! 고귀한 엘프인 이 몸을 두고 가다니 800년은 일러요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>18…그 아이도 괴롭겠지. 자기 마을이 이 지경이 됐으니…<end><if><value:$01><equal>4아, 도우러 가는구나. 후후, 정말 참견쟁이라니까!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19저기, 노엘이라는 언니 괜찮아 보여? <value:$28>.<end>…하지만 다시 일어설 수 있을까. 그녀…<end>"

## 550259
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（怪物もあの黒づくめもいない。<line-break>　…助かったのか。　　　　　　）<end>"
EN: "The monster and the one in black are both gone. Did we... survive?<end>"
KO: "(괴물도 그 검은 옷의 녀석도 없다. …살아난 건가.)<end>"

## 550298
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わたしが剣を抜いた。　　<end>"
EN: "I drew the sword.<end>"
KO: "…내가 검을 뽑았어.<end>"

## 550299
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、石化獣が復活して<line-break>みんなを石に変えた。<end>"
EN: "Then the Petrifying Beast came back to life and turned everyone to stone.<end>"
KO: "그리고 석화수가 부활해서 모두를 돌로 만들어 버렸어.<end>"

## 550300
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたしはなぜか<line-break>石にならなかった…。　　<end>"
EN: "For some reason, I wasn't turned to stone...<end>"
KO: "나는 어째서인지 돌이 되지 않았어….<end>"

## 550301
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わたしが剣を抜いた。　　<end>"
EN: "I drew the sword.<end>"
KO: "…내가 검을 뽑았어.<end>"

## 550302
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そしたら、石化獣が復活して<line-break>みんなを石に変えた。<end>"
EN: "And then the Petrifying Beast came back to life and turned everyone to stone.<end>"
KO: "그러자 석화수가 되살아나 모두를 돌로 만들었어.<end>"

## 550303
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたしだけは、なぜか<line-break>石にならなかった…。　　　<end>"
EN: "For some reason, I was the only one who wasn't turned to stone...<end>"
KO: "나만은 왜인지 돌이 되지 않았어….<end>"

## 550304
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わたしが剣を抜いた。　<end>"
EN: "I drew the sword.<end>"
KO: "…내가 검을 뽑았어.<end>"

## 550305

## 550306

## 550333

## 550334
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 550335

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 550340

## 550341

## 550342

## 550344

## 550349

## 550351
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 550352
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれは違う！<line-break>いいかげんに信じてほしいな…。　　　<line-break>あのときは、冒険者になりたてで…<line-break>ノエルの…、いや、あんたたちの仲間に<line-break>入りたかった。それだけだ。<end>"

KO: "그건 아니야! 이제 좀 믿어 줬으면 좋겠는데…. 그때는 모험자가 된 지 얼마 안 됐고… 노엘의…, 아니, 너희 동료가 되고 싶었어. 그것뿐이야.<end>"

## 550353
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "나와 함께 지평선 너머로 지는 석양을 보지 않을래?<end>"

## 550354
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그게 모험자로서 동료에 넣어 달라고 할 때 할 말인가?<end>"

## 550355
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그러니까… 그건 뭐라고 말해야 할지 몰랐던 거야.<end>"

## 550356
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっそぅ。<line-break>もう少し上手なウソがつけないの？　　<line-break>あなたを仲間に入れることに賛成した<line-break>ナーシェスの気まぐれに感謝するのね。<end>"

## 550357
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "뭐, 노엘은 포기하는 게 좋아.<end>"

## 550358

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 550359
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "…오해야.<end>"

## 550360

JP: "あ、あ、<value:$28>さん！<end>"
EN: "Oh! <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "아, 아, <value:$28> 씨!<end>"

## 550367
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、えと、また、助けていただきました。<line-break>あの…本当にありがとうございます！<end>"
EN: "Um... you've saved me again. Thank you so much!<end>"
KO: "아, 저, 또 도움을 받았네요. 그… 정말 감사합니다!<end>"

## 550368
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イヤねぇ…。みんなそろって<line-break>施文院の刺青してる。<line-break>　<line-break>あたしたち、<line-break>厄介なのに狙われてるわね。<end>"
EN: "How unpleasant... Every one of them bears the Inscription Order's tattoo. We've attracted the attention of a troublesome lot.<end>"
KO: "싫다 정말…. 다들 시문원의 문신을 하고 있어. 우리, 성가신 녀석들에게 찍힌 모양이네.<end>"

## 550369
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えと、…せもんいん？<end>"
EN: "Um... the Inscription Order?<end>"
KO: "저기, …세몬인?<end>"

## 550370
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "施文院とは、天空神ノトゥーンに　<line-break>仕える宗派のひとつだ。<line-break>　<line-break>もともとは、<line-break>各地を巡って邪悪なものを<line-break>封印していたのだ。<end>"
EN: "The Inscription Order is one of the sects devoted to Notun, the Sky God. Originally, its members traveled the lands sealing away evil.<end>"
KO: "시문원은 천공신 노툰을 섬기는 종파 중 하나다. 원래는 각지를 돌아다니며 사악한 것을 봉인하던 자들이지.<end>"

## 550371
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "中世には神聖王サラミスによって、<line-break>異端とされ、<line-break>ほとんどの宗徒が処刑された…。<line-break>だが、その生き残りは地下へ潜って<line-break>暗殺集団・告死天使を組織し、<line-break>歴史の裏舞台で暗躍を続けた。<end>"
EN: "In the Middle Ages, Holy King Salamis declared them heretics, and nearly all their adherents were executed. The survivors went underground and formed the assassin order known as the Angels of Death, continuing to work in the shadows of history.<end>"
KO: "중세에는 성왕 살라미스에게 이단으로 규정되어 대부분의 신도가 처형됐다…. 하지만 살아남은 자들은 지하로 숨어 암살집단 '고사천사'를 조직하고 역사의 뒤편에서 계속 암약했다.<end>"

## 550372
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、現在もな…。<end>"
EN: "And they remain active to this day...<end>"
KO: "그리고 지금도 말이지….<end>"

## 550373
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、この人たちは…。<end>"
EN: "Then these people are...<end>"
KO: "그럼 이 사람들은….<end>"

## 550374
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "間違いない。<line-break>施文院の暗殺者、告死天使だ。<end>"
EN: "There is no doubt. They're the Inscription Order's assassins―the Angels of Death.<end>"
KO: "틀림없어. 시문원의 암살자, 고사천사다.<end>"

## 550375
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0こんな、物騒な連中に<line-break>ねらわれるようなこと…したかしら。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、おかげでボウヤと<line-break>再会できたわけだし…。<line-break>フフフッ。<end>こんな、物騒な連中に<line-break>ねらわれるようなこと…したかしら。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、おかげで子猫ちゃんと<line-break>再会できたわけだし…。<line-break>フフフッ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0I wonder what we did to attract such dangerous company. Still, it did let me see you again, boy... Heh heh.<end>I wonder what we did to attract such dangerous company. Still, it did let me see you again, kitten... Heh heh.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0이런 험악한 녀석들에게 노려질 만한 짓을… 내가 했던가. 그래도 덕분에 꼬마와 다시 만났으니…. 후후후.<end>이런 험악한 녀석들에게 노려질 만한 짓을… 내가 했던가. 그래도 덕분에 아기고양이와 다시 만났으니…. 후후후.<end>"

## 550376
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦っているときの真剣な顔…<line-break>フフフ…、かわいかったわよ。<end>"
EN: "That serious look on your face while you fought... Heh heh. It was adorable.<end>"
KO: "싸울 때의 진지한 얼굴… 후후후… 귀여웠어.<end>"

## 550377
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec055-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの、それじゃ、<line-break>わたしはこれで失礼します！<end>"
EN: "U-um, I'll leave you now!<end>"
KO: "저, 저기, 그럼 저는 이만 실례하겠습니다!<end>"
