## 560001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（こんな遅くに…）<end>"
EN: "(At this hour...?)<end>"
KO: "(이렇게 늦게…)<end>"

## 560002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そろそろ、正体を現したらどうなんだ。<line-break>ナーシェス。<end>"
EN: "Isn't it about time you revealed your true identity, Narscess?<end>"
KO: "슬슬 정체를 드러내는 게 어때, 나셰스.<end>"

## 560003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（あの声は…）<end>"
EN: "(That voice...)<end>"
KO: "(저 목소리는…)<end>"

## 560004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、なんのことだ？<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Whatever do you mean?<end>"
KO: "훗, 무슨 소리냐?<end>"

## 560005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かつて、ディンガルの征服帝が<line-break>世界を支配しようとしたとき、　　　<line-break>　<line-break>七竜士を導いてその野望を砕いたのは<line-break>竜王の使いであるエルフの賢者<line-break>だったという…。<end>"
EN: "They say that when Dyneskal's Conqueror-Emperor sought to rule the world, an elven sage who served the Dragon King led the Seven Dragon Knights and shattered his ambition.<end>"
KO: "과거 딩갈의 정복제가 세계를 지배하려 했을 때, 칠룡사를 이끌어 그 야망을 꺾은 것은 용왕의 사자인 엘프 현자였다고 한다….<end>"

## 560006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほぅ…、詳しいな。<line-break>ただの、お軽い冒険者とは思えない。　<line-break>　<line-break>いや、なるほど、宗教上、いや職業上、<line-break>失われた伝承に詳しいのだったな。<line-break>おっと、もう足は洗ったのだったか。<end>"
EN: "Oh? You know your history. More than I'd expect from some carefree adventurer. Ah, of course. Your religion―no, your profession―made you well versed in lost lore. Oh, but you'd already left that life behind, hadn't you?<end>"
KO: "호오… 자세하군. 그저 가벼운 모험자라고는 생각되지 않아. 아니, 그렇군. 종교상… 아니 직업상 잃어버린 전승에 밝았었지. 아, 이제 그 일은 그만뒀던가.<end>"

## 560007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王の手先め。<line-break>何を企んでいる！<end>"
EN: "You lackey of the Dragon King! What are you plotting?<end>"
KO: "용왕의 앞잡이 놈. 무슨 꿍꿍이냐!<end>"

## 560008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一度は闇の手先となり、凶悪な魔物を<line-break>放った男に何を言う必要がある？<end>"
EN: "Why should I explain anything to a man who once served the darkness and unleashed a vicious monster?<end>"
KO: "한번은 어둠의 수하가 되어 흉악한 마물을 풀어놓은 남자에게 무슨 말을 할 필요가 있지?<end>"

## 560009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王の目的はなんだ？　あの子を使って<line-break>今度は何をしようというんだ。<end>"
EN: "What does the Dragon King want? What are you planning to do with that child this time?<end>"
KO: "용왕의 목적은 뭐지? 그 아이를 이용해 이번엔 무엇을 하려는 거야.<end>"

## 560010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ、世界の均衡をとること。　<end>"
EN: "Only to maintain the Balance of the World.<end>"
KO: "그저 세계의 균형을 맞추는 것.<end>"

## 560011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これまでも、そして、これからも<line-break>竜王様の目的は変わらぬ。<end>"
EN: "That has always been the Dragon King's purpose, and it always shall be.<end>"
KO: "지금까지도, 앞으로도 용왕님의 목적은 변하지 않는다.<end>"

## 560012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふざけるな！！　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>竜王と貴様がやってきたことは、<line-break>むやみに歴史に介入し、<line-break>混迷を深めただけじゃないか。<end>"
EN: "Don't give me that! All you and the Dragon King have done is meddle recklessly in history and deepen the chaos!<end>"
KO: "웃기지 마!! 용왕과 네놈이 한 짓은 함부로 역사에 개입해 혼란을 더 깊게 만든 것뿐이잖아.<end>"

## 560013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愚かな…。<line-break>我々はバランスをとっているのだ。<line-break>　<line-break>こざかしい人間どもが、<line-break>いたずらに数を増やし、<line-break>世界を破滅させぬよう…<end>"
EN: "Fool. We preserve the balance, lest insolent humans multiply unchecked and bring the world to ruin...<end>"
KO: "어리석군…. 우리는 균형을 맞추고 있다. 영악한 인간들이 함부로 수를 늘려 세계를 멸망시키지 않도록….<end>"

## 560014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界を分裂させておいて、<line-break>人間どもに共食いをさせ、　　　　<line-break>その数と力を抑制する。<line-break>世界を守り、浅はかな人間どもを<line-break>生かしておくには<line-break>竜王様の采配が必要なのだ。<end>"
EN: "We divide the world and make humans prey on one another, keeping their numbers and power in check. The Dragon King's guidance is necessary to protect the world and keep those shortsighted humans alive.<end>"
KO: "세계를 갈라놓고 인간들끼리 서로 잡아먹게 해 그 수와 힘을 억제한다. 세계를 지키고 어리석은 인간들을 살려 두려면 용왕님의 지휘가 필요한 것이다.<end>"

## 560015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "争いに消えた命はどうなる？<line-break>俺たちの悲しみはどうなる？<end>"
EN: "And the lives lost in those wars? What about our grief?<end>"
KO: "싸움 속에 사라진 목숨은 어떻게 되는 거지? 우리의 슬픔은 어떻게 되는 거냐?<end>"

## 560016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "知らんな。<end>"
EN: "I don't know.<end>"
KO: "모르겠군.<end>"

## 560017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part98.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ…。<end>"
EN: "Damn...<end>"
KO: "큭….<end>"

## 560018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰だ？<end>"
EN: "Who's there?<end>"
KO: "누구야?<end>"

## 560019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>さん！<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>あ、え…<value:$28>さんも、<line-break>となりにお泊まりだったんですね。<line-break>お話しできなくて、残念です。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>! Ah... so you were staying next door too, <value:$28>. It's a shame we didn't get a chance to talk.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28> 씨! 아, 저… <value:$28> 씨도 옆방에 묵고 계셨군요. 이야기하지 못해서 아쉬워요.<end>"

## 560020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、で、でも、<line-break>次にお会いするときは、きっと…。<line-break>あ、それじゃ、失礼します！<end>"
EN: "B-but surely the next time we meet... Oh! I should be going. Excuse me!<end>"
KO: "아, 하, 하지만 다음에 만날 때는 분명…. 아, 그럼 실례하겠습니다!<end>"

## 560021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんただと思ったよ…。<end>"
EN: "I thought it was you...<end>"
KO: "당신일 줄 알았어….<end>"

## 560022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ我を信じよ。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>滅びの日は近い。<line-break>天なる神と神の遣わしたる我に<line-break>祈る者のみが救われるのだ。<end>"
EN: "Believe only in me. The day of ruin draws nigh. Only those who pray to God in heaven and to me, whom God has sent, shall be saved.<end>"
KO: "그저 나를 믿어라. 멸망의 날은 가깝다. 하늘의 신과 그 신이 보낸 나에게 기도하는 자만이 구원받을 것이다.<end>"

## 560023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめろ！　やめんか！<end>"
EN: "Stop! I said stop!<end>"
KO: "그만둬! 그만두지 못해!<end>"

## 560024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こら、お前らもしょっぴくぞ！<end>"
EN: "Hey! Keep it up and I'll haul the lot of you in too!<end>"
KO: "이봐, 너희도 잡아간다!<end>"

## 560025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でたらめを言いふらして人心を惑わす<line-break>いんちき予言者が！<end>"
EN: "You false prophet! Spreading lies and leading the people astray!<end>"
KO: "헛소문을 퍼뜨려 사람들을 현혹하는 사기 예언가가!<end>"

## 560026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なにが滅びの日だ。な～にが神の使いだ。<line-break>もっともらしく杖なんて持ちおって。<end>"
EN: "“Day of ruin,” my foot. And what's this about being a divine messenger? Carrying a staff around to look the part, are you?<end>"
KO: "뭐가 멸망의 날이야. 뭐가 신의 사자야. 그럴듯하게 지팡이나 들고 말이지.<end>"

## 560027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おごり高ぶる罪深き輩よ。<line-break>神はお前たちの傲慢を打ち砕くだろう。<end>"
EN: "Proud and sinful fools. God shall crush your arrogance.<end>"
KO: "교만에 빠진 죄 많은 자들이여. 신은 너희의 오만을 깨뜨릴 것이다.<end>"

## 560028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだと？　ふざけおって。<line-break>痛い目にあわないとわからんようだな。<end>"
EN: "What did you say? Insolent bastard. Seems you won't understand until we beat some sense into you.<end>"
KO: "뭐라고? 장난치는군. 호되게 당해 봐야 알아듣겠나 보군.<end>"

## 560029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ！　せいぜい泣きわめいて、<line-break>お前の神様に救いを乞うがいい！<end>"
EN: "Hah! Go on, bawl your eyes out and beg your precious god to save you!<end>"
KO: "하! 실컷 울부짖으며 네 신에게 구원을 빌어 봐라!<end>"

## 560030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファスを助けなくては！<end>"
EN: "We have to help Elfas!<end>"
KO: "엘파스를 구해야 해!<end>"

## 560031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "関わらない方がいいだろう<end>"
EN: "Better not get involved.<end>"
KO: "관여하지 않는 게 좋겠지.<end>"

## 560032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無抵抗の人に暴力を振るうなんて<line-break>最低です！<end>"
EN: "Attacking someone who won't fight back is despicable!<end>"
KO: "저항하지 않는 사람에게 폭력을 휘두르다니 최악이에요!<end>"

## 560033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ、貴様ら。<line-break>まとめて牢にぶちこむぞ！<end>"
EN: "Who the hell are you? I'll throw the whole lot of you in jail!<end>"
KO: "뭐냐, 네놈들. 모조리 감옥에 처넣겠다!<end>"

## 560034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ、ネメアの威を借る<line-break>木っ端役人のくせに！　いくわよ！<end>"
EN: "Hah! You're nothing but a petty official hiding behind Nemea's authority! Let's go!<end>"
KO: "흥, 네메아의 위세를 빌린 말단 관리 주제에! 간다!<end>"

## 560035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レイヴン！<end>"
EN: "Raven!<end>"
KO: "레이븐!<end>"

## 560036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "레이븐! 왜 그래. 네메아를 만난 뒤로 너 이상해?<end>"

## 560037

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "What's the matter? Are you afraid?<end>"
KO: "왜 그래? 무서워?<end>"

## 560038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Heh heh... Poor thing.<end>"
KO: "후후후…, 불쌍하군.<end>"

## 560039

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "이걸 마셔라.<end>"

## 560040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "가자.<end>"

## 560042

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>씨!<end>"

## 560043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、えと…、なんか、うれしいんです。<line-break>助ける、っていう同じ判断ができて。<line-break>　<line-break>やっぱり…、えと、安心しました。<line-break>わたしも<value:$28>さんも、<line-break>目指すところは、同じなんだ…って。<end>"
EN: "Um... I'm just happy that we both made the same choice―to help. It was a relief to know that <value:$28> and I are working toward the same thing.<end>"
KO: "아, 그게… 왠지 기뻐요. 돕겠다는 같은 판단을 내려서요. 역시… 안심했어요. 저도 <value:$28> 씨도 향하는 곳은 같구나… 하고요.<end>"

## 560044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この間から…、少し悩んでいたんです！<line-break>焦燥の耳飾りのとき、アレでしたから…<line-break>でも…<end>"
EN: "I've been worried about it since the other day. After what happened with the Earring of Impatience... But...<end>"
KO: "며칠 전부터… 조금 고민하고 있었습니다! 초조의 귀걸이 때 그랬으니까요…. 하지만….<end>"

## 560045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル、行きましょ？<line-break>レイヴンをお願いね。<end>"
EN: "Come on, Noel. Look after Raven for me.<end>"
KO: "노엘, 가자. 레이븐을 부탁해.<end>"

## 560046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、えと…、はい！<end>"
EN: "Oh, um... yes!<end>"
KO: "아, 저기… 네!<end>"

## 560047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ねえ、ノエルは<line-break>あたしたちのリーダーなの。<line-break>　<line-break>レイヴンにとってもね…。<line-break>どういう気があるのか知らないけど<line-break>今、あのコを取ってくつもりなら…。<end>"
EN: "Listen. Noel is our leader. Raven's too. I don't know what you're after, but if you mean to take her away from us now...<end>"
KO: "…있잖아, 노엘은 우리 리더야. 레이븐에게도…. 무슨 생각인지 모르겠지만 지금 그 애를 데려갈 생각이라면….<end>"

## 560048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それなりの、<line-break>覚悟はしてほしいわね。<end>"
EN: "You'd better be prepared for the consequences.<end>"
KO: "그 나름의 각오는 해 줬으면 해.<end>"

## 560053

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560054

## 560061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml

## 560062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの、<value:$28>さん<line-break>わたしって、ちっちゃいですか？<end>"
EN: "Um, <value:$28>... am I very small?<end>"
KO: "저, 저기 <value:$28>님. 저, 작은 편인가요?<end>"

## 560063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、えと、この前、ボルダンの賞金首に<line-break>言われたんです。<line-break>　<line-break>小さすぎて、視界に入らないって…<line-break>…で、それ、ホントらしいんです。彼、<line-break>わたしの攻撃全部食らってましたから。<end>"
EN: "Um, one of Boldan's wanted men said so the other day. He said I was too small to see. And apparently it was true, because every one of my attacks hit him.<end>"
KO: "아, 저, 전에 볼단의 현상범한테 들었어요. 너무 작아서 시야에 들어오지도 않는다고…. …그런데 그거 진짜였던 것 같아요. 그 사람, 제 공격을 전부 맞았거든요.<end>"

## 560064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で、背がおっきくなりたいなあって、<line-break>そのとき、思いました！<line-break>　<line-break>あの、<value:$28>さん！<line-break>どうすれば背がおっきくなって<line-break>他人の視界に入れるんでしょう？<end>"
EN: "That's when I decided I wanted to be taller! So, <value:$28>, how can I grow tall enough for people to see me?<end>"
KO: "그래서 그때 키가 커지고 싶다고 생각했어요! 저기, <value:$28> 씨! 어떻게 해야 키가 커져서 다른 사람 눈에 들어올 수 있을까요?<end>"

## 560065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ。<value:$28>？<line-break>独り言言ってんじゃなかったんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>おチビちゃんと話してたんだ。<line-break>いやー、見えなくて<line-break>わからなかったぜ！　ハハ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, it's you, <value:$28>. So you weren't talking to yourself. You were talking to the little runt! Sorry, I couldn't see her! Ha ha!<end>"
KO: "뭐야, <value:$28>? 혼잣말하는 게 아니었네. 꼬마랑 얘기하고 있었구나. 이야, 안 보여서 몰랐어! 하하!<end>"

## 560066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "う～。ま、また言われてしまいました！<line-break>大きくなりたい…。<end>"
EN: "Ooh... I-I got called small again! I want to grow...<end>"
KO: "으으~. 또, 또 그런 말을 들었어요! 커지고 싶어….<end>"

## 560067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part98.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "牛乳だ。<end>"
EN: "Milk.<end>"
KO: "우유야.<end>"

## 560068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい？<end>"
EN: "What?<end>"
KO: "네?<end>"

## 560069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大きくなりたいなら、牛乳だ。<end>"
EN: "If you want to grow, drink milk.<end>"
KO: "크고 싶다면 우유다.<end>"

## 560070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "牛乳くらい飲んでます！<end>"
EN: "I already drink milk!<end>"
KO: "우유 정도는 마시고 있어요!<end>"

## 560071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "量が足りないんだ。　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Not enough of it.<end>"
KO: "양이 부족해.<end>"

## 560072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実は俺もチビでさ。<line-break>去年はお前よりも背が低かった。<end>"
EN: "I used to be tiny too. Last year, I was shorter than you.<end>"
KO: "사실 나도 꼬맹이였어. 작년엔 너보다 키가 작았지.<end>"

## 560073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが、その酒がめに一瓶分<line-break>毎日、牛乳を飲んだら、このとおり。<end>"
EN: "Then I started drinking a whole jar that size every day, and look at me now.<end>"
KO: "그런데 매일 그 술통에 한 병씩 우유를 마셨더니 이렇게 됐어요.<end>"

## 560074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、そ、そうだったんですか！<line-break>牛乳の量が足りなかったんですね！<line-break>　<line-break>えと、さっそく、毎日あれだけ<line-break>飲むことにします！！<line-break>あ、ありがとうございました！<end>"
EN: "R-really? So I just wasn't drinking enough! I'll start drinking that much every day right away! Thank you very much!<end>"
KO: "아, 그, 그랬군요! 우유 양이 부족했던 거네요! 그럼 바로 매일 그만큼 마시겠습니다!! 아, 감사합니다!<end>"

## 560075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、おい、ノエル！<line-break>今のウソ…。<end>"
EN: "Hey, wait, Noel! That was a lie...<end>"
KO: "아, 야, 노엘! 방금 건 거짓말….<end>"

## 560076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まじいな。<line-break>本気で飲むかな、あいつ…。<end>"
EN: "This is bad. She isn't really going to drink it, is she...?<end>"
KO: "…곤란하군. 저 녀석 진짜 마시려나….<end>"

## 560077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "腹こわすぞ！　まったく！<line-break>おーい！　ノエル～！　今のウソ！<end>"
EN: "She'll make herself sick! Honestly... Noel! I was lying!<end>"
KO: "배탈 난다! 정말! 어이, 노엘~! 방금 건 거짓말이야!<end>"

## 560078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしてあんたは<line-break>無理な仕事ばっかりやりたがるの？<end>"
EN: "Why do you always want to take jobs we can't handle?<end>"
KO: "왜 당신은 무리한 일만 하려고 하는 거야?<end>"

## 560079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無理じゃないさ。<line-break>こっちの方が、がっぽり稼げるしね。<end>"
EN: "We can handle them. Besides, these pay a fortune.<end>"
KO: "무리는 아니야. 이쪽이 훨씬 짭짤하게 벌 수 있거든.<end>"

## 560080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失敗したら元も子もないじゃない！<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>失敗するだけなら、まだいいわ。<line-break>この子が危険な目にあったら<line-break>どうするの？<end>"
EN: "If we fail, it will all be for nothing! And merely failing would be the least of it. What if this child gets hurt?<end>"
KO: "실패하면 아무 소용 없잖아! 실패만 한다면 그나마 괜찮아. 이 아이가 위험한 일을 당하면 어쩔 거야?<end>"

## 560081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいこと？<line-break>確実な仕事を数こなすのよ！<end>"
EN: "Listen to me. We take plenty of reliable jobs and earn our money that way!<end>"
KO: "알겠어? 확실한 일을 많이 해내는 거야!<end>"

## 560082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お姉さま。格好は大胆なくせに、<line-break>考えることは、みみっちいですね。<end>"
EN: "My lady... for someone who dresses so boldly, you do think rather small.<end>"
KO: "…언니. 차림은 대담하면서 생각하는 건 참 쩨쩨하네요.<end>"

## 560083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "堅実と言いなさい！　堅実と！<end>"
EN: "It's called being prudent! Prudent!<end>"
KO: "착실하다고 해! 착실하다고!<end>"

## 560084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0あら、ボウヤ、仕事熱心ね。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、あたしたちの仕事を<line-break>横取りしたら、<line-break>ただじゃおかないわよ。<end>あら、子猫ちゃん、仕事熱心ね。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、あたしたちの仕事を<line-break>横取りしたら、<line-break>ただじゃおかないわよ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Well, aren't you a diligent boy. But if you steal our job, you'll regret it.<end>Well, aren't you a diligent kitten. But if you steal our job, you'll regret it.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0어머 꼬마야, 일에 열심이네. 하지만 우리 일을 가로채면 가만두지 않을 거야.<end>어머 아기고양이, 일에 열심이네. 하지만 우리 일을 가로채면 가만두지 않을 거야.<end>"

## 560085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "金のことになるとコワイね、<line-break>お姉さまは。<end>"
EN: "Money certainly brings out the scary side of you, my lady.<end>"
KO: "돈 이야기가 나오면 무섭네, 누님은.<end>"

## 560086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんにちは！<line-break><value:$28>さんもお仕事を？　　<line-break>　<line-break>いつもカフィンとレイヴンが<line-break>仕事を選ぶのを手伝ってくれるんです。<end>"
EN: "Hello! Are you looking for work too, <value:$28>? Coffin and Raven always help me choose which jobs to take.<end>"
KO: "안녕하세요! <value:$28>님도 일을 하시나요? 늘 카핀과 레이븐이 제가 일을 고르는 걸 도와줘요.<end>"

## 560087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私に何か？<end>"
EN: "Did you want something?<end>"
KO: "나한테 무슨 용건이지?<end>"

## 560088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエルの仲間になった理由？　　　　<end>"
EN: "Why I became one of Noel's companions?<end>"
KO: "노엘의 동료가 된 이유?<end>"

## 560089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前に話す必要はなかろう。<line-break>…いずれ、いやでも知ることになる。<end>"
EN: "I have no need to tell you. In time, you'll find out whether you want to or not.<end>"
KO: "네게 말할 필요는 없겠지. …머지않아 싫어도 알게 될 테니.<end>"

## 560090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0ボウヤ、いいところに来たわ。<line-break>フフ、ここにおかけなさい。<end>子猫ちゃん、いいところに来たわ。<line-break>フフ、ここにおかけなさい。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Perfect timing, boy. Heh heh. Come and sit here.<end>Perfect timing, kitten. Heh heh. Come and sit here.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0꼬마야, 잘 왔어. 후후, 여기 앉으렴.<end>아기 고양이야, 잘 왔어. 후후, 여기 앉으렴.<end>"

## 560091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…天の救いだな。<line-break>あとはお前に頼んだ。<end>"
EN: "A gift from heaven. I'll leave the rest to you.<end>"
KO: "…하늘이 도왔군. 나머지는 네게 맡긴다.<end>"

## 560092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待つのよ、ナーシェス！<line-break>フフフ、３人で飲みましょう？<end>"
EN: "Wait, Narscess! Heh heh heh. Why don't the three of us have a drink?<end>"
KO: "기다려, 나셰스! 후후후, 셋이서 마시자?<end>"

## 560093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう十分に楽しませてもらった。<line-break>おばさんの色気ならな。<end>"
EN: "I've had quite enough of an old woman's charms.<end>"
KO: "이제 충분히 즐겼다. 아줌마의 색기 말이야.<end>"

## 560094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今、何か言った？<end>"
EN: "Did you just say something?<end>"
KO: "방금 뭐라고 했어?<end>"

## 560095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、美しいお姉さまの<line-break>相手をしてやってくれ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, keep this beautiful lady company for me.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 아름다운 누님 상대 좀 해 줘.<end>"

## 560096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナーシェス…、覚えてなさい。<end>"
EN: "Narscess... you'll pay for that.<end>"
KO: "나셰스…, 두고 봐.<end>"

## 560097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ、元気そうだな。　　　　　　　<line-break>今日は１人か。<line-break>　<line-break>あんたの仲間は、なんつうか…こう<line-break>変わった奴が多いな。<end>"
EN: "Hey. You look well. On your own today? Your companions are... how should I put this? An unusual bunch.<end>"
KO: "여, 건강해 보이네. 오늘은 혼자인가. 네 동료들은 뭐랄까… 좀 특이한 녀석들이 많군.<end>"

## 560098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヘヘ…、確かにうちも普通じゃない。<line-break>まぁ、まともなのは俺くらいかな。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Mine aren't exactly normal either. I suppose I'm the only sensible one.<end>"
KO: "헤헤… 확실히 우리도 평범하진 않아. 뭐, 정상적인 건 나 정도려나.<end>"

## 560099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん！<line-break>あ…レイヴンも。<line-break>　<line-break>２人して<line-break>なに話してたんですか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! Oh... Raven too. What were you two talking about?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님! 아… 레이븐도. 둘이서 무슨 이야기하고 있었어요?<end>"

## 560100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "子供には言えない話さ。<end>"
EN: "Nothing fit for children.<end>"
KO: "아이한텐 할 수 없는 이야기야.<end>"

## 560101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひ、ひどい！<line-break>わたし、もう子供じゃありません。<end>"
EN: "Th-that's mean! I'm not a child anymore.<end>"
KO: "너, 너무해! 나 이제 어린애가 아니야.<end>"

## 560102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大人だっていうなら、<line-break>夜中にベッドから落ちたり、<line-break>　<line-break>好き嫌い言って食べ物を<line-break>残したりしないことだ。<end>"
EN: "Then stop falling out of bed at night and leaving food on your plate because you don't like it.<end>"
KO: "어른이라고 할 거면 한밤중에 침대에서 떨어지거나 편식한다고 음식을 남기지 마.<end>"

## 560103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レイヴン！　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>さんの前で<line-break>そんなこと言うなんて。<line-break>いじわるっ！<end>"
EN: "Raven! Saying that in front of <value:$28>... You're so mean!<end>"
KO: "레이븐! <value:$28>님 앞에서 그런 말을 하다니. 심술궂어!<end>"

## 560104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クセのある仲間ばっかりで、<line-break>お互い苦労するね。<end>"
EN: "We're both saddled with some peculiar companions, aren't we?<end>"
KO: "개성 강한 동료들뿐이라 서로 고생이네.<end>"

## 560105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずるいです。<line-break>内緒話なんて！<end>"
EN: "That's not fair. Keeping secrets from me!<end>"
KO: "치사해요. 비밀 이야기라니!<end>"

## 560106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれ？<line-break>やきもち妬いてるのかな？<end>"
EN: "What's this? Are you jealous?<end>"
KO: "어라? 질투하는 건가?<end>"

## 560107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だって、<value:$28>さんを<line-break>独り占めするだもん。<end>"
EN: "Well, you were keeping <value:$28> all to yourself!<end>"
KO: "왜냐면 <value:$28>님을 독차지하잖아.<end>"

## 560108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああそう。お目当ては<line-break><value:$28>の方ね。<line-break>　<line-break>わかったよ。<line-break>邪魔者は去りましょう。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I see. It's <value:$28> you wanted. Very well. I'll make myself scarce.<end>"
KO: "아, 그래. 목적은 <value:$28> 쪽이구나. 알았어. 방해꾼은 사라져 줄게.<end>"

## 560109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当は何を話してたんですか？<end>"
EN: "What were you really talking about?<end>"
KO: "사실 무슨 얘기 했어요?<end>"

## 560110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><less>50あ、えと、<value:$28>さん、<line-break>こんにちは。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>その…、すごい活躍ぶりですね。<line-break>わたしも、負けないように、<line-break>がんばります！<end>あ、えと、<value:$28>さん、<line-break>その…、すごい活躍ぶりですね。　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$2B>なんてあだ名が<line-break>あるくらいですから！<line-break>わたしも、負けずにがんばります！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><less>50Oh, um... hello, <value:$28>. You've been doing amazing things. I'll have to work hard to keep up!<end>Um, <value:$28>... you've been doing amazing things. You even have a nickname like “<value:$2B>”! I'll work hard to keep up!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><less>50아, 저기, <value:$28> 씨, 안녕하세요. 그… 굉장히 활약하고 계시네요. 저도 지지 않도록 열심히 할게요!<end>아, 저기, <value:$28> 씨, 그… 굉장히 활약하고 계시네요. <value:$2B>라는 별명까지 있을 정도니까요! 저도 지지 않게 열심히 할게요!<end>"

## 560111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0あらボウヤ、偶然ね。<line-break>ボウヤもここに泊まるの？　<line-break>　<line-break>フフフ、そんな顔しないの。<line-break>取って食いやしないわ。<end>あら子猫ちゃん、偶然ね。<line-break>子猫ちゃんもここに泊まるの？<line-break>　<line-break>フフフ、そんな顔しないの。<line-break>取って食いやしないわ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Well, boy, what a coincidence. Are you staying here too? Heh heh. Don't look so worried. I won't eat you alive.<end>Well, kitten, what a coincidence. Are you staying here too? Heh heh. Don't look so worried. I won't eat you alive.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0어머 꼬마야, 우연이네. 너도 여기 묵는 거야? 후후후, 그런 표정 짓지 마. 잡아먹진 않아.<end>어머 아기고양이, 우연이네. 너도 여기 묵는 거야? 후후후, 그런 표정 짓지 마. 잡아먹진 않아.<end>"

## 560112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたか…、よく会うね。　　　　<end>"
EN: "You again. We keep running into each other.<end>"
KO: "너인가… 자주 마주치네.<end>"

## 560113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、座んなよ。ちょうど話し相手が<line-break>ほしかったところさ。<end>"
EN: "Go on, sit down. I was just looking for someone to talk to.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 앉아. 마침 말상대가 필요하던 참이야.<end>"

## 560114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いつか告死天使が俺たちを<line-break>襲ったことがあったろ。<line-break>　<line-break>あれは…俺を狙ってたんだ。<line-break>告死天使を抜けた俺を<line-break>殺しにきたのさ。<end>"
EN: "Remember when the Angels of Death attacked us? They were after me. They'd come to kill me for leaving them.<end>"
KO: "…언젠가 고사천사가 우리를 습격한 적 있었지. 그건… 나를 노린 거였어. 고사천사를 빠져나온 나를 죽이러 온 거야.<end>"

## 560115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう、俺は施文院の暗殺部隊　　　　　<line-break>告死天使の一員だった。<line-break>　<line-break>それも死の羽音と呼ばれる<line-break>屈指の暗殺者…<line-break>…いや、殺人機械だった。<end>"
EN: "Yes. I belonged to the Angels of Death, the Inscription Order's assassination unit. I was one of their finest assassins, known as Death's Wingbeat. No... I was a killing machine.<end>"
KO: "그래, 난 시문원의 암살부대 고사천사의 일원이었다. 그것도 '죽음의 날갯소리'라 불리던 손꼽히는 암살자… 아니, 살인기계였지.<end>"

## 560116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "感傷もなく殺し、感動もなく処理した。<line-break>あの日…初めてネメアと戦った<line-break>あの日までは…。<end>"
EN: "I killed without remorse and disposed of my targets without feeling. Until that day―the day I first fought Nemea.<end>"
KO: "감상도 없이 죽이고 감동도 없이 처리했다. 그날… 처음 네메아와 싸운 그날까지는….<end>"

## 560117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は、エルファスより<line-break>ネメア暗殺の命を受けた。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>勇者ネメア…。<line-break>そのときの俺には<line-break>相手が誰であろうと関係なかった。<end>"
EN: "Elfas ordered me to assassinate Nemea. Nemea the Hero... At the time, it made no difference to me who my target was.<end>"
KO: "나는 엘파스에게 네메아 암살 명령을 받았다. 용사 네메아…. 그때의 내겐 상대가 누구든 상관없었다.<end>"

## 560118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冷静に奴を誘き寄せ、追いつめた。<line-break>だが…<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I calmly lured him out and cornered him. But...<end>"
KO: "냉정하게 녀석을 유인해서 몰아붙였다. 하지만….<end>"

## 560119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前は強い、それ故私の強さがわかる。<line-break>そして恐怖する。<line-break>　<line-break>感情を持たない人形よ。<line-break>お前は恐怖を知った。<line-break>もはや人形ではない。人間だ。<end>"
EN: "You are strong. That is why you understand my strength―and why you fear it. You, the puppet without emotion, have learned fear. You are a puppet no longer. You are human.<end>"
KO: "너는 강하다. 그렇기에 내 강함을 안다. 그리고 두려워한다. 감정을 갖지 않은 인형이여. 너는 공포를 알았다. 이제 인형이 아니다. 인간이다.<end>"

## 560120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前を操る者はもういない。<line-break>自分で自分を操れるか！<line-break>自分で生きることができるか！<end>"
EN: "No one controls you now. Can you master yourself? Can you live by your own will?<end>"
KO: "이제 너를 조종하는 자는 없다. 스스로 자신을 다룰 수 있겠나! 스스로 살아갈 수 있겠나!<end>"

## 560121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "う、うわああああぁぁっ！<end>"
EN: "A-aaargh!<end>"
KO: "으, 으아아아아아앗!<end>"

## 560122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアを前にして、<line-break>俺は初めて恐怖を知った。<line-break>…そして逃げた。<end>"
EN: "Facing Nemea, I felt fear for the first time. And I ran.<end>"
KO: "네메아 앞에서 나는 처음으로 공포를 알았다. …그리고 도망쳤다.<end>"

## 560124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "暖かさ、優しさ、喜び…　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Warmth. Kindness. Joy...<end>"
KO: "따뜻함, 다정함, 기쁨…<end>"

## 560125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悲しみ、不安、嫉妬、そして…怒り…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>俺は感情の波に揺さぶられ、<line-break>もてあそばれて…また逃げ出した。<end>"
EN: "Sorrow, anxiety, jealousy... and anger. The tide of emotion tossed me about, played with me, until I ran away again.<end>"
KO: "슬픔, 불안, 질투, 그리고… 분노…. 나는 감정의 파도에 흔들리고 농락당하다가… 또 도망쳤다.<end>"

## 560126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエルに記憶を消す薬を飲ませ、<line-break>彼女の前を去った。<end>"
EN: "I gave Noel a potion to erase her memories, then left her.<end>"
KO: "노엘에게 기억을 지우는 약을 먹이고 그녀 앞을 떠났다.<end>"

## 560127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "遅かったな。<line-break>迎えを出そうかと思っていたところだ。<end>"
EN: "You're late. I was just considering sending someone to fetch you.<end>"
KO: "늦었군. 마중을 내보낼까 하던 참이야.<end>"

## 560128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、お前を殺しやしない。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. I have no intention of killing you.<end>"
KO: "훗, 널 죽이진 않아.<end>"

## 560129

## 560131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一からやり直しだ。もう一度<line-break>剣の握り方から教えてやろう。<line-break>　<line-break>いいな、死の羽音よ。<end>"
EN: "We shall begin again from nothing. I will teach you how to hold a sword once more. Do you understand, Death's Wingbeat?<end>"
KO: "처음부터 다시다. 다시 검 쥐는 법부터 가르쳐 주지. 알겠나, 죽음의 날갯소리여.<end>"

## 560132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死の羽音という殺人機械は<line-break>再び動き出した。<end>"
EN: "The killing machine called Death's Wingbeat began to move once more.<end>"
KO: "'죽음의 날갯소리'라 불리는 살인기계가 다시 움직이기 시작했다.<end>"

## 560133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "調子はどうだ？　死の羽音よ。<end>"
EN: "How do you feel, Death's Wingbeat?<end>"
KO: "상태는 어떠냐? 죽음의 날갯소리여.<end>"

## 560135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シルヴァという村に<line-break>魔物が封印されている。<line-break>　<line-break>その封印を解き、<line-break>村を壊滅させてくるのだ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "A monster is sealed within a village called Silva. Break the seal and destroy the village.<end>"
KO: "실바라는 마을에 마물이 봉인되어 있다. 그 봉인을 풀어 마을을 괴멸시키고 와라.<end>"

## 560136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの村には、告死天使の<line-break>秘密を握る者がいる。<line-break>ノエルという少女…。<line-break>これで、少女を抹殺することも<line-break>できよう。<end>"
EN: "Someone in that village knows the secrets of the Angels of Death: a girl named Noel. This will also serve to eliminate the girl.<end>"
KO: "그 마을에는 고사천사의 비밀을 쥔 자가 있다. 노엘이라는 소녀…. 이걸로 그 소녀를 말살할 수도 있겠군.<end>"

## 560137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、あんたに出くわした。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>石化獣の封印を解いた黒ずくめの男。<line-break>…あれが俺だ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Then I ran into you. The man in black who broke the petrifying beast's seal... that was me.<end>"
KO: "그리고 너와 마주쳤지. 석화수의 봉인을 푼 검은 옷의 남자. …그게 나다.<end>"

## 560138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "石化獣に立ち向かうノエルを見て、<line-break>俺は、また人間に戻っちまった。<line-break>　<line-break>そして…今度こそ<line-break>自分の意志で生きていこうと決めた。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "When I saw Noel stand against the petrifying beast, I became human again. This time, I resolved to live by my own will.<end>"
KO: "석화수에 맞서는 노엘을 보고 나는 다시 인간으로 돌아왔다. 그리고… 이번엔 내 의지로 살아가겠다고 결심했지.<end>"

## 560139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "告死天使からノエルを守るため<line-break>レイヴンと名乗って仲間に加わった。<line-break>　<line-break>だがエルファスは、何度となく追っ手を<line-break>差し向け、挙げ句の果てには、<line-break>カフィンを使って俺たちを消そうと…。<end>"
EN: "I took the name Raven and joined the group to protect Noel from the Angels of Death. But Elfas sent pursuers after us again and again. In the end, he even tried to use Coffin to dispose of us...<end>"
KO: "고사천사에게서 노엘을 지키기 위해 레이븐이라는 이름으로 동료에 합류했다. 하지만 엘파스는 몇 번이고 추격자를 보냈고, 끝내는 카핀을 이용해 우리를 없애려 했다….<end>"

## 560140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お笑いぐさだ…。<line-break>ノエルを守るどころか、<line-break>自分が厄介のもとになってる。<line-break>そのうえ、ネメアと対峙し、<line-break>またもや恐怖に押しつぶされた。<line-break>へへ…、救いようがないな。<end>"
EN: "It's laughable. Instead of protecting Noel, I'm the one causing trouble. Then I faced Nemea and let fear crush me all over again. Heh... I'm beyond saving.<end>"
KO: "웃기는 꼴이군…. 노엘을 지키기는커녕 내가 문제의 원인이 됐어. 게다가 네메아와 맞서고 또다시 공포에 짓눌렸지. 헤헤… 구제불능이야.<end>"

## 560141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "結局、俺は人間として、<line-break>自分で生きていく方法を知らないんだ。<end>"
EN: "In the end, I simply don't know how to live on my own as a human being.<end>"
KO: "결국 난 인간으로서 스스로 살아가는 방법을 모르는 거야.<end>"

## 560142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "間違いない。<line-break>この奥へ逃げ込んだようだ。<end>"
EN: "No doubt about it. It fled farther inside.<end>"
KO: "틀림없어. 이 안쪽으로 도망친 모양이야.<end>"

## 560143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度こそ、逃がさない！<end>"
EN: "It won't escape this time!<end>"
KO: "이번엔 놓치지 않아!<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml

## 560176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いいわ。そうしましょう。　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あなたをこんなに苦しめるなんて<line-break>一言、言ってやらないと<line-break>気が収まらない！<end>"

## 560177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おい、レイヴンって<line-break>単独活動を始めたのかい？　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>いっつも、小さな勇者様ノエルに<line-break>ぴったり、くっついてたのに<line-break>この間、１人でうろついてたって噂だ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, has Raven started working alone? He used to stick to little Hero Noel like glue, but I hear he was wandering around by himself the other day.<end>"
KO: "야, 레이븐이 단독 행동을 시작했대? 늘 작은 용사님 노엘한테 딱 붙어 다니더니, 얼마 전 혼자 돌아다니는 걸 봤다는 소문이야.<end>"

## 560178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "霊峰トールに行くって言ってたってさ。<line-break>ノエルにふられでもして、<line-break>神官にでもなるつもりなのかね。<end>"
EN: "They say he was headed for Sacred Mount Thor. Maybe Noel spurned him and he's decided to become a priest.<end>"
KO: "영봉 토르에 간다고 했다더군. 노엘에게 차이기라도 해서 신관이라도 될 생각인가 보지.<end>"

## 560179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "天空神ノトゥーンよ！<line-break>なぜ、あんな所に賭事場があるのだ！　<line-break>　<line-break>どうして、俺に金があるときに<line-break>毎度毎度、俺は賭事場にぶつかるのだ！<line-break>そして、いつもながら大負けするのだ！<end>"
EN: "O Notun, Sky God! Why did there have to be a gambling den in a place like that? Why is it that every time I have money, I stumble across one? And why do I always lose so badly?<end>"
KO: "천공신 노툰이여! 왜 그런 곳에 도박장이 있는 거냐! 왜 내가 돈이 있을 때마다 꼭 도박장을 만나게 되는 거냐! 그리고 왜 늘 크게 잃는 거냐!<end>"

## 560180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "妹たちは、俺を責めないだろう…。<line-break>だが、あのがっかりした顔を見ると…。<line-break>天空神よ！　俺に金を…！<end>"
EN: "My sisters won't blame me... But I can't bear those disappointed faces. Sky God! Grant me money...!<end>"
KO: "여동생들은 나를 탓하지 않겠지…. 하지만 그 실망한 얼굴을 보면…. 천공신이여! 내게 돈을…!<end>"

## 560181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560187

## 560189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…死の羽音レイヴンが<line-break>この辺りで行くところ、な。　　<line-break>　<line-break>…俺はやっぱりついていない。<line-break>呪いがかかっていて<line-break>俺はそれを言うとさらに…。<end>"

## 560198

## 560199

## 560200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "めずらしい客人だ。<line-break>死の羽音…いや、レイヴン。　　<line-break>　<line-break>残念ながら、私に会いに来たわけでは<line-break>ないようだが…。<line-break>　<end>"

## 560201

## 560206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死の羽音…これまでにない<line-break>傑作であったはずだが…　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>どこで育て方を間違えたのか…。<end>"

## 560210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、人間の悪い癖だ。<line-break>つまらんことで大局を見失う。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>いつまでも、こだわっていたいなら<line-break>勝手にするがいい。<line-break>一生が短い分、悩むのも短いだろう。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. A bad human habit: losing sight of the larger picture over trifles. If you want to keep dwelling on it forever, suit yourself. Human lives are short, so at least the worrying won't last long.<end>"
KO: "흥, 인간의 나쁜 버릇이지. 사소한 일에 매달려 큰 흐름을 놓친다. 계속 집착하고 싶다면 마음대로 해라. 평생이 짧으니 고민하는 시간도 짧겠지.<end>"

## 560212

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このあいだ、<line-break>故郷の村に行ってきました。<line-break>　<line-break>カフィンにも、<line-break>もちろんナーシェスや<line-break>レイヴンに内緒で…。<end>"
EN: "I went back to my home village the other day. I kept it secret from Coffin, and of course from Narscess and Raven.<end>"
KO: "얼마 전에 고향 마을에 다녀왔습니다. 카핀은 물론 나셰스와 레이븐에게도 비밀로 하고….<end>"

## 560218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "石化獣を封じても、<line-break>村はもとに戻っていませんでした。<end>"

KO: "석화수를 봉인했는데도 마을은 원래대로 돌아오지 않았습니다.<end>"

## 560219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "みんな石のまま…。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>努力しても、<line-break>どうしようもないことって、<line-break>あるのかな…。<end>"
EN: "Everyone was still stone... Are there some things that no amount of effort can ever change?<end>"
KO: "다들 돌인 채로…. 노력해도 어쩔 수 없는 일이 있는 걸까….<end>"

## 560220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうですよね。<line-break>願い続ければかないますよね。　　<line-break>　<line-break>私、どうしたんだろ。<line-break>そう、きっと、この話も<line-break>ハッピーエンドで終わるんですよ。<end>"
EN: "Y-you're right. If we keep wishing, it'll come true. What's gotten into me? Of course this story will have a happy ending.<end>"
KO: "그, 그렇죠. 계속 바라면 이루어지겠죠. 나 왜 이러지. 그래, 분명 이 이야기도 해피엔딩으로 끝날 거예요.<end>"

## 560221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今は、まだ悲しい場面だけど…。<end>"
EN: "Even if we're still in the sad part right now...<end>"
KO: "지금은 아직 슬픈 장면이지만….<end>"

## 560223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファスを倒す者がいるとすれば、<line-break>それは<value:$28>だろう。<line-break>　<line-break>そして、<value:$28>は、<line-break>比類なき力を手に入れる。<line-break>　<end>"

## 560224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうならないことを祈るがね。<end>"
EN: "I pray it never comes to that.<end>"

## 560225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml

## 560227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml

## 560229

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この子が、あの子猫ちゃんをどんなに<line-break>慕っているか、知ってるでしょう？<line-break>　<line-break>この子に何をさせるつもり？<end>"
EN: "You know how much this child adores that little kitten, don't you? What are you asking this child to do?<end>"
KO: "이 아이가 그 꼬마를 얼마나 따르는지 알잖아? 이 아이에게 뭘 시킬 생각이야?<end>"

## 560231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人間どもが好むよう、あえて<line-break>回りくどく説明してやったんだが…<end>"
EN: "I went out of my way to explain it in the roundabout fashion you humans favor...<end>"
KO: "인간들이 좋아하도록 일부러 빙빙 돌려 설명해 줬건만….<end>"

## 560232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最初からこうすべきだったか。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps I should have done this from the start.<end>"
KO: "처음부터 이렇게 했어야 했나.<end>"

## 560233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王様の意を伝える。　　　　<end>"
EN: "I bring word of the Dragon King's will.<end>"

## 560234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル、予の見いだせし勇者よ。　<line-break>もしも汝が予の意を果たし、<line-break>　<line-break>世界の均衡を崩さんとする<line-break>もう１人の無限のソウルの持ち主を<line-break>排除したなら、<end>"

KO: "노엘, 짐이 찾아낸 용사여. 그대가 짐의 뜻을 이루어 세계의 균형을 무너뜨리려는 또 한 명의 무한의 소울 소유자를 제거한다면,<end>"

## 560235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "汝の願いを聞き入れよう。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>たとえ島にあっても、<line-break>石化の呪いを解くことなど、<line-break>造作もないことだ。<end>"
EN: "I shall grant your wish. Even on the island, lifting the curse of petrification would be a simple matter.<end>"
KO: "그대의 소원을 받아들이겠다. 섬에 있다 해도 석화의 저주를 푸는 것쯤은 어려운 일이 아니다.<end>"

## 560236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…村のみんなを？<line-break>だけど…、だけど…<end>"

## 560237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그런 짓을 하면 노엘이 얼마나 괴로워할지 모르겠어?<end>"

## 560238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560239

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560240

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560252
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560253
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願い！　言わないで！　　　　　　　<line-break>聞くのが…恐い…。<line-break>　<line-break>一番大切な仲間なのに<line-break>ときどき、すごく遠くに感じる…。<line-break>　<end>"

## 560254

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560256

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560259
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560260

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560261

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560310

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "思ったとおり、施文院の奴らよ。<line-break>しつこいったらないわ。<line-break>　<line-break>誰がお目当てだか知らないけど、<line-break>もしも身に覚えがあるなら、<line-break>さっさと白状なさい？<end>"

## 560311

## 560312
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あらそう、レイヴン。さっそく<line-break>正直に名乗り出てくれてうれしいわ。<line-break>　<line-break>だけど、あんたが<line-break>告死天使にねらわれるような大物には<line-break>見えないんだけど？<end>"
EN: "Oh, really, Raven? How kind of you to volunteer so honestly. But you don't look important enough for the Angels of Death to target.<end>"
KO: "그래, 레이븐. 바로 솔직하게 이름을 밝혀 줘서 기쁘네. 하지만 네가 고사천사에게 노려질 만큼 거물로는 안 보이는데?<end>"

## 560313
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0ボウヤ、今日はありがと。<line-break>このお礼は、いつかゆっくりと、ね。<end>子猫ちゃん、今日はありがと。<line-break>このお礼は、いつかゆっくりと、ね。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Thanks for today, boy. I'll take my time thanking you properly someday, all right?<end>Thanks for today, little kitten. I'll take my time thanking you properly someday, all right?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0꼬마야, 오늘은 고마웠어. 이 답례는 언젠가 천천히, 알지?<end>아가씨, 오늘은 고마웠어. 이 답례는 언젠가 천천히, 알지?<end>"

## 560314
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、<value:$28>さん<line-break>失礼します！<end>"
EN: "Well then, if you'll excuse me, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "그럼 <value:$28>님, 실례하겠습니다!<end>"

## 560315
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "物騒になったものだ。<line-break>白昼堂々と襲ってくるとは。<line-break>　<line-break>あいにく、こんなところで<line-break>犬死するわけには<line-break>いかないのでな。<end>"
EN: "Things have certainly grown dangerous if they'll attack in broad daylight. Unfortunately, I can't afford to die for nothing in a place like this.<end>"
KO: "험한 세상이 됐군. 대낮에 당당히 습격하다니. 유감이지만 이런 곳에서 개죽음할 수는 없어서 말이야.<end>"

## 560316
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ、いらっしゃい？<line-break>何人だって相手してあげるわ。<end>"
EN: "Well, come on. I'll take on as many of you as you like.<end>"
KO: "후후, 어서 와? 몇 명이든 상대해 줄게.<end>"

## 560317
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>さん、<line-break>ありがとうございます！　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>わたしも、一日も早く<line-break><value:$28>さんみたいな<line-break>一人前の冒険者になりたいな…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, thank you, <value:$28>! I hope I can become a full-fledged adventurer like <value:$28> as soon as possible...<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28> 씨, 감사합니다! 저도 하루빨리 <value:$28> 씨 같은 어엿한 모험자가 되고 싶어요…<end>"

## 560318
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、施文院の連中なの？<end>"
EN: "More people from the Inscription Order?<end>"
KO: "또 시문원 녀석들이야?<end>"

## 560319
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "間違いない、告死天使だ。<end>"
EN: "No doubt about it. They're Angels of Death.<end>"
KO: "틀림없어, 죽음의 천사다.<end>"

## 560320
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたし…、告死天使の親玉を<line-break>たぶらかした覚えはないけど。<end>"
EN: "I don't recall seducing the leader of the Angels of Death...<end>"
KO: "나… 고사천사의 우두머리를 꼬신 기억은 없는데.<end>"

## 560321
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、お前の色気にひっかかる程度の<line-break>相手なら、ありがたいのだが…。<end>"
EN: "If our opponent were the sort to fall for your charms, I'd be grateful...<end>"
KO: "그래, 네 색기에 넘어갈 정도의 상대라면 고맙겠지만….<end>"

## 560322
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どういう意味？<end>"
EN: "What's that supposed to mean?<end>"
KO: "무슨 뜻이야?<end>"

## 560323
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こらえて、こらえて。<line-break>怒鳴るとしわが増えるよ。<end>"
EN: "Easy, easy. Shouting will give you more wrinkles.<end>"
KO: "참아, 참아. 소리 지르면 주름 늘어.<end>"

## 560324
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたねぇ！！<end>"
EN: "Why, you...!<end>"
KO: "너 정말!!<end>"

## 560325
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、えと、ケンカは、<line-break>ダメですよ。<end>"
EN: "Um... please don't fight.<end>"
KO: "아, 저기, 싸우면 안 돼요.<end>"

## 560326
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん。<line-break>ありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님. 감사합니다.<end>"

## 560327
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪いが、急いでいる。<line-break>道を空けろ。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, but there's no time. Stand aside.<end>"
KO: "미안하지만 급하다. 길을 비켜라.<end>"

## 560328
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "通してくれそうもないわよ。<line-break>あたし、乱暴なタイプ嫌いなんだけど…<line-break>　<line-break>どうすんの？<line-break>この心ときめかない人たち。<end>"
EN: "They don't look inclined to let us through. I don't care for the violent type... So what do we do with this lot? They don't exactly set my heart aflutter.<end>"
KO: "통과시켜 줄 것 같지 않네. 난 난폭한 타입 싫어하는데…. 어떡할래? 전혀 설레지 않는 사람들이야.<end>"

## 560329
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうするって…こいつら<line-break>引き返したって追いかけてくるんだろ？<end>"
EN: "What do we do? Even if we turn back, they'll only chase us, right?<end>"
KO: "어떻게 하냐고… 이 녀석들 돌아가도 쫓아올 거잖아?<end>"

## 560330
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっちゃうしかないんじゃない？<line-break>ノエル、気をつけろ。<end>"
EN: "Looks like we'll have to take them down. Noel, be careful.<end>"
KO: "해치우는 수밖에 없지 않을까? 노엘, 조심해.<end>"

## 560331
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0ボウヤ、ありがと。<line-break>やさしいのね。<end>子猫ちゃん、ありがと。<line-break>やさしいのね。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Thanks, boy. How sweet of you.<end>Thanks, little kitten. How sweet of you.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0꼬마야, 고마워. 상냥하구나.<end>아기 고양이야, 고마워. 상냥하구나.<end>"

## 560332
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ、ご褒美が欲しいの？<end>"
EN: "Oh? Do you want a reward?<end>"
KO: "후후, 상이 받고 싶어?<end>"

## 560333
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいのよ。<line-break>正直に言ってごらんなさい。<end>"
EN: "It's all right. Go on, tell me honestly.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아. 솔직하게 말해 봐.<end>"

## 560334
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カフィン…、<line-break>子供をからかうもんじゃないよ。<end>"
EN: "Coffin... don't tease the child.<end>"
KO: "카핀…, 아이를 놀리면 안 돼.<end>"

## 560335
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんただって子供のくせに、<line-break>生意気なこと言わないの。<end>"
EN: "You're a child yourself, so don't get cheeky with me.<end>"
KO: "너도 애면서 건방진 소리 하지 마.<end>"

## 560336
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、お前から見れば<line-break>たいていは子供の年以下だろうな。<end>"
EN: "Well, from your perspective, most people must seem like children―or younger.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 네 눈에는 대부분 애들 나이 이하로 보이겠지.<end>"

## 560337
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、告死天使だ。<end>"
EN: "The Angels of Death again.<end>"
KO: "또 고사천사다.<end>"

## 560338
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうして、<line-break>わたしたちを、ねらうのかな…。<end>"
EN: "Why do they keep coming after us...?<end>"
KO: "왜… 우리를 노리는 걸까….<end>"

## 560339
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエルが望むなら、<line-break>今度、問いつめてあげる。<end>"
EN: "If Noel wants, I'll interrogate one next time.<end>"
KO: "노엘이 원한다면 다음에 내가 따져 줄게.<end>"

## 560340
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無駄だ。簡単に口を割るような<line-break>連中じゃない。<end>"
EN: "It would be pointless. They're not the sort to talk easily.<end>"
KO: "소용없어. 쉽게 입을 열 녀석들이 아니야.<end>"

## 560341
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたしの美貌には<line-break>逆らえないわ。<end>"
EN: "No one can resist my beauty.<end>"
KO: "내 미모에는 거역할 수 없지.<end>"

## 560342
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えと…、<value:$28>さん、<line-break>ありがとうございました！　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>わたしも<value:$28>さんに<line-break>負けないくらい、強く、なります！<end>"
EN: "Um... thank you, <value:$28>! I'll become strong enough to hold my own beside <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "저기… <value:$28>님, 감사합니다! 저도 <value:$28>님에게 지지 않을 만큼 강해질게요!<end>"

## 560353
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>こんなところで会うなんて珍しいわね。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, what a surprise, running into you here.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 이런 곳에서 만나다니 드문 일이네.<end>"

## 560354
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私？<line-break>ちょっとしたお使い。<line-break>そっちは？<end>"
EN: "Me? Just running a little errand. What about you?<end>"
KO: "나? 잠깐 심부름. 너는?<end>"

## 560355
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。<line-break>調子はいいみたいね。<line-break>安心したわ。<line-break>じゃあ、私はロセンに戻るわ。<line-break>しっかりね。<line-break><value:$28><end>"
EN: "I see. You seem to be doing well. That's a relief. Well, I'm heading back to Rocen. Keep at it, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그래. 상태는 좋아 보이네. 안심했어. 그럼 난 로센으로 돌아갈게. 잘해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 560356
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28><line-break>そっちの調子はどう？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, how are things with you?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 그쪽은 어때?<end>"

## 560357
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その余裕。<line-break>…さすがね。<line-break>カルラ様が目を掛けるわけだわ。<line-break>と、とにかく、あなたがいると<line-break>私も負けていられないって気に<line-break>なるから。<end>"
EN: "That composure of yours... impressive, as ever. No wonder Lady Karula has taken an interest in you. A-anyway, having you around makes me feel like I can't afford to fall behind either.<end>"
KO: "그 여유… 역시 대단하네. 카를라 님이 눈여겨볼 만해. 아, 아무튼 네가 있으면 나도 질 수 없다는 기분이 드니까.<end>"

## 560358
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、お互いがんばりましょう。<end>"
EN: "Well then, let's both give it our best.<end>"
KO: "그럼 서로 힘내죠.<end>"

## 560359
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、<value:$28><line-break>こんな所で会うなんて怪しいわね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. Fancy running into you here. How suspicious.<end>"
KO: "어머, <value:$28>. 이런 곳에서 만나다니 수상하네.<end>"

## 560360
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふん、どうかしらね。<line-break>あなたの言うことなんて<line-break>信用できないわ。<line-break>カルラ様を裏切るような真似をすれば…<line-break>いつでも私が成敗してあげるわ。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. I'm not so sure. I can't trust a word you say. If you ever do anything to betray Lady Karula... I'll deal with you myself.<end>"
KO: "흥, 글쎄. 네 말 같은 건 믿을 수 없어. 칼라 님을 배신하는 짓을 하면… 언제든 내가 처단해 주겠어.<end>"

## 560361
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>調子はどう？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, how are things going?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 상태는 어때?<end>"

## 560362
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。<line-break><value:$28>も今や<line-break>我が軍に欠かせない存在だものね。<end>"
EN: "I see. You've become indispensable to our army, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그래. <value:$28>도 이제 우리 군에 없어서는 안 될 존재니까.<end>"

## 560363
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、だからこそ<line-break>今度の戦いは要注意よ。<line-break>今までどおりにはいきそうにないから。<line-break>私も１つの軍を預かる身だから<line-break>あなたのことをかまっていられない。<end>"
EN: "But that's precisely why you need to be careful in the coming battle. It won't be like the ones we've fought before. I have an army of my own to command, so I won't have time to look after you.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그래서 이번 싸움은 특히 조심해야 해. 지금까지처럼 되진 않을 것 같거든. 나도 한 군을 맡은 몸이라 너를 신경 쓸 여유가 없어.<end>"

## 560364
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、私のこと心配してくれてるのね<line-break>私は大丈夫よ<line-break>私もあなたのことを信頼してるから<end>"
EN: "Heh. So you're worried about me. I'll be fine. I trust you too.<end>"
KO: "후후, 나를 걱정해 주는 거구나. 난 괜찮아. 나도 널 믿고 있으니까.<end>"

## 560365
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあ、<value:$28>、<line-break>しっかりね。<end>"
EN: "Well then, <value:$28>, keep your wits about you.<end>"
KO: "그럼, <value:$28>, 잘해 봐.<end>"

## 560366
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>あなたはうまくいってる？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, are things going well for you?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 너는 잘되고 있어?<end>"

## 560367
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうなの…<line-break>私は相変わらず忙しいわ。<end>"
EN: "Is that so... I'm as busy as ever.<end>"
KO: "그렇구나…. 난 여전히 바빠.<end>"

## 560368
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当に猫の手も借りたいくらい。<line-break>休もうとしても、頭の中から<line-break>軍務のことが離れなくって<line-break>だから、ちょっと疲れ気味。<end>"
EN: "I could use all the help I can get. Even when I try to rest, I can't get my military duties out of my head. So I'm a little worn out.<end>"
KO: "정말 고양이 손이라도 빌리고 싶을 정도야. 쉬려고 해도 머릿속에서 군무가 떠나질 않아서 좀 지쳤어.<end>"

## 560369
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ。<line-break><value:$28>に<line-break>心配されるなんてね。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. Imagine having <value:$28> worry about me.<end>"
KO: "후후후. <value:$28>에게 걱정을 다 받다니.<end>"

## 560370
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、あなたがいて<line-break>こうして話すこともできるから<line-break>正直、助かってると思うわ。<line-break>私１人だったら、<line-break>重圧に押しつぶされてしまったかも<line-break>しれない…<end>"
EN: "Honestly, having you here to talk to helps. If I were on my own, the pressure might have crushed me...<end>"
KO: "하지만 당신이 있어서 이렇게 이야기할 수도 있으니 솔직히 도움이 돼. 나 혼자였다면 중압감에 짓눌렸을지도 몰라…<end>"

## 560371
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、泣き言はここまで。<line-break>私は戻るとするわ。<line-break>それじゃ。<end>"
EN: "That's enough complaining. I should get back. See you.<end>"
KO: "자, 푸념은 여기까지. 난 돌아갈게. 그럼.<end>"

## 560372
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>そちらの調子はどう？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, how are things on your end?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 그쪽은 어때?<end>"

## 560373
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがね。<line-break>でも今度の戦いは<line-break>これまでのようにはいかないわ。<end>"
EN: "Just as I'd expect from you. But this next battle won't be like the ones we've fought before.<end>"
KO: "역시 대단하네. 하지만 이번 싸움은 지금까지처럼 쉽지 않을 거야.<end>"

## 560374
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敵将のゼネテスは剣狼と呼ばれ<line-break>優れた指揮官として評判よ。<line-break>くれぐれも油断しないことね。<line-break>あなたの腕前は理解しているけど、<line-break>えてして、そういうところに<line-break>油断が生まれるものよ。<end>"
EN: "The enemy commander Xenetes is known as the Sword Wolf and has a reputation as a skilled commander. Whatever you do, don't let your guard down. I know how capable you are, but that's precisely where carelessness can creep in.<end>"
KO: "적장 제네테스는 검랑이라 불리며 뛰어난 지휘관으로 이름 높아. 절대 방심하지 마. 네 실력은 알고 있지만 오히려 그런 데서 방심이 생기는 법이니까.<end>"

## 560375
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言っておくけど、<line-break>別に私はあなたの命を<line-break>心配しているのではないわ。<line-break>それじゃ、私は忙しいから<line-break>行くわ。<end>"
EN: "Just so we're clear, it's not as though I'm worried about your life. Now, I'm busy, so I'll be going.<end>"
KO: "말해 두지만, 딱히 네 목숨을 걱정하는 건 아니야. 그럼 난 바쁘니까 갈게.<end>"

## 560376
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>こんな所で何をしている！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, what are you doing in a place like this?!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 이런 데서 뭐 하고 있어!<end>"

## 560377
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何を言ってるの！<line-break>指揮官のあなたがそんなことでは<line-break>全軍の士気に関わるわ。<end>"
EN: "What are you talking about? If you, the commander, act like that, it'll affect the morale of the entire army.<end>"
KO: "무슨 말을 하는 거야! 지휘관인 네가 그러면 전군의 사기에 영향을 줘.<end>"

## 560378
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度の戦いでは、<line-break>どんな奇策が待っているか<line-break>わからないのよ。<line-break>そんな状態では敵に<line-break>つけ込むスキを与えるようなものだわ。<line-break>しっかりしてもらいたいものね。<end>"
EN: "There's no telling what sort of cunning scheme the enemy has waiting for us in the coming battle. In your present state, you're practically inviting them to exploit an opening. I need you to pull yourself together.<end>"
KO: "이번 싸움에서는 어떤 기책이 기다리고 있을지 몰라. 그런 상태로는 적에게 파고들 틈을 주는 거나 마찬가지야. 정신 바짝 차려 줬으면 좋겠어.<end>"

## 560379
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私に余裕がない？<line-break>当たり前じゃない、戦争なのよ！<end>"
EN: "You think I'm tense? Of course I am. We're at war!<end>"
KO: "내게 여유가 없다고? 당연하잖아, 전쟁이라고!<end>"

## 560380
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたのようにぼんやりしている方が<line-break>どうかしているわ。<line-break>どういう神経をしているのか…。<line-break>私は忙しいから<line-break>あなたもしっかりやりなさい。<end>"
EN: "You're the strange one, standing around in a daze like that. I can't imagine what you're thinking. I'm busy, so you had better get your act together too.<end>"
KO: "당신처럼 멍하니 있는 쪽이 이상한 거예요. 무슨 신경을 가진 건지…. 저는 바쁘니까 당신도 제대로 하세요.<end>"

## 560381
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もし、カルラ様や私の動きを<line-break>邪魔するようなことがあれば…。<line-break>その時は…<end>"
EN: "If you do anything to interfere with Lady Karula or me... then...<end>"
KO: "만약 칼라 님이나 제 움직임을 방해하는 일이 있다면…. 그때는…<end>"

## 560382
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "長生きしたいのなら<line-break>そのつもりでいることね。<end>"
EN: "If you want to live a long life, you'd better keep that in mind.<end>"
KO: "오래 살고 싶다면 그럴 각오는 해 둬.<end>"
