## 560389
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人の死に慣れてしまったのでしょうか。<line-break>幼いときから一緒にいた方の死なのに<line-break>感慨がありません…<line-break>でも、安らかなお顔でしたね。<end>"
EN: "Have I grown accustomed to death? Someone who had been with me since childhood is dead, and yet I feel nothing... Still, there was such peace on that face.<end>"
KO: "사람의 죽음에 익숙해져 버린 걸까요. 어릴 때부터 함께한 분의 죽음인데도 아무 감정이 없어요…. 그래도 편안한 얼굴이셨네요.<end>"

## 560390
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバ様も思えば、<line-break>おかわいそうな方です。<line-break>　<line-break>何かにつけて、いとこの<line-break>レムオン様、エスト様に比べられ、<line-break>あげつらわれてきたのですから。<end>"
EN: "When you think about it, Tartuba is to be pitied as well. At every turn, Tartuba was compared to the cousins, Lemghon and Est, and picked apart.<end>"
KO: "생각해보면 타르튜바 님도 참 가엾은 분입니다. 사사건건 사촌인 레무온 님, 에스트 님과 비교당하고 비난받아왔으니까요.<end>"

## 560391
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバ様も貴族に生まれなければ…<line-break>このような悲しいことには<line-break>ならなかったのかもしれません<end>"
EN: "If Tartuba had not been born into the nobility... perhaps this tragedy might never have happened.<end>"
KO: "타르튜바 님도 귀족으로 태어나지 않았다면… 이렇게 슬픈 일은 없었을지도 모릅니다.<end>"

## 560392
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…<line-break>少しだけ、一人にさせてください<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Please leave me alone for a little while.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님… 잠시만 혼자 있게 해 주세요.<end>"

## 560393
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "びっくりさせてしまって<line-break>ごめんなさい。<line-break>大丈夫ですよ。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry I startled you. It's all right.<end>"
KO: "놀라게 해서 미안해요. 괜찮아요.<end>"

## 560394
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さっきの水蛇がミズチです。<line-break>妖精種族の中でも、<line-break>もっとも精霊に近い存在なんです。<end>"
EN: "The water serpent from earlier is a Mizuchi. Of all the fairy races, Mizuchi are the closest to spirits.<end>"
KO: "아까 그 물뱀이 미즈치입니다. 요정 종족 중에서도 가장 정령에 가까운 존재죠.<end>"

## 560395
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何も食べません。もちろん人間も。<line-break>とても、おとなしくて、自分から<line-break>何かを襲うことはないんです。<end>"
EN: "Mizuchi don't eat anything―not even humans. They're very docile and never attack anything of their own accord.<end>"
KO: "아무것도 먹지 않습니다. 물론 인간도요. 아주 얌전해서 먼저 무언가를 습격하는 일은 없습니다.<end>"

## 560396
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、すいません。<line-break>ひとりでしゃべっちゃって…<line-break>アキュリュースへは観光ですか？<end>"
EN: "Oh, I'm sorry. I've been doing all the talking... Are you sightseeing in Aqyurius?<end>"
KO: "아, 죄송해요. 혼자 계속 말했네요… 아큐류스에는 관광하러 오셨나요?<end>"

## 560397
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか…<line-break>あ、私、水の精霊神殿の巫女で<line-break>イークレムンって言います。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I see... By the way, I'm Iiklmn, a priestess at the Temple of the Water Spirit.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요… 아, 저는 물의 정령신전 무녀 이크레문이라고 합니다.<end>"

## 560398
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん<line-break>…ですか。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... is that right?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨…인가요.<end>"

## 560399
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はじめまして、<line-break><value:$28>さん。<end>"
EN: "It's a pleasure to meet you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "처음 뵙겠습니다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 560400
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イークレムン様…<end>"
EN: "Lady Iiklmn...<end>"
KO: "이클레문 님…<end>"

## 560401
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いくら、ミズチと<line-break>言葉を交わせるからといって、<line-break>よその方を驚かすものではありませんよ。<end>"
EN: "Just because you can speak with Mizuchi doesn't mean you should frighten a visitor like that.<end>"
KO: "아무리 미즈치와 말을 나눌 수 있다고 해도 남을 놀라게 하면 안 됩니다.<end>"

## 560402
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "申しわけありません、神官長様。<end>"
EN: "My apologies, High Priest.<end>"
KO: "죄송합니다, 신관장님.<end>"

## 560403
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イークレムン様。<line-break>まもなく着きます。お早く。<end>"
EN: "Lady Iiklmn. We're almost there. Please hurry.<end>"
KO: "이크레문 님. 곧 도착합니다. 서두르십시오.<end>"

## 560404
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん。<line-break>もし、お時間があるのでしたら、<line-break>神殿を訪ねてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, if you have the time, please come and visit the temple.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님. 시간이 괜찮으시면 신전에 들러 주세요.<end>"

## 560405
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来たか…。<end>"
EN: "So, you have come...<end>"
KO: "왔나….<end>"

## 560407
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やはり…<line-break>君には人類を革新へと導くだけの<line-break>素質があるのかもしれん。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps you do possess the potential to guide humanity toward Humanity's Evolution.<end>"
KO: "역시… 자네에겐 인류를 혁신으로 이끌 자질이 있을지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 560409
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こいつを倒すことができたなら…<line-break>君の可能性を信じよう。<end>"
EN: "Defeat this, and I will believe in your potential.<end>"
KO: "이 녀석을 쓰러뜨릴 수 있다면… 네 가능성을 믿겠다.<end>"

## 560410
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、君の力を見せてくれ。<end>"
EN: "Now, show me your power.<end>"
KO: "자, 네 힘을 보여줘.<end>"

## 560411
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ<line-break>見事だ。<line-break> <line-break>よかろう。<line-break>このベルゼーヴァ・ベルライン<line-break>人類の革新のため、力を貸そう。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Impressive. Very well. I, Beelzeva Berline, shall lend you my strength in the cause of Humanity's Evolution.<end>"
KO: "흥, 훌륭하다. 좋아. 이 벨제바 벨라인, 인류의 혁신을 위해 힘을 빌려주지.<end>"

## 560412
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫屋敷で呼び出すのか。<line-break>なるほど、かの猫屋敷か。<line-break>興味深いな。<line-break>それでは心待ちにしている。<line-break>さらばだ、諸君。<end>"
EN: "You mean to summon me at Cat Manor? Ah, that Cat Manor. Intriguing. I shall look forward to it. Farewell, all of you.<end>"
KO: "고양이 저택에서 불러내는 건가. 그렇군, 그 고양이 저택인가. 흥미롭군. 그럼 기대하고 있겠다. 잘 있게, 제군들.<end>"

## 560413
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>か。<line-break>やはり、ここで会うことになったな。<line-break> <line-break>運命の女神の書く脚本は陳腐だな。<line-break>あまりにも予定調和だ。<line-break>そうは思わぬか？<end>"
EN: "So, <value:$28>. It seems we were destined to meet here after all. The Goddess of Fate writes such hackneyed scripts. This is all far too predictable, wouldn't you agree?<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>인가. 역시 여기서 만나게 됐군. 운명의 여신이 쓰는 각본은 진부하군. 너무 예정조화적이야. 그렇게 생각하지 않나?<end>"

## 560414
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "汝には、礼を言わねばならぬ。<line-break>マノンの力を除去し、<line-break>余の力を解放してくれたこと…。<line-break>そして、この身体を手に入れる<line-break>おぜん立てをしてくれたこと。<line-break>おかげで余はこうして自由に動ける。<end>"
EN: "I owe you my thanks. You removed Manon's power and set mine free. You even made it possible for me to acquire this body. Thanks to you, I can now move freely.<end>"
KO: "그대에게 감사를 해야겠군. 마논의 힘을 제거해 나의 힘을 해방해 준 일…. 그리고 이 육체를 얻을 발판을 마련해 준 일. 덕분에 나는 이렇게 자유롭게 움직일 수 있다.<end>"

## 560415
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我が子、ベルゼーヴァの身体を<line-break>乗っ取ってな。フフフ…。<line-break>さすがは我が子よ。強く、相性がよい。<end>"
EN: "I took possession of my own son Beelzeva's body. Heh heh heh... As expected of my son: strong, and an excellent match for me.<end>"
KO: "내 아이 벨제바의 육체를 빼앗아서 말이지. 후후후…. 역시 내 아이야. 강하고 궁합도 좋아.<end>"

## 560416
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "余の目的は、この奥にある<line-break>先人類の闇の禁呪デモリッシュ…。<line-break>おそらくは汝の目的も同じであろう。<line-break>つまり、残念ながら<line-break>余と汝の目的は競合する。<line-break>余は汝を排除せねばならぬ。<end>"
EN: "My objective lies deeper within: Demolish, the dark Forbidden Spell of the humanity that came before. I suspect your objective is the same. Unfortunately, our objectives conflict. I must eliminate you.<end>"
KO: "짐의 목적은 이 안에 있는 선인류의 어둠의 금주 데몰리시…. 아마 네 목적도 같겠지. 즉 유감스럽게도 짐과 네 목적은 충돌한다. 짐은 너를 제거해야 한다.<end>"

## 560417
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "余と同じ夢を見ながら<line-break>その夢を汝やネメアごときに託した<line-break>愚かなる息子は、もうおらぬ。<line-break>その息子の思い人が、余を阻もうと<line-break>しておる…。<line-break>フフ…陳腐な脚本ではないか？<end>"
EN: "My foolish son dreamed the same dream as I, yet entrusted it to the likes of you and Nemea. Now he is gone, and the one he loved seeks to stop me. Heh... What a hackneyed script, wouldn't you say?<end>"
KO: "짐과 같은 꿈을 꾸면서도 그 꿈을 너와 네메아 따위에게 맡긴 어리석은 아들은 이제 없다. 그 아들이 사랑한 여자가 짐을 막으려 하고 있군…. 후후… 진부한 각본 아닌가?<end>"

## 560418
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかろう、余は息子への手向けに<line-break>汝を余の中に吸収しよう。<line-break>無限のソウルは余を強くするであろう。<end>"
EN: "Very well. As an offering to my son, I shall absorb you into myself. The Infinite Soul will make me stronger.<end>"
KO: "좋다. 아들에게 바치는 조의로서 너를 내 안에 흡수하겠다. 무한의 소울은 나를 더욱 강하게 하리라.<end>"

## 560419
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆくぞ、無限のソウルを持つ者よ。<line-break>人類の新たなる革新のため、<line-break>尊き犠牲となるがよい。<end>"
EN: "Here I come, Bearer of the Infinite Soul. Become a noble sacrifice for a new phase of Humanity's Evolution.<end>"
KO: "간다, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여. 인류의 새로운 혁신을 위해 고귀한 희생이 되어라.<end>"

## 560420
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐふっ、余が、超人類である余が…<line-break>汝ごときに敗れるとはな…。フフ…。<line-break>しょせんはベルゼーヴァの身体か…。<line-break>余は死なぬ。１０００年を待ったのだ…<line-break>もう少し時期を待とう…。<line-break>ふ、ふ、ふふ…。<end>"
EN: "Ghh... That I, a superhuman, should be defeated by the likes of you... Heh... After all, this is Beelzeva's body... I cannot die. I waited a thousand years... I shall simply wait a little longer... Heh... heh heh heh...<end>"
KO: "크흑, 초인류인 내가… 그대 따위에게 패하다니…. 후후…. 결국 벨제바의 육체인가…. 나는 죽지 않는다. 천 년을 기다렸다…. 조금 더 때를 기다리지…. 후, 후, 후후….<end>"

## 560421
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "デモリッシュを習得した。<end>"
EN: "Learned Demolish.<end>"
KO: "데몰리시를 습득했다.<end>"

## 560422
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "憐れみはいらん…。<line-break>父に…あのウジ虫に利用されるならば<line-break>しょせん、私もその程度だったのだ…。<line-break>…<value:$28>、<line-break>…人類の…革新を…頼む。<line-break>人は…あまりにも…弱すぎる…。<end>"
EN: "I need no pity... If I could be used by my father―by that maggot―then that was all I amounted to... <value:$28>... I entrust Humanity's Evolution to you. People are... far too weak...<end>"
KO: "동정은 필요 없다…. 아버지에게… 그 구더기 같은 놈에게 이용당할 정도라면 결국 나도 그 정도였던 거다…. …<value:$28>, …인류의… 혁신을… 부탁한다. 인간은… 너무나… 약하다….<end>"

## 560423
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人が…強くあったならば…<line-break>この世の悲しみは…もっと…<line-break>少ないはずだ…、頼む…革新を…。<end>"
EN: "If people were stronger... there would be less sorrow in this world... Please... bring about Humanity's Evolution...<end>"
KO: "인간이… 강했다면… 이 세상의 슬픔은… 좀 더… 적었을 것이다… 부탁한다… 혁신을….<end>"

## 560424
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やはり…やはり…<line-break>君とはここで会うことになったか。<line-break><value:$28>よ…。<end>"
EN: "Just as I thought... just as I thought. So we were destined to meet here, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "역시… 역시… 너와는 여기서 만나게 됐군. <value:$28>여….<end>"

## 560425
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "運命の女神の書く脚本は、どうして、<line-break>こんなに陳腐で予定調和なのだろうな？<line-break>その脚本で動く役者としては不満だな。<end>"
EN: "Why must the script penned by the Goddess of Fate be so trite and predictable? As one of its actors, I find it rather unsatisfying.<end>"
KO: "운명의 여신이 쓰는 각본은 왜 이렇게 진부하고 예정조화뿐인 걸까? 그 각본대로 움직이는 배우로서는 불만이군.<end>"

## 560426
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の目的はこの奥にある<line-break>先人類の遺した<line-break>闇の禁呪デモリッシュ…。<end>"
EN: "My objective lies beyond: Demolish, the dark Forbidden Spell left behind by the humanity that came before...<end>"
KO: "내 목적은 이 안쪽에 있는 선인류가 남긴 어둠의 금주 데몰리시….<end>"

## 560427
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あらたなる人類のために<line-break>旧人は滅ぼさねばならぬ。<end>"
EN: "For the sake of a new humanity, the old humanity must be destroyed.<end>"
KO: "새로운 인류를 위해 구인류는 멸망해야 한다.<end>"

## 560428
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あらたなる人類のために<line-break>旧人は滅ぼさねばならぬ。<end>"
EN: "For the sake of a new humanity, the old humanity must be destroyed.<end>"
KO: "새로운 인류를 위해 구인류는 멸망해야 한다.<end>"

## 560429
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうやら、話しても無駄なようだな。<line-break>残念だが、<line-break>私は君を排除せねばならん。<end>"
EN: "It seems there is no point in talking. Regrettably, I must eliminate you.<end>"
KO: "아무래도 말로 해서는 소용없는 것 같군. 유감이지만 나는 자네를 제거해야 하네.<end>"

## 560430
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆくぞ！<end>"
EN: "Here I come!<end>"
KO: "간다!<end>"

## 560431
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "憐れみはいらん…。<line-break>君に…ここで敗れるのならば…<line-break>しょせん、私もその程度だったのだ…。<end>"
EN: "I need no pity... If I am to fall to you here... then this was all I ever amounted to...<end>"
KO: "동정은 필요 없다…. 너에게… 여기서 진다면… 결국 나도 그 정도였던 거다….<end>"

## 560432
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>、<line-break>…人類の…革新を…頼む。<line-break>人は…あまりにも…弱すぎる…。<line-break>人が…強くあったならば<line-break>この世の悲しみは…もっと…<line-break>少ないはずだ…、頼む…革新を…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Please... bring about Humanity's Evolution. Humanity is... far too weak... If people were stronger, there would surely be... less sorrow in this world... Please... achieve Humanity's Evolution...<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>, …인류의… 혁신을… 부탁한다. 인간은… 너무나… 약하다…. 인간이… 강했다면 이 세상의 슬픔은… 더… 적었을 거다…. 부탁한다… 혁신을….<end>"

## 560433
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "革新は…ならぬ…のか…！<line-break>弱き…人の…悲しみ…は…<line-break>続く…のか…。ああ…！<end>"
EN: "Can Humanity's Evolution... not be achieved...? Must the sorrow... of weak humanity... go on...? Ah...!<end>"
KO: "혁신은… 안 되는… 것인가…! 약한… 사람의… 슬픔은… 계속되는… 건가…. 아아…!<end>"

## 560434
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪いが、はずしてくれ。<line-break>片付けてしまいたい仕事がある。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, but leave me alone. I have some work I want to finish.<end>"
KO: "미안하지만 자리를 비켜 줘. 끝내고 싶은 일이 있어.<end>"

## 560435
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、君か。<line-break>この私に何か用か？<end>"
EN: "Ah, it's you. What business do you have with me?<end>"
KO: "아, 자네인가. 이 나에게 무슨 용건이지?<end>"

## 560436
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たまには君から話すがいい。<line-break>今日は、君の話を聞こう。<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者の身の上を。<line-break>君の故郷は？　ご両親は健在か？<line-break>君の活躍ぶりを、<line-break>さぞや誇らしくお思いのことだろう。<end>"
EN: "You do the talking for a change. Today, I'd like to hear your story―the story of the Bearer of the Infinite Soul. Where are you from? Are your parents still alive? They must be very proud of all you've accomplished.<end>"
KO: "가끔은 네가 이야기해 봐. 오늘은 네 이야기를 듣겠다. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자의 신상 말이지. 고향은 어디지? 부모님은 건강하신가? 네 활약을 분명 자랑스러워하시겠군.<end>"

## 560437
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは…余計なことを言ったようだ。<end>"
EN: "I... seem to have said something I shouldn't have.<end>"
KO: "이런… 쓸데없는 말을 한 것 같군.<end>"

## 560438
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…この私も両親はいない。<line-break>私を息子と呼ぶ者はいるが、<line-break>奴など親とは思いたくもない。<end>"
EN: "...I have no parents either. There is someone who calls me son, but I would never consider that wretch a parent.<end>"
KO: "…나도 부모가 없다. 나를 아들이라 부르는 자는 있지만 녀석 따위를 부모로 생각하고 싶지도 않아.<end>"

## 560439
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フ…先に心ない質問をしたのだ。<line-break>愚かな生みの親について<line-break>話さぬわけにもいくまい。<line-break>私を生んだ両親は、<line-break>ディンガル家の一族。しかし、<line-break>その地位は低く、生活は貧しかった。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. I was the one who asked such a thoughtless question, so I can hardly refuse to speak of my foolish birth parents. They belonged to the Dyneskal family, but their standing was low and they lived in poverty.<end>"
KO: "후… 먼저 무정한 질문을 했으니 어리석은 친부모에 대해 말하지 않을 수도 없겠군. 나를 낳은 부모는 딘갈 가문의 일족이었다. 하지만 지위는 낮고 생활은 가난했지.<end>"

## 560440
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこにゾフォル…あの予言者が現れた。<line-break>当時、宰相であったゾフォルは…<line-break> <line-break>一夜、肉体を貸せば、<line-break>その後の地位と財産を保証する。<line-break>…そう言ったという。<end>"
EN: "Then Zofor appeared―the prophet. Zofor was Chancellor at the time. “Lend your bodies for a single night, and your position and fortune afterward will be assured.” So the story goes.<end>"
KO: "그곳에 조포르… 그 예언자가 나타났다. 당시 재상이던 조포르는… 하룻밤 몸을 빌려주면 이후의 지위와 재산을 보장하겠다. …그렇게 말했다고 한다.<end>"

## 560441
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "父と母は、ゾフォルに連れられて<line-break>魔道の塔へとおもむき、<line-break>シャロームに肉体を貸した。<line-break>そして、奴の子を身ごもったのだ。<end>"
EN: "My father and mother were taken by Zofor to the Arcane Tower, where they lent their bodies to Shalom. That was how his child was conceived.<end>"
KO: "아버지와 어머니는 조포르에게 이끌려 마도의 탑으로 가 샬롬에게 육체를 빌려주었다. 그리고 그의 아이를 가졌다.<end>"

## 560442
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのとき受けた強力な魔法がたたって、<line-break>間もなくふたりは世を去った。<line-break>シャロームの子ベルゼーヴァを残して。<end>"
EN: "The powerful magic they were subjected to took its toll, and before long they both passed away, leaving behind Beelzeva, Shalom's child.<end>"
KO: "그때 받은 강력한 마법의 후유증으로 얼마 지나지 않아 두 사람은 세상을 떠났다. 샬롬의 아이 벨제바를 남기고.<end>"

## 560443
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼らは愚かだろうか？<line-break>…彼らは弱かっただけだ。<line-break>あるいはその両方かも知れない。<line-break>なんにせよ、弱さが、<line-break>この世の悲しみを生むのだ。<end>"
EN: "Were they fools? No. They were merely weak. Or perhaps they were both foolish and weak. In any case, it is weakness that gives rise to the sorrow in this world.<end>"
KO: "그들은 어리석었을까? …그저 약했을 뿐이다. 아니면 둘 다였을지도 모르지. 어쨌든 약함이 이 세상의 슬픔을 낳는 것이다.<end>"

## 560450
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、来てくれてたんだー。<line-break>ちょうどいいや。<line-break><line-break>あんたに伝えなきゃいけないことが<line-break>あったんだ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, there you are. Perfect timing. There's something I need to tell you.<end>"
KO: "아, 와 줬구나. 잘됐네. 네게 전해야 할 말이 있었어.<end>"

## 560451
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍の内情？<line-break>ああ、いーからいーから。<line-break>どーせ、筒抜けだかんねー。<end>"
EN: "The inner workings of the Liberation Army? Oh, don't worry about that. It all leaks out anyway.<end>"
KO: "해방군 사정? 아, 됐어 됐어. 어차피 다 새고 있으니까.<end>"

## 560452
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなことよりも、<line-break>次はリベルダムの攻略が待ってるから、<line-break>戦いに備えて強くなっててよ。<end>"
EN: "More importantly, we'll be taking Liberdam next, so get stronger and be ready to fight.<end>"
KO: "그런 것보다 다음에는 리벨덤 공략이 기다리고 있으니까 싸움에 대비해 강해져 있어.<end>"

## 560453
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それと、ここには<line-break>ひんぱんに顔を出すようにね。<line-break>期待してるからさ。ヨロシクねー。<end>"
EN: "And make sure you stop by here often. I'm counting on you, okay?<end>"
KO: "그리고 여기엔 자주 얼굴을 비추도록 해. 기대하고 있으니까. 잘 부탁해~.<end>"

## 560454
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、そうだ。あんたさぁ…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, right. Say...<end>"
KO: "아, 맞다. 너 말이야….<end>"

## 560455
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "騎士とかになってぇ、盾とかほしい？<line-break>騎士の証だっていうくだらない盾。<end>"
EN: "Want to become a knight or something and get yourself a shield? One of those stupid shields that's supposed to prove you're a knight.<end>"
KO: "기사가 돼서 방패 같은 거 갖고 싶어? 기사 증표라는 시시한 방패 말이야.<end>"

## 560456
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part98.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "欲しい<end>"
EN: "I want one.<end>"
KO: "갖고 싶어<end>"

## 560457
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part98.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらない<end>"
EN: "I don't want one.<end>"
KO: "필요 없어<end>"

## 560458
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うっわー、権威主義ナノねー。<line-break>あーんなうざいものがほしいわけ？<line-break>ま、防御力は増すけどさ。<line-break>攻撃は最大の防御って気持ちでなきゃ。<line-break>あたしのカッコ見なよ。ったく。<line-break>うーい、こちらさんに盾一丁。<end>"
EN: "Wow, you're such a sucker for authority. You really want one of those annoying things? Well, it does improve your defense. But you've got to believe the best defense is a good offense. Just look at my outfit. Honestly. All right, one shield for our friend here.<end>"
KO: "우와~ 권위주의적이네. 그런 귀찮은 게 갖고 싶은 거야? 뭐, 방어력은 오르지만. 공격이야말로 최고의 방어라는 마음가짐이어야지. 내 차림 좀 봐. 정말. 자, 여기 방패 하나.<end>"

## 560459
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほい、ひざまづいて。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "All right, kneel down, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "자, 무릎 꿇어. <value:$28>.<end>"

## 560460
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ、いい眺めっしょ？<line-break>あたしのアンヨちゃんてば<line-break>きれいだもんねー。<end>"
EN: "Hee-hee, nice view, huh? My little leggies are just so pretty.<end>"
KO: "우후후, 보기 좋죠? 제 다리 정말 예쁘거든요~.<end>"

## 560461
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんか難しい儀礼用のセリフが<line-break>長々あったけど面倒なので省略。<line-break>ほい、盾あげる。以上。<end>"
EN: "There was some long, complicated ceremonial speech, but it was a pain, so I'm skipping it. Here, you get a shield. That's it.<end>"
KO: "뭔가 어려운 의례용 대사가 길게 있었는데 귀찮으니 생략. 자, 방패 줄게. 이상.<end>"

## 560462
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神速の攻撃、これ青竜軍のスタイルよ。<line-break>わかってないよねー、あんたってさ。<line-break>とっとと行っちゃってよ。<end>"
EN: "Lightning-fast attacks―that's the Azure Dragon Army way. You really don't get it, do you? Now hurry up and get out of here.<end>"
KO: "신속한 공격, 그게 청룡군 스타일이야. 전혀 모르네, 너. 얼른 가 버려.<end>"

## 560463
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そーだよね。くだらない盾なんて。<line-break>神速の攻撃、これが青竜軍のスタイル。<line-break>わかってるじゃん。<line-break>あんたってイケてるよ。<line-break>んじゃ、また来てねー。<line-break>待ってるわん。<end>"
EN: "Exactly. Who needs some stupid shield? Lightning-fast attacks―that's the Azure Dragon Army way. See, you get it. You're pretty cool, you know. Come back again, okay? I'll be waiting!<end>"
KO: "그렇지. 쓸데없는 방패 따윈 필요 없어. 신속한 공격, 그게 청룡군의 스타일이지. 잘 알고 있네. 너 꽤 괜찮은데. 그럼 또 와. 기다릴게.<end>"

## 560464
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、そろそろ、リベルダムも<line-break>攻略しとこうかって思うわけ。<line-break>その作戦を説明するわ。<line-break>リベルダム市民、アンティノが<line-break>我々ディンガル帝国に内通しているのは<line-break>知ってのとおり。<end>"
EN: "Well, I think it's about time we took Liberdam, so let me explain the plan. As you know, Antino, a citizen of Liberdam, has been secretly working with the Dyneskal Empire.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 슬슬 리벨덤도 공략해 둘까 싶어. 그 작전을 설명하지. 리벨덤 시민 안티노가 우리 딘갈 제국에 내통하고 있는 건 알고 있겠지.<end>"

## 560465
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あり、言ってなかったっけ？<line-break>実は解放軍のエライ人が裏切り者なのだ。<line-break>そりゃ解放軍もダメダメだーね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, didn't I mention that? One of the Liberation Army's big shots is a traitor. They really are hopeless, aren't they?<end>"
KO: "어라, 말 안 했었나? 사실 해방군의 높은 사람이 배신자야. 이래서 해방군도 글렀지.<end>"

## 560466
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で、今回のリベルダム攻略も<line-break>そのアンティノにリベルダム城門を<line-break>中から開けさせて、一気に叩くわけ。<end>"
EN: "So this time, we'll have Antino open Liberdam's gates from the inside, then strike all at once.<end>"
KO: "그래서 이번 리벨덤 공략도 그 안티노에게 리벨덤 성문을 안에서 열게 한 뒤 단숨에 치는 거야.<end>"

## 560467
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "リベルダムの金持ちどもは<line-break>城門を頼りにして安心しきってるぶん、<line-break>いざ城門が破られたら大混乱だね。<line-break>こっちは被害なしに街に侵入し<line-break>パニックに乗じて制圧するだけ。<line-break>簡単に言えばこんなとこ。質問は？<end>"
EN: "Liberdam's rich folk feel so safe behind those gates that they'll be thrown into utter chaos when they fall. We just march into the city without taking any losses and seize it in the panic. That's the plan in a nutshell. Questions?<end>"
KO: "리벨덤의 부자들은 성문만 믿고 완전히 안심하고 있으니, 막상 성문이 뚫리면 대혼란이겠지. 우리는 피해 없이 도시에 들어가 혼란을 틈타 제압하면 그만이야. 간단히 말하면 이런 거지. 질문 있어?<end>"

## 560468
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "将軍。アンティノとやらを<line-break>そこまで信用してよろしいのですか？<end>"
EN: "General, are you certain we can place that much trust in this Antino?<end>"
KO: "장군. 안티노라는 자를 그렇게까지 믿어도 괜찮습니까?<end>"

## 560469
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くす。だいじょーぶ。<line-break>あたしは彼を信用してんじゃない。<line-break>利用するだけ。<line-break>自分は誰かを裏切っといて<line-break>自分は裏切られないと考えてんだから、<line-break>彼はしやあせな人やね。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Don't worry. I don't trust him. I'm just using him. He betrays someone else, then assumes no one would ever betray him. What a blissful life he must lead.<end>"
KO: "후후. 괜찮아. 난 그를 믿는 게 아니야. 이용할 뿐이지. 자기는 누군가를 배신해 놓고 자기는 배신당하지 않을 거라 생각하니, 참 행복한 사람이네.<end>"

## 560470
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、アンティノも…？<end>"
EN: "Then Antino will also...?<end>"
KO: "그럼 안티노도…?<end>"

## 560471
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もっちろん。<line-break>さて、他に質問がないようなら、<line-break>全軍進撃と行こーかな！<end>"
EN: "Of course. Now, if there are no more questions, let's get the whole army moving!<end>"
KO: "물론이지. 자, 다른 질문이 없다면 전군 진격으로 갈까!<end>"

## 560472
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンの先発隊も<line-break>うまく突入できたみたいだし、<line-break>まずは作戦成功ってところやね。<line-break>さーて、それじゃ、リベルダムを<line-break>徹底的に破壊しちゃおっかな。<end>"
EN: "Looks like Irene's advance force got in without a hitch, so I'd call the operation a success. Now then, I think it's time we thoroughly demolished Liberdam.<end>"
KO: "아이린의 선발대도 잘 돌입한 것 같고, 일단 작전 성공이네. 자, 그럼 리벨덤을 철저히 부숴 볼까.<end>"

## 560473
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>の言いたいことも<line-break>わかるけどさ。<line-break>ま、戦争だかんね～、これってば。<end>"
EN: "I know what you're trying to say, <value:$28>. But this is war, you know.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>이 무슨 말 하는지는 알겠지만. 뭐, 이건 전쟁이니까 말이야~.<end>"

## 560474
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだディンガルに逆らおうって連中が<line-break>抵抗するのを断念するくらいに<line-break>リベルダムを破壊すればぁ…<line-break>このあとのロストール攻略なんかも<line-break>サクサク進んで、最終的には<line-break>全体の被害が小さく抑えられるっしょ。<end>"
EN: "If we destroy Liberdam so thoroughly that anyone still thinking of defying Dyneskal gives up resisting, the Rostorl Campaign will go much more smoothly, and overall losses will be lower in the end, right?<end>"
KO: "아직 딩갈에 맞서려는 녀석들이 저항을 포기할 만큼 리벨덤을 파괴해 버리면… 이후 로스톨 공략도 척척 진행되고 결국 전체 피해도 작게 줄일 수 있겠지.<end>"

## 560475
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンみたく<line-break>甘～いこと言って<line-break>ダダこねないで。<end>"
EN: "Don't whine and carry on with the same soft-hearted nonsense as Irene.<end>"
KO: "아이린처럼 달콤한 소리 하면서 떼쓰지 마.<end>"

## 560476
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "報告します！<line-break>アイリーン将軍が、アンティノの放った<line-break>デスギガースに苦戦しているとのことです！<end>"
EN: "Reporting! General Irene is having trouble with the Death Gigas unleashed by Antino!<end>"
KO: "보고드립니다! 아이린 장군이 안티노가 풀어놓은 데스기가스에게 고전 중이라고 합니다!<end>"

## 560477
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "デスギガース？<line-break>ま～だ、そんなの隠し持ってんだ。<line-break>ウザイなぁ…<line-break>しょーがない、<value:$28>は<line-break>アイリーンの応援に行って。<line-break>残りはあたしと一緒に市街の破壊ね。<end>"
EN: "Death Gigas? So he was still hiding something like that. What a nuisance... Fine. <value:$28>, go help Irene. Everyone else, come with me. We're demolishing the city.<end>"
KO: "데스기가스? 아직 그런 걸 숨겨 뒀구나. 귀찮네…. 어쩔 수 없지, <value:$28>은 아이린을 지원하러 가. 나머지는 나랑 시가지를 파괴하는 거야.<end>"

## 560478
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あなたは…<line-break><value:$28>…。<line-break> <line-break>…カルラの部下になったと聞いたわ。<line-break>わたくしを捕らえに来たのね。<end>"
EN: "Y-you… <value:$28>. I heard you'd entered Karula's service. You've come to arrest me, haven't you?<end>"
KO: "다, 당신은… <value:$28>…. …카를라의 부하가 됐다고 들었어요. 저를 잡으러 온 거군요.<end>"

## 560479
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "反乱者は逃がせない<end>"
EN: "I can't let a rebel escape.<end>"
KO: "반란자는 놓칠 수 없다<end>"

## 560480
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一緒に逃げよう<end>"
EN: "Let's escape together.<end>"
KO: "같이 도망치자<end>"

## 560481
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "反乱者…。<line-break>…わたくしは反乱者の<line-break>親玉として殺されるのね。<end>"
EN: "A rebel… So I'll be put to death as the leader of the rebellion.<end>"
KO: "반란자…. …저는 반란자의 우두머리로 죽게 되는 거군요.<end>"

## 560482
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうね、わたくしは殺されても<line-break>文句言えないくらい<line-break>人を傷つけてきたと思うから。<line-break>おじさまも、あなたも、<line-break>わたくしから離れていった。<line-break>もう、わたくしは…疲れた…。<end>"
EN: "Yes… I suppose I've hurt enough people that I have no right to complain if I'm killed. Uncle left me, and so did you. I'm… tired now.<end>"
KO: "…그래, 난 죽어도 할 말 없을 만큼 사람들을 상처 입혀 왔다고 생각해. 아저씨도, 너도, 내 곁을 떠났어. 이제 난… 지쳤어….<end>"

## 560483
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "将軍ー！<line-break><value:$28>将軍ー！<end>"
EN: "General! General <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "장군님~! <value:$28> 장군님~!<end>"

## 560484
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、こいつはクリュセイス・クロイス。<line-break>反乱者の首魁！！<line-break>抵抗しても無駄だ。おとなしくしろ！<end>"
EN: "Th-this is Khryuseis Krois, the leader of the rebellion! Resistance is useless. Come quietly!<end>"
KO: "이, 이자는 크류세이스 크로이스. 반란군의 수괴다!! 저항해도 소용없다. 얌전히 있어!<end>"

## 560485
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…さわらないで。<line-break>抵抗はしないわ。<line-break> <end>"
EN: "Don't touch me. I won't resist.<end>"
KO: "…만지지 마. 저항은 안 할게.<end>"

## 560486
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>将軍。<line-break>お手柄です！<line-break>さっそく、連行いたします！！<end>"
EN: "General <value:$28>, you've done splendidly! We'll take her away at once!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 장군. 큰 공을 세우셨습니다! 바로 연행하겠습니다!!<end>"

## 560487
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ごめん…なさい…。<end>"
EN: "I'm… sorry…<end>"
KO: "…미안…해요….<end>"

## 560488
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "反乱者？<line-break>…あなたもしばらく前まで<line-break>その片割れだったのではなくて？<end>"
EN: "A rebel? Weren't you one of them yourself until quite recently?<end>"
KO: "반란자? …당신도 얼마 전까지만 해도 그쪽 편 아니었나요?<end>"

## 560489
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、もう、いいわ。<line-break>…あなたはともかく、おじさまに    <line-break>裏切られるなんて思わなかった…。<line-break>あなたがカルラ軍にいるということは<line-break>解放軍の拠点は明かされて<line-break>いるのね。もう…逃げられない。<end>"
EN: "But it doesn't matter anymore. I might have expected it from you, but I never thought Uncle would betray me… If you're with Karula's Army, then the Liberation Army's base must have been exposed. There's… nowhere left to run.<end>"
KO: "하지만 이제 됐어. …당신은 그렇다 쳐도 아저씨에게 배신당할 줄은 몰랐어…. 당신이 카를라군에 있다는 건 해방군의 거점도 들켰다는 뜻이겠지. 이제… 도망칠 수도 없어.<end>"

## 560490
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…フフ。<line-break>…悔し涙も…もう出ないわ。<line-break> <end>"
EN: "Heh… I don't even have any bitter tears left to shed.<end>"
KO: "…후후. …분해서 흘릴 눈물도… 이제 안 나와.<end>"

KO: "…<value:$28>. 아니, …아무것도 아니야.<end>"

## 560496
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたくしと一緒に…<line-break>逃げるというの？<end>"
EN: "You mean… you want me to run away with you?<end>"
KO: "저와 함께… 도망치겠다는 건가요?<end>"

## 560497
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんなこと、できるわけないわ！<line-break>今のあなたは<line-break>カルラの部下でしょう！？<end>"
EN: "Th-there's no way we can do that! You serve Karula now, don't you?!<end>"
KO: "그, 그런 걸 할 수 있을 리 없잖아! 지금 당신은 카를라의 부하잖아!?<end>"

## 560498
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こっちだ。<line-break>クリュセイスはこっちに逃げたぞ！<end>"
EN: "This way! Khryuseis fled this way!<end>"
KO: "이쪽이다. 크류세이스는 이쪽으로 도망쳤다!<end>"

## 560499
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、何を…<end>"
EN: "W-what are you…<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭘…<end>"

## 560500
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、クリュセイス！<line-break>将軍、まさか！　裏切られるか！！<line-break> <end>"
EN: "Ah, Khryuseis! General―don't tell me you're betraying us!<end>"
KO: "앗, 크류세이스! 장군, 설마! 배신당한 건가!!<end>"

## 560501
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ここまで来れば、安全のようね。<line-break>話してもらえるかしら、<line-break>あなたの本心を。<end>"
EN: "It looks as though we're safe now. Will you tell me what you truly feel?<end>"
KO: "…여기까지 오면 안전한 것 같네. 말해 줄래? 네 진심을.<end>"

## 560502
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…償いたかった？<line-break>わたくしに償いたかったと言うの？<end>"
EN: "You wanted to make amends? You're saying you wanted to make amends to me?<end>"
KO: "…속죄하고 싶었다고? 나한테 속죄하고 싶었다는 거야?<end>"

## 560503
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ごめんなさい<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "…미안해요<end>"

## 560504
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…謝っても許してもらえるとは<line-break>思っていません。でも、一度<line-break>はっきりと言っておきたかった。<line-break>あなたが失敗したギルドの仕事。<line-break>あれは、わたくしとおじさまが<line-break>仕組んでいたのです。<end>"
EN: "I don't expect you to forgive me, even after I've apologized. But I wanted to say this plainly, just once. That Guild job you failed? Uncle and I set it up.<end>"
KO: "…사과한다고 용서받을 거라고 생각하진 않아요. 하지만 한 번은 확실히 말해 두고 싶었어요. 당신이 실패했던 길드 의뢰. 그건 저와 아저씨가 꾸민 일이었습니다.<end>"

## 560505
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうして、あなたの身柄を拘束し、<line-break>闘技場や、解放軍の危険な任務で<line-break>あなたを抹殺するつもりだったのです。<end>"
EN: "We meant to take you into custody, then dispose of you in the arena or on one of the Liberation Army's dangerous missions.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 당신의 신병을 확보한 뒤 투기장이나 해방군의 위험한 임무를 이용해 당신을 제거할 생각이었습니다.<end>"

## 560506
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたくしにはあなたに命をかけてまで<line-break>守ってもらえる資格はありません。<line-break>償うなんて言ってもらう資格も…。<end>"
EN: "I don't deserve to have you risk your life protecting me. I don't even deserve to hear you say you wanted to make amends...<end>"
KO: "저는 당신이 목숨까지 걸고 지켜 줄 자격이 없습니다. 보상하겠다는 말을 들을 자격도….<end>"

## 560507
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずっと、謝ろうと思っていました。<line-break>でも、どうしても言い出せなかった。<line-break>一度言い出してしまうと…<end>"
EN: "I've wanted to apologize for so long. But I could never bring myself to say it. Once I started...<end>"
KO: "계속 사과해야 한다고 생각했어요. 하지만 도저히 말을 꺼낼 수 없었어요. 한번 입 밖에 내버리면…<end>"

## 560508
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一度言い出してしまうと、<line-break>もう、おさえが<line-break>きかなくなりそうだったから…<end>"
EN: "Once I started, I was afraid I wouldn't be able to stop myself...<end>"
KO: "한번 말을 꺼내 버리면 이제 멈출 수 없을 것 같아서…<end>"

## 560509
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…泣いても、いいですか？<line-break>今まで…、つらいことが…<end>"
EN: "May I cry? All this time... it's been so hard...<end>"
KO: "…울어도 될까요? 지금까지… 힘든 일이….<end>"

## 560510
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ありがとう。<line-break>どうか、このまま…一緒に…。<line-break>もう少しだけ、このまま…。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. Please... stay with me like this. Just a little longer...<end>"
KO: "…고마워. 부디 이대로… 함께…. 조금만 더, 이대로….<end>"

## 560511
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、いいわ！<line-break>もう、たくさん！<end>"
EN: "Enough! I've had enough!<end>"
KO: "이제 됐어! 이제 지긋지긋해!<end>"

## 560512
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "みんなみんな、わたくしをだまして！<line-break>おじさまさえ、わたくしを捨てたのよ！<line-break>今さら、あなたを信じられると思って？<line-break>助けてくれたことには感謝します。<line-break>でも、もうわたくしに<line-break>つきまとわないで！<end>"
EN: "Everyone lied to me! Even Uncle abandoned me! Do you really think I can trust you now? I'm grateful that you saved me. But stop following me around!<end>"
KO: "모두, 모두 나를 속였어! 아저씨마저 나를 버렸다고! 이제 와서 내가 당신을 믿을 것 같아? 도와준 건 감사해요. 하지만 이제 더는 나를 따라다니지 마!<end>"

## 560513
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ<line-break>こいつ…強すぎる。<line-break>私ひとりでは…<end>"
EN: "Damn, this thing's too strong. I can't beat it alone...<end>"
KO: "큭, 이 녀석… 너무 강해. 나 혼자서는….<end>"

## 560514
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっくっく、ディンガルの犬め！<line-break>アンティノ商会製の<line-break>デスギガースはどうだ？<line-break>私を総督にする約束を破ったことを<line-break>今ごろ後悔しても遅いぞ！<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh! How do you like the Death Gigas, you Dyneskal dog? Built by the Antino Trading Company itself! It's too late to regret breaking your promise to make me governor!<end>"
KO: "크크크, 딘갈의 개들아! 안티노 상회제 데스기가스 맛이 어떠냐? 나를 총독으로 삼겠다는 약속을 어긴 걸 이제 와 후회해도 늦었다!<end>"

## 560515
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "黙れ！<line-break>簡単に裏切るような人間を<line-break>カルラ様が信用するはずがあるまい。<end>"
EN: "Shut up! Lady Karula would never trust anyone so quick to betray others.<end>"
KO: "닥쳐! 쉽게 배신하는 인간을 카를라 님이 믿을 리 없잖아.<end>"

## 560516
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うるさい小娘だ！<line-break>お前のあとでカルラも<line-break>このデスギガースで叩き潰してくれる。<end>"
EN: "What an irritating little brat! Once I'm through with you, I'll use this Death Gigas to crush Karula too!<end>"
KO: "시끄러운 계집애군! 너 다음엔 칼라도 이 데스기가스로 짓뭉개 주마.<end>"

## 560517
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あなた…！<end>"
EN: "Y-you...!<end>"
KO: "다, 당신…!<end>"

## 560518
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、おのれ、覚えておれ！<line-break>いつか、必ず復讐してやる！<end>"
EN: "D-damn you! Remember this! One day, I swear I'll have my revenge!<end>"
KO: "이, 이놈! 기억해 둬라! 언젠가 반드시 복수해 주마!<end>"

## 560519
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何をしているの！<line-break>さっさとアンティノを追いなさい！<end>"
EN: "What are you doing? Hurry up and go after Antino!<end>"
KO: "뭘 하고 있어! 빨리 안티노를 쫓아가!<end>"

## 560520
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、あなたに助けられるなんて…。<line-break>私はアンティノを追うわ。<line-break>あなたは…あなたの任務に戻って。<end>"
EN: "Heh. To think you'd be the one to save me... I'm going after Antino. You... get back to your mission.<end>"
KO: "후후, 당신에게 도움을 받다니…. 나는 안티노를 쫓을게. 당신은… 당신의 임무로 돌아가.<end>"

## 560521
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私もあなたも、ただの冒険者なら、<line-break>根拠も現実味もない理想だけを<line-break>語っていればいいわ。<line-break>だけどあなたも私も騎士！<line-break>世界の行く末に責任がある立場なの！<line-break>わかるでしょ！？<end>"
EN: "If you and I were nothing but adventurers, we could spend our time talking about groundless, unrealistic ideals. But we're both knights! The fate of the world is our responsibility! You understand that, don't you?!<end>"
KO: "나도 당신도 그냥 모험자라면 근거도 현실성도 없는 이상만 말하면 돼. 하지만 당신도 나도 기사야! 세계의 앞날에 책임이 있는 입장이라고! 알겠어!?<end>"

## 560522
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたには納得いかないだろうけど、<line-break>この街を徹底的に破壊することで、<line-break>最終的には全体の犠牲者が減るのよ。<end>"
EN: "I know you can't accept it, but destroying this city completely will ultimately mean fewer casualties overall.<end>"
KO: "당신은 납득하지 못하겠지만 이 도시를 철저하게 파괴하면 결과적으로 전체 희생자는 줄어들어.<end>"

## 560523
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私は行くわ。<line-break>あなたも、早く行きなさい！<end>"
EN: "I'm going. You need to hurry too!<end>"
KO: "…나는 갈게. 너도 빨리 가!<end>"

## 560524
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…確かに、あなたの言うとおり<line-break>戦争を早く終わらせることができるなら<line-break>何やってもいいなんて思ってない。<line-break>騎士にあこがれて、ロストールを<line-break>飛び出したころには、街を破壊する<line-break>指揮を執るなんて思いもしなかった…<end>"
EN: "You're right. I don't believe ending the war sooner means we can do whatever we please. When I left Rostorl dreaming of becoming a knight, I never imagined I'd end up commanding the destruction of a city...<end>"
KO: "…확실히 네 말대로 전쟁을 빨리 끝낼 수만 있다면 무슨 짓을 해도 된다고 생각하진 않아. 기사에 동경해 로스톨을 뛰쳐나왔을 때는 도시를 파괴하라는 지휘를 하게 될 줄은 꿈에도 몰랐어….<end>"

## 560525
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私としたことが…不覚だったわ。<line-break>あんなのを飼っているなんて。<line-break>あなたのおかげで助かったわ<line-break>私はアンティノを追うわ。<line-break>あなたは…あなたの任務に戻って。<end>"
EN: "How careless of me... To think something like that was being kept around. You saved me. I'm going after Antino. You... get back to your mission.<end>"
KO: "나답지 않게… 방심했어. 그런 걸 기르고 있었다니. 네 덕분에 살았어. 난 안티노를 쫓을게. 넌… 네 임무로 돌아가.<end>"

## 560526
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私もあなたも、ただの冒険者なら、<line-break>根拠も現実味もない理想だけを<line-break>語っていればいいわ。<line-break>だけどあなたも私も騎士！<line-break>世界の行く末に責任がある立場なの！<line-break>わかるでしょ！？<end>"
EN: "If you and I were nothing but adventurers, we could spend our time talking about groundless, unrealistic ideals. But we're both knights! The fate of the world is our responsibility! You understand that, don't you?!<end>"
KO: "나도 당신도 그냥 모험자라면 근거도 현실성도 없는 이상만 말하면 돼. 하지만 당신도 나도 기사야! 세계의 앞날에 책임이 있는 입장이라고! 알겠어!?<end>"

## 560527
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560528

JP: "…私は行くわ。<line-break>あなたも、早く行きなさい！<end>"
EN: "I'm going. You need to hurry too!<end>"
KO: "…나는 갈게. 너도 빨리 가!<end>"

## 560529
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…確かに、あなたの言うとおり<line-break>戦争を早く終わらせることができるなら<line-break>何やってもいいなんて思ってない。<line-break>騎士にあこがれて、ロストールを<line-break>飛び出したころには、街を破壊する<line-break>指揮を執るなんて思いもしなかった…<end>"
EN: "You're right. I don't believe ending the war sooner means we can do whatever we please. When I left Rostorl dreaming of becoming a knight, I never imagined I'd end up commanding the destruction of a city...<end>"
KO: "…확실히 네 말대로 전쟁을 빨리 끝낼 수만 있다면 무슨 짓을 해도 된다고 생각하진 않아. 기사에 동경해 로스톨을 뛰쳐나왔을 때는 도시를 파괴하라는 지휘를 하게 될 줄은 꿈에도 몰랐어….<end>"

## 560530
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私としたことが…不覚だったわ。<line-break>しかも、あなたに助けられるなんて<line-break>一応、礼は言っておくわ。<line-break>私はこれからアンティノを追う。<line-break>あなたは…あなたの任務に戻って。<end>"
EN: "How careless of me... And to think you were the one who saved me. I suppose I should at least thank you. I'm going after Antino now. You... get back to your mission.<end>"
KO: "나답지 않게… 방심했어. 게다가 네 도움을 받다니. 일단 고맙다는 말은 해 둘게. 난 이제 안티노를 쫓아갈 거야. 넌… 네 임무로 돌아가.<end>"

## 560531
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わかるかしら。あなたも私も騎士。<line-break>世界の行く末に責任がある立場なのよ。<line-break>理想を語るときじゃ…ないわ。<line-break>この街を徹底的に破壊することで、<line-break>最終的には全体の犠牲者が減る。<line-break>カルラ様の言うことは…正しいわ。<end>"
EN: "Do you understand? You and I are both knights. The fate of the world is our responsibility. This is no time... to talk about ideals. Destroying this city completely will mean fewer casualties in the end. Lady Karula is... right.<end>"
KO: "…알겠어? 너도 나도 기사야. 세계의 앞날에 책임이 있는 입장이지. 이상을 말할 때가… 아니야. 이 도시를 철저히 파괴함으로써 결과적으로 전체 희생자는 줄어들어. 카를라 님의 말은… 옳아.<end>"

## 560532
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あなた、今度は<line-break>カルラ様を裏切るつもり？<end>"
EN: "Are you planning to betray Lady Karula this time?<end>"
KO: "…당신, 이번엔 카를라 님을 배신할 생각이야?<end>"

## 560533
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>はロセンでは<line-break>我が身の危険をかえりみずに<line-break>鐘で仲間の危機を知らせてくれました。<line-break>そして、今回は命がけでわたくしを<line-break>助け出してくれました。解放軍は<line-break>あなたの勇気ある行動に感謝します。<end>"
EN: "In Rocen, <value:$28>, you put your own safety aside and rang the bell to sound the alarm when your companions were in danger. This time, you risked your life to rescue me. The Liberation Army thanks you for your courageous actions.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>는 로센에서 자기 위험도 돌보지 않고 종을 울려 동료들의 위기를 알렸습니다. 그리고 이번에는 목숨을 걸고 저를 구해 주었어요. 해방군은 당신의 용기 있는 행동에 감사드립니다.<end>"

## 560534
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>これからも、<line-break>わたくしを助けてくださいますか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, will you continue to help me?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 앞으로도 저를 도와주시겠습니까?<end>"

## 560535
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍はあなたを歓迎します。<line-break>リベルダムの陥落により、解放軍も<line-break>大変ですが、がんばりましょう。<end>"
EN: "The Liberation Army welcomes you. The fall of Liberdam has made things difficult for us as well, but let us do our best.<end>"
KO: "해방군은 당신을 환영합니다. 리벨덤의 함락으로 해방군도 어려운 상황이지만 함께 힘냅시다.<end>"

## 560536
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、頼りにしています。<line-break>わたくしには…、<line-break> <line-break>いえ、わたくしたち解放軍には<line-break>あなたのような人が必要なのです。<end>"
EN: "I'm counting on you, <value:$28>. I need... No, the Liberation Army needs someone like you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 믿고 있습니다. 저에게는… 아니, 우리 해방군에는 당신 같은 사람이 필요합니다.<end>"

## 560537
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "同志<value:$28>。<line-break>君がいれば、我々はまだ戦える。<end>"
EN: "Comrade <value:$28>. With you, we can still fight.<end>"
KO: "동지 <value:$28>. 자네가 있다면 우리는 아직 싸울 수 있다.<end>"

## 560538
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、あんたは英雄だ。<line-break>あんたさえいれば、<line-break>いつかは俺らが勝つ日が来るさ。<end>"
EN: "You're a hero, <value:$28>. As long as you're with us, our day will come.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 넌 영웅이야. 네가 있기만 하면 언젠가는 우리가 이길 날이 올 거야.<end>"

## 560539
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん、<line-break>状況は厳しいです。<line-break>気を引き締めてかかりましょう。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, the situation is grim. We must stay focused.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨, 상황이 어렵습니다. 마음을 다잡고 갑시다.<end>"

## 560540
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これでリベルダムまで制圧っと。<line-break>アンティノには逃げられたけど、<line-break>解放軍は一掃できたしぃ。<end>"
EN: "And that's us in control all the way to Liberdam. Antino got away, but we wiped out the Liberation Army.<end>"
KO: "이걸로 리벨덤까지 제압 완료. 안티노는 놓쳤지만 해방군은 쓸어버렸고.<end>"

## 560541
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほい、これ報酬ね。<end>"
EN: "Here. This is your reward.<end>"
KO: "자, 이게 보상이야.<end>"

## 560542
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "街も見事にガレキの山だしね。<line-break>うん。ちゃーんと予定通り<line-break>進んでるじゃん。<end>"
EN: "And the city's been reduced to a splendid heap of rubble. Yep. Everything's going exactly to plan.<end>"
KO: "거리도 멋지게 잔해 더미가 됐네. 응. 제대로 예정대로 진행되고 있잖아.<end>"

## 560544
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気を悪くしないでね、アイリーン。<line-break>今度のことは戦争を早く終えるため<line-break>仕方のなかったことなの。<line-break>これで、ロストールの貴族たちも<line-break>士気が落ちてるはず。次の戦いでは<line-break>あまり人が死なずにすむわ。<end>"
EN: "Don't be upset, Irene. This was unavoidable if we wanted to end the war sooner. The nobles of Rostorl should have lost heart by now. That means fewer people will have to die in the next battle.<end>"
KO: "기분 나빠하지 마, 아이린. 이번 일은 전쟁을 빨리 끝내기 위해 어쩔 수 없었어. 이걸로 로스톨 귀족들의 사기도 떨어졌을 거야. 다음 전투에서는 많은 사람이 죽지 않아도 될 거야.<end>"

## 560545
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わかっています。<line-break>カルラ様の判断は、<line-break>…正しかったと思います。<end>"
EN: "I understand. I believe Lady Karula made... the right decision.<end>"
KO: "…알고 있습니다. 칼라 님의 판단은… 옳았다고 생각합니다.<end>"

## 560546
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがと。<end>"
EN: "Thanks.<end>"
KO: "고마워.<end>"

## 560547
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>も<line-break>それでいいかな？<end>"
EN: "Is that all right with you too, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>도 그걸로 괜찮겠어?<end>"

## 560548
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くす。だから、アイリーンも<line-break><value:$28>も好きよ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. That's why I like both Irene and <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "후후. 그래서 아이린도 <value:$28>도 좋아해.<end>"

## 560549
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んじゃ、今日は解散。<end>"
EN: "Well, that's all for today.<end>"
KO: "그럼 오늘은 해산.<end>"

## 560551
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>、<line-break>今回はあなたに助けられたわね。<end>"
EN: "...<value:$28>, you really came through for me this time.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>, 이번에는 네 도움을 받았네.<end>"

## 560552
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつか、こんな日が来るのかなとは<line-break>思ってたけど。<line-break>ちょっと…悔しいわね…。<end>"
EN: "I always wondered if a day like this might come. Still... it's a little frustrating.<end>"
KO: "언젠가 이런 날이 올 거라고 생각은 했지만. 조금… 분하네….<end>"

## 560553
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…今回はあなたに助けられたわね。<line-break>あなたの実力は知ってるけど、<line-break>ちょっと…悔しいわね。<end>"
EN: "You really came through for me this time. I know how capable you are, but... it's still a little frustrating.<end>"
KO: "…이번에는 네게 도움을 받았네. 네 실력은 알고 있지만 조금… 분하네.<end>"

## 560554
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…今回はあなたに助けられたわね。<line-break>あなたの実力は認める。<line-break>あなたを疑っていたことも謝るわ。<end>"
EN: "...You really came through for me this time. I admit you're capable, and I'm sorry I doubted you.<end>"
KO: "…이번에는 네게 도움을 받았네. 네 실력은 인정해. 널 의심했던 것도 사과할게.<end>"

## 560555
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい。<line-break>それと、ありがとう。<line-break>言いたいことはそれだけ。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. And thank you. That's all I wanted to say.<end>"
KO: "미안해. 그리고 고마워. 하고 싶은 말은 그것뿐이야.<end>"

## 560556
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ様が以前、私におっしゃったの。<line-break>…この世界で確かなものは、<line-break>痛みだけだって。<end>"
EN: "Lady Karula once told me that pain is the only certainty in this world.<end>"
KO: "카를라 님이 전에 내게 그러셨어. …이 세계에서 확실한 건 고통뿐이라고.<end>"

## 560557
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは、間違ってはいないと思う。<line-break>でも、他にも何かがあると信じたいの。<line-break>痛み以外の何かもあるって。<end>"
EN: "I don't think she was wrong. But I want to believe there's something else, too. Something besides pain.<end>"
KO: "그게 틀렸다고는 생각하지 않아. 그래도 다른 무언가가 있다고 믿고 싶어. 고통 말고도 뭔가가 있다고.<end>"

## 560558
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…とにかく、ありがとう。<line-break><value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "Anyway, thank you, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "…아무튼 고마워, <value:$28>….<end>"

## 560559
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、お前は…<line-break>み、味方なのか…<line-break>も、もう、目が見えないんだ。<end>"
EN: "Y-you... are you on our side? I-I can't see anymore.<end>"
KO: "너, 너는… 아, 아군인가…? 이, 이제 눈이 보이지 않아.<end>"

## 560561
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "た、頼む、我らコーンスの未来を<line-break>しょ、将軍をお守りしてくれ…<line-break>ごふっ…<end>"
EN: "P-please... the future of the Konsu... p-protect the general... Guh...<end>"
KO: "부, 부탁한다, 우리 콘스의 미래를… 자, 장군을 지켜 줘… 콜록…<end>"

## 560562
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は貴様のように<line-break>我欲のために戦っているのではない！<line-break>種族の未来のために戦っている！<line-break>このままではコーンスは<line-break>数で勝る人間に押され、<line-break>種族的に滅びてしまう。<end>"
EN: "I am not fighting to satisfy my own desires, as you are! I fight for the future of my people! If this continues, the Konsu will be overwhelmed by the more numerous humans and driven to extinction.<end>"
KO: "나는 네놈처럼 사욕을 위해 싸우는 게 아니다! 종족의 미래를 위해 싸우는 거다! 이대로라면 콘스는 수가 많은 인간에게 밀려 종족 자체가 멸망하고 말아.<end>"

## 560563
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア様亡き今、聖光石で力を付けて<line-break>世界を支配するより、<line-break>我らコーンスに生き延びる道無し！<end>"
EN: "Now that Lord Nemea is dead, the Konsu have no hope of survival but to gain power through the Holy Light Stones and rule the world!<end>"
KO: "네메아 님이 돌아가신 지금, 성광석으로 힘을 키워 세계를 지배하는 것 외에는 우리 콘스가 살아남을 길이 없다!<end>"

## 560564
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…で、ジラークちゃん、<line-break>コーンスが支配する世界を作って<line-break>どうするつもりなの？<line-break>コーンスってだけで<line-break>自分は特別だって面して歩く、<line-break>特権バカコーンスを量産するわけ？<end>"
EN: "So, Zsilak dear, what do you plan to do once you've made a world ruled by the Konsu? Churn out a whole crop of privilege-addled fools who strut around thinking they're special just because they're Konsu?<end>"
KO: "…그래서 지라크, 콘스가 지배하는 세상을 만들어서 뭘 어쩔 셈이야? 콘스라는 이유만으로 자기가 특별한 줄 알고 으스대는 특권 바보 콘스를 양산하려고?<end>"

## 560565
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ち、違う！<end>"
EN: "Th-That's not it!<end>"
KO: "아, 아니야!<end>"

## 560566
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "違わないわよ。<line-break>特権を持つ奴らって<line-break>いっつもそうなるの。<line-break>ネメア様は種族による特権は認めず、<line-break>種族平等の世界を望まれた。<line-break>これって、それに対する侮辱じゃない？<end>"
EN: "Yes, it is. That's what always happens to people with privilege. Lord Nemea rejected privilege based on race. He wanted a world where all races were equal. Isn't this an insult to everything he stood for?<end>"
KO: "틀리지 않았어. 특권을 가진 녀석들은 늘 그렇게 돼. 네메아 님은 종족에 따른 특권을 인정하지 않고 종족 평등의 세계를 바라셨어. 이건 그 뜻에 대한 모욕 아니야?<end>"

## 560567
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ち、違う！<line-break>種族の誇りを持った存続のためには…！<end>"
EN: "N-No! If we are to survive with our racial pride intact...!<end>"
KO: "아, 아니야! 종족의 긍지를 지키며 살아남으려면…!<end>"

## 560568
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたはそうやって<line-break>種族間の憎悪を育ててるだけ。<line-break>人間の数ってのを無視しちゃダメよん。<line-break>力で押さえても、数がはねのけるわ。<line-break>それからコーンスはダルケニス同様に<line-break>人間によって駆逐されるわ。<end>"
EN: "All you're doing is feeding hatred between the races. You can't ignore the sheer number of humans. You may hold them down by force, but their numbers will eventually break your grip. Then the humans will wipe out the Konsu, just as they did the Darkenith.<end>"
KO: "당신은 그렇게 종족 간의 증오만 키우고 있어. 인간의 수를 무시하면 안 돼. 힘으로 억눌러도 숫자가 밀어낼 거야. 그러고 나면 콘스는 다르케니스처럼 인간에게 쫓겨나겠지.<end>"

## 560569
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は…、同胞種族のために…！<line-break>同胞種族は特権に迷いはしない…！<end>"
EN: "I fight... for my own people...! They would never be led astray by privilege...!<end>"
KO: "나는… 동족을 위해…! 동족은 특권에 현혹되지 않아…!<end>"

## 560570
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなあいまいなもののために戦うの？<line-break>だから、結局は、<line-break>滅びの道をたどっちゃうのよね～。<line-break>まだ、我欲のために戦う<line-break>あたしのほうがマシじゃん？<line-break>少なくとも、あたしは満足してる。<end>"
EN: "You're fighting for something that vague? That's why you end up marching straight toward extinction. At least fighting for my own desires is better than that, isn't it? At least I'm satisfied.<end>"
KO: "그런 애매한 걸 위해 싸우는 거야? 그러니까 결국 멸망의 길을 걷게 되는 거겠지~. 차라리 사욕을 위해 싸우는 내가 더 낫잖아? 적어도 난 만족하고 있어.<end>"

## 560571
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、我欲のために戦って<line-break>満足しておるだと？<end>"
EN: "Hmph. You fight for your own desires and call yourself satisfied?<end>"
KO: "흥, 욕심을 위해 싸우면서 만족하고 있다고?<end>"

## 560572
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう！　そうは見えぬがな！！<line-break>貴様は苦しんでいるのではないか？<line-break> <line-break>貴様は表面は笑っているが、<line-break>その笑いは偽りのものではないか！<end>"
EN: "Oh? You hardly look satisfied to me! Are you not suffering? You smile, but isn't that smile a lie?<end>"
KO: "호오! 그렇게는 안 보이는군!! 네놈은 괴로운 게 아니냐? 겉으로는 웃고 있지만 그 웃음은 거짓이 아닌가!<end>"

## 560573
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、何よ！<end>"
EN: "Wh-What are you talking about?!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐야!<end>"

## 560574
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "図星だったようだな！<line-break>私は間違っているのかもしれぬ！<line-break>だが、貴様も私を笑えぬのだ！<end>"
EN: "It seems I struck a nerve! I may be wrong, but you are in no position to laugh at me!<end>"
KO: "정곡이었나 보군! 내가 틀렸을지도 모른다! 하지만 네놈도 나를 비웃을 수는 없다!<end>"

## 560575
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、貴様の手下が来たぞ。<line-break>さあ！　過ちがないか、<line-break>私に挑め！<line-break>そして、私を倒して<line-break>乗り越えてゆけ！<end>"
EN: "Heh. Your underling has arrived. Come! Test me and see whether I am wrong! Defeat me, and surpass me!<end>"
KO: "후후, 네 부하가 왔군. 자! 잘못이 없는지 나에게 도전해라! 그리고 나를 쓰러뜨려 넘어서는 거다!<end>"

## 560576
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>。<line-break>どうして…<line-break> <line-break>ええーい！<line-break>とにかく、いくわよ！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Why...? Oh, to hell with it! Let's just do this!<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>. 왜… 에잇! 어쨌든 간다!<end>"

## 560577
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "み、見事だ…。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>私が間違っていたのだな…。<line-break>同胞種族のため…、焦って<line-break>ものが見えなくなっていたのだ…。<end>"
EN: "W-Well done... So I was wrong... In my desperation to save my people, I let panic blind me...<end>"
KO: "대, 대단하군…. 내가 틀렸던 거구나…. 동족을 위해 조급한 나머지 아무것도 보이지 않았어….<end>"

## 560578
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ…、頼む、<line-break>非は私１人にある…、<line-break>同胞種族を…罰しないでくれ…。<end>"
EN: "Karula... please. The blame is mine alone... Do not punish my people...<end>"
KO: "칼라…, 부탁한다. 잘못은 나 하나에게 있다…. 동족을… 벌하지 말아 줘….<end>"

## 560580
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…フフ、甘いんじゃない？<line-break>あたしってば、小心者だからさ、<line-break>逆らう連中は…。<end>"

## 560582
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた、不思議に思わなかったの？<line-break>なぜ、アルノートゥンが聖光石を<line-break>採掘してこなかったのか？<end>"
EN: "Didn't you ever wonder why Alnortun never mined the Holy Light Stones?<end>"
KO: "너 이상하다고 생각하지 않았어? 왜 알노툰이 성광석을 채굴하지 않았는지?<end>"

## 560583
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖光石さえ採掘していれば、<line-break>世界を支配することも<line-break>できたかもしれないのに？<end>"

## 560584
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なにか、採掘できない理由でもないと<line-break>説明がつかないでしょ？<line-break> <line-break>これがその理由みたいね。<line-break>あんなのが埋まってりゃ、<line-break>そりゃ掘れないわよ。<end>"
EN: "There had to be some reason mining was impossible, or none of it makes sense. Looks like this is the reason. With that thing buried down here, of course no one could dig.<end>"
KO: "채굴할 수 없는 이유라도 없으면 설명이 안 되잖아? 이게 그 이유인 모양이네. 저런 게 묻혀 있으면 당연히 못 파지.<end>"

## 560585
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560586
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560587
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 560588
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "コーンスの未来を託せるものは<line-break>今、この場に、<line-break>お前たちしかおらぬ。<line-break>私の反乱でコーンスに向けられている<line-break>人間たちの憎悪を収拾できるのは、<line-break>お前しかおらぬのだ！<end>"
EN: "You are the only ones here to whom the future of the Konsu can now be entrusted. You alone can quell the humans' hatred of the Konsu that my rebellion has stirred!<end>"
KO: "콘스의 미래를 맡길 수 있는 자는 지금 이 자리에서 너희뿐이다. 내 반란 때문에 콘스에게 향한 인간들의 증오를 수습할 수 있는 것도 너뿐이다!<end>"

## 560589
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "지라크, 무슨 짓을…!<end>"

## 560590
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前しか同胞コーンスを<line-break>守れないのだ！<line-break>お前の命は私が守ってみせる！<line-break>…頼む、同胞を<line-break>コーンスを守ってくれ…！<end>"
EN: "Only you can protect our fellow Konsu! I swear I will protect your life! Please... protect our people. Protect the Konsu...!<end>"
KO: "네가 아니면 동포 콘스를 지킬 수 없다! 네 목숨은 내가 지켜 보이겠다! …부탁한다, 동포를, 콘스를 지켜 줘…!<end>"

## 560591
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　…ちっとも面白くなかった<line-break><line-break><line-break><line-break>　　　絶っ対、笑ってようって誓ったのに<line-break>　　　　　そのために力を求めたのに<line-break>そのために幾万の人を戦火に踏みにじったのに…<line-break><line-break>　　　あたしは、ぜんぜん面白くなかった<line-break>　　　　　　　　笑えなかった<line-break>　　　　　　いつでも、心の底には<line-break>　　　　泣いてる、村娘の自分がいた…<end>"
EN: "…None of it was any fun. I swore I'd keep smiling, no matter what. That was why I sought power. Why I trampled tens of thousands of people underfoot in the fires of war… But none of it was any fun. I couldn't smile. Deep down, there was always a village girl inside me, crying…<end>"
KO: "…전혀 재미있지 않았어. 반드시 계속 웃겠다고 맹세했는데, 그러려고 힘을 원했는데, 그러려고 수만 명을 전쟁의 불길에 짓밟았는데…. 나는 하나도 재미있지 않았어. 웃을 수 없었어. 언제나 마음 깊은 곳에는 울고 있는 시골 소녀인 내가 있었어….<end>"

## 560605

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ様の命とあれば<line-break>従わないわけにはいかないわ。<line-break>あなたの仲間になってあげる。<line-break>だけど、あくまで、<line-break>私はカルラ様の配下だってことを<line-break>忘れないで。<end>"
EN: "If that is Lady Karula's command, I have no choice but to obey. I'll join you. But don't forget that first and foremost, I serve Lady Karula.<end>"
KO: "칼라 님의 명령이라면 따르지 않을 수 없지. 네 동료가 되어 줄게. 하지만 어디까지나 나는 칼라 님의 부하라는 걸 잊지 마.<end>"

## 560629
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ。<line-break>冒険に連れていってくれるの、<line-break>待ってるわ。<end>"
EN: "Well, then. I'll be waiting for you to take me adventuring.<end>"
KO: "그럼. 모험에 데려가 줄 때까지 기다리고 있을게.<end>"

## 560630
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねぼすけ竜ちゃん？<line-break>ご機嫌いかが？<end>"
EN: "How's our sleepyhead dragon feeling?<end>"
KO: "잠꾸러기 용아? 기분은 어때?<end>"

## 560631
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…おのれ！<line-break>我こそは邪竜イシュバアル…！<line-break>うぬらには惨たらしい最期をくれやる！<end>"
EN: "Curse you! I am Evil Dragon Ishbaal! I shall give you all a gruesome end!<end>"
KO: "…이놈! 내가 바로 사룡 이슈바알…! 너희에게 처참한 최후를 안겨 주마!<end>"

## 560632
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "強がりいってもダメだよん。<line-break>あれだけの魔力くらって無事なはず<line-break>ないでしょ。<line-break>いくよ！<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Acting tough won't help. There's no way you came through that much magic unscathed. Let's go, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "강한 척해도 소용없어. 그만한 마력을 맞고 멀쩡할 리 없잖아. 간다! <value:$28>!<end>"

## 560633
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仲間を呼ぶ<end>"
EN: "Call for allies<end>"
KO: "동료를 부른다<end>"

## 560634
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日はやめておく<end>"
EN: "Not today<end>"
KO: "오늘은 그만둔다<end>"

## 560635
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "た、大変だ…<line-break>お、王宮に、化け物…が<end>"
EN: "T-this is terrible... Th-there's a monster in the royal palace...<end>"
KO: "크, 큰일이야… 왕, 왕궁에 괴물이…!<end>"

## 560636
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか……<end>"
EN: "I see...<end>"
KO: "그렇군요……<end>"

## 560637
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…何者か？<line-break>すくなくとも余よりは、<line-break>賢いものには違いないだろうが。<end>"
EN: "…What are you? Whatever you are, you must at least be wiser than I am.<end>"
KO: "…누구냐고? 적어도 나보다 현명한 자임은 틀림없겠지.<end>"

## 560638
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "余は１８年前、王になるため、<line-break>愛のない結婚をした。<line-break>エリスを出世の道具として利用した。<line-break>そのことはずっと<line-break>あれに対する負い目になって、<line-break>余を苦しめた。<end>"
EN: "Eighteen years ago, I entered into a loveless marriage so that I might become king. I used Eris to further my own ambitions. The guilt I bore toward her has tormented me ever since.<end>"
KO: "짐은 18년 전 왕이 되기 위해 사랑 없는 결혼을 했다. 에리스를 출세의 도구로 이용했지. 그 일은 줄곧 그녀에 대한 죄책감이 되어 짐을 괴롭혔다.<end>"

## 560639
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナが、もしも、<line-break>余の娘でなければ、<line-break>余はあれと対等になれたかもしれん。<line-break>だが、あれは余に忠実であった。<line-break>余がどんなに情けない男であろうとも、<line-break>あれはあくまで余に忠実であった。<end>"
EN: "Had Tiana not been my daughter, perhaps Eris and I could have stood as equals. But she remained loyal to me. No matter how wretched a man I proved to be, her loyalty never wavered.<end>"
KO: "티아나가 만약 짐의 딸이 아니었다면 짐은 그 아이와 대등해질 수 있었을지도 모른다. 하지만 그 아이는 짐에게 충실했다. 짐이 아무리 한심한 남자여도 끝까지 충실했지.<end>"

## 560640
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれは悪くない。しかし、<line-break>それが余に苦痛をもたらしたのだ。<line-break>いや、余の苦痛そのものだった。<line-break>レムオンにあれの死刑執行書に<line-break>サインするように言われたとき、<line-break>余はやっと解放されると思った。<end>"
EN: "She did nothing wrong. Yet that was what caused me pain. No, it was pain itself. When Lemghon asked me to sign the order for her execution, I thought I would finally be free.<end>"
KO: "그 아이는 잘못이 없다. 하지만 그 존재가 짐에게 고통을 안겼다. 아니, 그 아이 자체가 짐의 고통이었다. 레무온에게 그 아이의 사형 집행서에 서명하라는 말을 들었을 때, 짐은 드디어 해방될 수 있다고 생각했다.<end>"

## 560641
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、もはや取り返しが付かぬ<line-break>という思いが、なおさら、<line-break>余を苦しめるようになった。<end>"
EN: "But knowing it was already too late to put things right only tormented me all the more.<end>"
KO: "하지만 이제 돌이킬 수 없다는 생각이 오히려 더 짐을 괴롭히게 되었다.<end>"

## 560642
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　その思いは、<line-break>　　我を呼び覚ますに十分であった。<end>"
EN: "That thought was enough to awaken me.<end>"
KO: "그 마음은 나를 깨우기에 충분했다.<end>"

## 560643
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　ティアナ、<line-break>　　　お前さえ、余の娘でなかったら、<line-break>　余は孤独な罪人とはならなかったろうに。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　　余とエリスは<line-break>　　　ふたりでお互いの罪を見ながら、<line-break>　　　　 醜く共存できたろうに…。<end>"
EN: "Tiana, if only you had not been my daughter, I would never have become a lonely sinner. Eris and I could have coexisted in our ugliness, each looking upon the other's sins...<end>"
KO: "티아나, 네가 짐의 딸만 아니었다면 짐은 고독한 죄인이 되지 않았을지도 모르겠구나. 짐과 에리스는 서로의 죄를 바라보며 추하게나마 함께 살아갈 수 있었을 텐데….<end>"

## 560644
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…余は縛られし者よ。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>ソウ、我ハ縛ラレシモノ…<line-break>…破壊神ノ円卓騎士サムスン！<end>"
EN: "…I am the Bound One. YES, I AM THE BOUND ONE… SAMSON, THE GOD OF DESTRUCTION'S KNIGHT OF THE ROUND TABLE!<end>"
KO: "…짐은 속박된 자. 그래, 나는 속박된 자… 파괴신의 원탁기사 삼손!<end>"

## 560645
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最初に愛していなくても、<line-break>次の日から<line-break>愛そうとすればよかったのだ…。<line-break>時間を浪費した。<line-break>すまんな…エリス。<line-break>今すぐ、お前にわびにゆく。<end>"
EN: "I may not have loved you at first, but I should have begun trying the very next day… I wasted time. I'm sorry, Eris. I'm coming to apologize to you now.<end>"
KO: "처음에는 사랑하지 않았더라도 다음 날부터라도 사랑하려고 했으면 됐던 거다…. 시간을 낭비했다. 미안하군… 에리스. 지금 바로 너에게 사과하러 가마.<end>"

## 560646
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勇者殿…。<line-break>闇が覆おうとしているこの世界に<line-break>光を…。<line-break>そして、我が娘ティアナを…<line-break>た、頼…みま…す。<end>"
EN: "Hero… Bring light to this world, before darkness engulfs it… And my daughter, Tiana… P-please… take care of her…<end>"
KO: "용자여…. 어둠이 뒤덮으려는 이 세계에 빛을…. 그리고 내 딸 티아나를… 부, 부탁…하오….<end>"

## 560647

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "<value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님!<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part98.toml

## 560649
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ様はもう…<line-break>陛下…<end>"
EN: "Lady Tiana is already… Your Majesty…<end>"
KO: "티아나 님은 이미… 폐하…<end>"

## 560650
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break><value:$28>様が<line-break>いてくださらなければ、<line-break>私も陛下のように…<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>… If <value:$28> hadn't been there for me, I might have ended up like His Majesty…<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님…. <value:$28>님이 계시지 않았다면 저도 폐하처럼….<end>"

## 560651
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は…どうすればいいのでしょうか？<line-break>私は…<end>"
EN: "What am I supposed to do? I...<end>"
KO: "저는… 어떻게 해야 하는 걸까요? 저는….<end>"

## 560652
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は…<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>亡くなられた陛下のために…<line-break>なにかしてあげることが<line-break>できるのでしょうか？<end>"
EN: "I... Is there anything I can do for the late sovereign?<end>"
KO: "저는… 돌아가신 폐하를 위해… 뭔가 해 드릴 수 있을까요?<end>"

## 560653
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…。<line-break>…そうですね。<line-break> <line-break>残された物を守ること。<line-break>そして、私にしかできないこと…<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Yes. I must protect what remains. And do what only I can...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님…. …그렇군요. 남겨진 것을 지키는 것. 그리고 저만이 할 수 있는 일….<end>"

## 560654
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私…やってみます。<end>"
EN: "I'll try.<end>"
KO: "저… 해볼게요.<end>"

## 560655
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　カルラの侵攻、レムオンのクーデター、<line-break>　　　　　そして、セルモノーの死。<line-break>　　　国政の中心人物を次々と失った<line-break>　　ロストールの混迷はここに極まった。<end>"
EN: "Karula's invasion, Lemghon's coup, and the death of Selmono. With the central figures of its government lost one after another, Rostorl's turmoil had reached its height.<end>"
KO: "칼라의 침공, 레무온의 쿠데타, 그리고 셀모노의 죽음. 국정의 중심인물을 차례로 잃은 로스톨의 혼란은 여기서 극에 달했다.<end>"

## 560656
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　残され貴族達に事態を収拾するだけの力はなく、<line-break>　　王女アトレイアがロストールの王位に就くことに<line-break>　　　　　　意を唱えるものもいなかった。<end>"
EN: "The nobles who remained lacked the power to bring the crisis under control, and no one opposed Princess Atleia's accession to the throne of Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "남겨진 귀족들에게는 사태를 수습할 힘이 없었고, 왕녀 아트레이아가 로스톨의 왕위에 오르는 데 이의를 제기하는 자도 없었다.<end>"

## 560657
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　飽くことを知らぬかのように広がり続ける戦渦。<line-break>　　　　まことしやかに伝えられる破壊神復活の噂。<line-break>　　　　　　　深まりゆく闇の気配…<end>"
EN: "The ravages of war continued to spread as though they knew no bounds. Rumors of the God of Destruction's return circulated with every appearance of truth. Signs of a deepening darkness...<end>"
KO: "끝을 모르는 듯 계속 번져 가는 전란. 그럴듯하게 퍼지는 파괴신 부활의 소문. 더욱 짙어지는 어둠의 기운….<end>"

## 560658
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　すべての人々が終末への予感を<line-break>　　　　　　　抱き始めていた…。<end>"
EN: "Everyone had begun to sense that the end was near...<end>"
KO: "모든 사람들이 종말의 예감을 품기 시작했다….<end>"

## 560659
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お父様…、<line-break>私は私の王女という役割を<line-break>心底嫌っていました。<line-break>逃れられない運命のように思い、<line-break>ただ、憎むことで、逃げることで<line-break>日々を生きてきました。<end>"
EN: "Father… I hated my role as a princess with all my heart. It seemed like a fate I could never escape, and so I lived each day hating it and running from it.<end>"
KO: "아버님…, 저는 제 왕녀라는 역할을 진심으로 싫어했습니다. 피할 수 없는 운명처럼 여기며 그저 미워하고 도망치며 나날을 살아왔습니다.<end>"

## 560660
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが愚かであったことに<line-break>私は、今、気づきました。<line-break>私が誰なのか、決めるのは私なのです。<line-break>私は私にしかできない道を歩んでみます。<line-break><value:$28>様。<line-break>見ていて…ください。<end>"
EN: "Only now do I see how foolish that was. I am the one who decides who I am. I will try to follow the path that only I can walk. <value:$28>… please watch me.<end>"
KO: "그것이 어리석었다는 걸 이제 깨달았습니다. 내가 누구인지 정하는 건 나 자신입니다. 저는 저만이 걸을 수 있는 길을 가 보겠습니다. <value:$28> 님. 지켜봐… 주세요.<end>"

## 560661
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　カルラの侵攻、レムオンのクーデター、<line-break>　　　　　そして、セルモノーの死。<line-break>　　　国政の中心人物を次々と失った<line-break>　　ロストールの混迷はここに極まった。<end>"
EN: "Karula's invasion, Lemghon's coup, and the death of Selmono―one after another, Rostorl had lost the central figures of its government. Now the realm's turmoil reached its height.<end>"
KO: "칼라의 침공, 레무온의 쿠데타, 그리고 셀모노의 죽음. 국정의 중심인물을 차례로 잃은 로스톨의 혼란은 여기서 극에 달했다.<end>"

## 560662

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　残され貴族達に事態を収拾するだけの力はなく、<line-break>　　　王女ティアナがロストールの王位に就くことに<line-break>　　　　　　意を唱えるものもいなかった。<end>"
EN: "The nobles who remained had no power to bring the crisis under control, and no one opposed Princess Tiana's accession to the throne of Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "남겨진 귀족들에게는 사태를 수습할 힘이 없었고, 왕녀 티아나가 로스톨의 왕위에 오르는 데 이의를 제기하는 자도 없었다.<end>"

## 560663
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　飽くことを知らぬかのように広がり続ける戦渦。<line-break>　　　　まことしやかに伝えられる破壊神復活の噂。<line-break>　　　　　　　深まりゆく闇の気配…<end>"
EN: "The maelstrom of war spread ever wider, as though it could never be sated. Rumors of the God of Destruction's revival circulated with the ring of truth. An ever-deepening sense of darkness...<end>"

## 560664
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
