JP: "　　　　　すべての人々が終末への予感を<line-break>　　　　　　　抱き始めていた…。<end>"
EN: "Everyone had begun to sense that the end was drawing near...<end>"
KO: "모든 사람들이 종말의 예감을 품기 시작했다….<end>"

## 560666
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か、ちょうどいい。<line-break>今、ロセンの潜入作戦について<line-break>説明している。お前も聞いておけ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, perfect timing. I'm explaining the plan to infiltrate Rocen. You should hear this too.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가, 마침 잘 왔다. 지금 로센 잠입 작전을 설명 중이다. 너도 들어 둬라.<end>"

## 560667
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、<line-break>先日の戦いで、ロセン王国は<line-break>カルラ率いる東方攻略軍に敗れた。<line-break>噂によると、次はこのリベルダムを<line-break>攻略するための準備を行っているらしい。<end>"
EN: "Now, in the recent battle, the Kingdom of Rocen was defeated by the Eastern Campaign Army under Karula. Rumor has it they're now preparing to take Liberdam.<end>"
KO: "자, 지난 전투에서 로센 왕국은 카를라가 이끄는 동방 공략군에 패했다. 소문에 따르면 다음에는 이 리벨덤을 공략할 준비를 하고 있다더군.<end>"

## 560668
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこでだ。我々はカルラ軍の<line-break>リベルダム攻略の詳細をつかむべく<line-break>その作戦草案を奪う。<line-break>カルラ軍の作戦がつかめれば、<line-break>そのスキをついて、<line-break>カルラを討つことも可能だ。<end>"
EN: "So we will steal the draft of Karula's Army's plans and learn precisely how they intend to take Liberdam. Once we know their strategy, we may even be able to exploit an opening and kill Karula.<end>"
KO: "그래서 말인데, 우리는 카를라군의 리벨덤 공략 세부 내용을 알아내기 위해 그 작전 초안을 빼앗는다. 카를라군의 작전을 파악하면 그 틈을 노려 카를라를 쓰러뜨리는 것도 가능하다.<end>"

## 560669
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仮に敵に草案の奪取を覚られても、<line-break>その時は敵も作戦に修正を加えねば<line-break>ならんだろう。<line-break>その間、我々は次の手はずを整える<line-break>時間が稼げるというわけだ。<line-break>良策だろう、クリュセイス？<end>"
EN: "Even if the enemy realizes the draft has been stolen, they'll have to revise their plans. That will buy us time to prepare our next move. A sound plan, wouldn't you agree, Khryuseis?<end>"
KO: "설령 적에게 초안 탈취가 들통나도 그때는 적도 작전을 수정해야 할 테지. 그동안 우리는 다음 수를 준비할 시간을 벌 수 있어. 좋은 계책이지, 크류세이스?<end>"

## 560670
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わかりました。<line-break>その作戦でいきましょう。<line-break>で、具体的な段取りは？<end>"
EN: "...Very well. We'll proceed with that plan. What are the specifics?<end>"
KO: "…알겠습니다. 그 작전으로 가죠. 그래서 구체적인 절차는?<end>"

## 560671
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "部隊をふたつにわける。<line-break>まず１部隊が単独でロセンに潜入<line-break>城門を開き、本隊を引き入れる。<line-break>先に潜入した部隊は陽動部隊となり、<line-break>ディンガル兵を引きつける。<line-break>その間に本隊が草案を奪うのだ。<end>"
EN: "We'll split our forces in two. One unit will infiltrate Rocen alone, open the castle gate, and let the main force in. The advance unit will then serve as a diversion and draw off the Dyneskal soldiers while the main force steals the draft.<end>"
KO: "부대를 둘로 나눈다. 먼저 한 부대가 단독으로 로센에 잠입해 성문을 열고 본대를 들인다. 먼저 잠입한 부대는 양동부대가 되어 딘갈 병사를 끌어들인다. 그 사이 본대가 초안을 빼앗는 거다.<end>"

## 560672
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこでだ、<value:$28>。<line-break>お前の任務だが…<end>"
EN: "Now then, <value:$28>. As for your assignment...<end>"
KO: "그래서 말인데, <value:$28>. 네 임무는….<end>"

## 560673
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前には、先に単独で<line-break>ロセンに潜入してもらう。<end>"
EN: "You will infiltrate Rocen alone, ahead of the others.<end>"
KO: "너는 먼저 혼자 로센에 잠입해라.<end>"

## 560674
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんですって？<end>"
EN: "What did you say?<end>"
KO: "뭐라고요?<end>"

## 560675
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むろん、大きな危険をともなうが、<line-break>この役目を任せることのできるのは<line-break>知恵も勇気も優れた者に限られる。<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>お前なら果たせると思うが…。<end>"
EN: "Naturally, it carries considerable risk, but this duty can only be entrusted to someone of exceptional courage and resourcefulness. <value:$28>, I believe you can do it...<end>"
KO: "물론 큰 위험이 따르지만 이 역할을 맡길 수 있는 건 지혜와 용기가 뛰어난 자뿐이다. <value:$28>. 너라면 해낼 수 있을 것 같은데….<end>"

## 560677
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっくっく、そうか！<line-break>やってくれるか、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh, I see! You'll do it, then, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "큭큭큭, 그런가! 해 주겠나, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 560678
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我々の今後を左右する<line-break>重大な任務です。<line-break>…健闘を祈ります。<end>"
EN: "This is a vital mission that will determine our future. ...I wish you success.<end>"
KO: "우리의 앞날을 좌우할 중대한 임무입니다. …건투를 빕니다.<end>"

## 560679
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、聞いてのとおりだ。<line-break>この作戦が成功すれば、<line-break>ロセン復興の日も近いぞ！<line-break>すぐ準備にとりかかるのだ！！<end>"
EN: "Good. You heard the plan. If it succeeds, the restoration of Rocen will soon be at hand! Begin preparations at once!<end>"
KO: "좋아, 들은 대로다. 이 작전이 성공하면 로센 부흥의 날도 머지않았다! 당장 준비에 착수하라!!<end>"

## 560680
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうした？<line-break>浮かない顔だな。<end>"
EN: "What's wrong? You look troubled.<end>"
KO: "왜 그래? 표정이 어둡군.<end>"

## 560681
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロティの…お前の父の敵を討つため<line-break>わざわざ、奴にあの任務を与えたのだ。<line-break>この任務なら、必ず死ぬ。<end>"
EN: "I gave that one the assignment precisely to avenge Lottie... to avenge your father. This mission means certain death.<end>"
KO: "로티의… 네 아버지의 원수를 갚기 위해 일부러 그에게 그 임무를 준 거다. 그 임무라면 반드시 죽는다.<end>"

## 560682
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、我らの目的の達成も近づく。<line-break>奴はロセン解放の英雄として死ぬのだ。<line-break>もったいないくらいではないか。<end>"
EN: "And it will bring us closer to achieving our purpose. That one will die a hero of Rocen's liberation. Almost more honor than is deserved, wouldn't you say?<end>"
KO: "그리고 우리의 목적 달성도 가까워진다. 그자는 로센 해방의 영웅으로 죽는 것이다. 오히려 과분하지 않은가.<end>"

## 560683
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…確かに、そうですわね。<line-break>父の仇を討つことができるのは、<line-break>うれしいですわ。<line-break>しかし、これはリベルダムの未来が<line-break>かかった大事。私情で戦士を死地へ<line-break>追いやる真似は、今後、認めません。<end>"
EN: "...Yes, I suppose so. I am glad that my father will be avenged. But the future of Liberdam is at stake. From now on, I will not allow personal feelings to be used to send a warrior to certain death.<end>"
KO: "…확실히 그렇군요. 아버지의 원수를 갚을 수 있다는 건 기쁩니다. 하지만 이건 리벨덤의 미래가 걸린 중대사. 사적인 감정으로 전사를 죽음의 땅으로 몰아넣는 건 앞으로 인정하지 않겠습니다.<end>"

## 560684
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よろしくて、アンティノ・マモン。<line-break>あなたが、父の親友でも、市民でも<line-break>特別扱いはしません。<end>"
EN: "Am I understood, Antino Mammon? Whether you were my father's close friend or are a citizen makes no difference. You will receive no special treatment.<end>"
KO: "잘 들으세요, 안티노 마몬. 당신이 아버지의 절친이든 시민이든 특별대우는 하지 않겠습니다.<end>"

## 560685
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど、立派な心がけだ。<line-break>公正な指揮官、というわけだ。<line-break>肝に銘じよう、クリュセイス。<line-break>まあ、ロティならこう言うだろうがね。<line-break>クリュセイス、なぜ怒るのだね？<line-break>お前のためを思ってしたことだよ、と。<end>"
EN: "I see. A most admirable conviction. So you mean to be an impartial commander. I shall take that to heart, Khryuseis. Though Lottie would probably say, “Khryuseis, why are you angry? I only did it for your sake.”<end>"
KO: "과연, 훌륭한 마음가짐이군. 공정한 지휘관이라는 거지. 명심하마, 크류세이스. 뭐, 로티라면 이렇게 말하겠지. '크류세이스, 왜 화를 내느냐? 다 너를 위해 한 일이란다.'라고.<end>"

## 560686
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…父の死後、少しずつ、お父様のことが<line-break>わたくしにもわかってきました。<line-break> <line-break>お父様が亡くなる前のおとなしくて<line-break>従順なわたくしなら、気づきもしない<line-break>ようなことも、たくさん…。<end>"
EN: "...Since my father's death, I have gradually come to understand him. There are so many things I would never even have noticed when he was alive, when I was still meek and obedient...<end>"
KO: "…아버지가 돌아가신 뒤 조금씩 아버지에 대해 저도 알게 됐어요. 아버지가 돌아가시기 전의 얌전하고 순종적인 저라면 눈치채지도 못했을 많은 것들을….<end>"

## 560687
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そして、ロティがそんな奴だったと<line-break>認められるようになったのも、あ奴の<line-break>おかげだというのに気づいているかな？<line-break>…気づいてるな。<line-break>意識的にではないにせよ、な。<line-break>小娘というものはずるいものだからな。<end>"
EN: "...And do you realize it was thanks to that one that you came to accept Lottie for the man he was? Of course you do. Perhaps not consciously, but you do. Young girls can be so sly.<end>"
KO: "…그리고 로티가 그런 녀석이었다는 걸 인정하게 된 것도 그 사람 덕분이라는 사실을 깨닫고 있나? …깨닫고 있군. 의식적으로 그런 건 아니더라도 말이야. 어린 아가씨란 참 약삭빠른 법이니까.<end>"

## 560688
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>はあの娘の支え…。<line-break>だから、両方に消えて<line-break>もらわなくちゃいかん。<line-break>だんだん賢くなっていくあの娘に<line-break>私がロティの偽善に我慢できずに、<line-break>毒殺したのを知られる前に。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> is that girl's support... so both of them must disappear, before that increasingly clever girl learns that I could no longer stomach Lottie's hypocrisy and poisoned him.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>는 그 아이의 버팀목…. 그러니 둘 다 사라져 줘야 해. 점점 영리해지는 그 아이가 내가 로티의 위선에 참지 못하고 독살했다는 걸 알아채기 전에.<end>"

## 560689
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待って、<value:$28>。<line-break>これは…なんか変だよ。<end>"
EN: "Wait, <value:$28>. Something... something's wrong.<end>"
KO: "잠깐, <value:$28>. 이거… 뭔가 이상해.<end>"

## 560690
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…妙だ。<line-break>人がいない。<end>"
EN: "...Strange. There's no one here.<end>"
KO: "…이상해. 사람이 없어.<end>"

## 560691
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、変だと思わない。<line-break>ぼくたちだけしかいないよ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, don't you think this is strange? We're the only ones here.<end>"
KO: "저기, 이상하지 않아? 우리밖에 없어.<end>"

## 560692
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（…静かすぎる、<line-break>まさか…まちぶせ…？）<end>"
EN: "(It's too quiet... Could this be... an ambush?)<end>"
KO: "(…너무 조용해. 설마… 매복…?)<end>"

## 560693
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃーい。待ってたよん、<line-break>罠にかかったドブネズミちゃん。<line-break>死ぬ７分前の気分ってどんな感じ？<end>"
EN: "Welcoooome! I've been waiting, my little sewer rat caught in a trap. How does it feel to have seven minutes left to live?<end>"
KO: "어서 와~. 기다렸어, 함정에 걸린 시궁쥐 양. 죽기 7분 전 기분은 어때?<end>"

## 560694
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひっさしぶりー！<line-break><value:$28>、元気そうじゃん？<line-break>会いたかったよー！　でもさ……<line-break>あたしたちの関係もこ・こ・ま・で？<line-break>別れは、いつも悲しいものね。<line-break>てなわけで、死んじゃってちょうだい？<end>"
EN: "Long time no see! Looking well, aren't you, <value:$28>? I've missed you! But... I guess this is the end of our little relationship. Parting is always sad, isn't it? So do me a favor and die, okay?<end>"
KO: "오랜만이야~! <value:$28>, 잘 지낸 것 같네? 보고 싶었어~! 하지만 말이야…… 우리 사이도 이·제·끝인가? 이별은 언제나 슬픈 법이지. 그러니까 죽어 줄래?<end>"

## 560695
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ったく、あたしが統治してから<line-break>ロセンはリベルダムをしのぐ<line-break>大都市になったってのにさー<line-break>あんたたちは何が気に入らないのかねー<line-break>人間、助け合っていかないと<line-break>人類、発展しないよん？<end>"
EN: "Honestly, since I took over, Rocen has grown into a great city surpassing Liberdam, and you're still unhappy? Humanity will never advance unless people help each other, you know?<end>"
KO: "정말, 내가 통치하고 나서 로센은 리벨덤을 능가하는 대도시가 됐는데 너희는 뭐가 마음에 안 드는 거야. 인간은 서로 도우며 살아야 인류가 발전한다고?<end>"

## 560696
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ところで、侵入者ちゃん。<line-break>あんたたちとは初対面みたいだし<line-break>殺しちゃう前に名乗っとくね<line-break>アタシは青竜将軍カルラ。<line-break>ディンガル東方攻略軍司令。<line-break>口が悪いのは青い死神って呼んでる。<end>"
EN: "By the way, my little intruders, it looks like we haven't met. I suppose I should introduce myself before I kill you. I'm Karula, the Azure Dragon General, commander of Dyneskal's Eastern Campaign Army. Those with filthy mouths call me the Blue Reaper.<end>"
KO: "그런데 침입자 양. 너희와는 처음 만나는 것 같으니 죽이기 전에 이름부터 말해 둘게. 나는 청룡장군 카를라. 딘갈 동방 공략군 사령관. 입이 험한 사람들은 푸른 사신이라 부르지.<end>"

## 560697
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、死神にでくわしたら、<line-break>どうなるかは決まってるやね。<line-break>この世とおサラバする準備はできてる？<end>"
EN: "Well, there's only one way an encounter with the Reaper can end. Ready to kiss this world goodbye?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 사신과 마주치면 어떻게 될지는 정해져 있지. 이 세상과 작별할 준비는 됐어?<end>"

## 560698
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうそう、外であんたの合図を<line-break>待っている連中のことなら<line-break>心配しないでいいから。<line-break>あんたたちを処分したあと、<line-break>おびき寄せて、だまし討ち。<line-break>ちゃ～んと地獄に送ってあげるよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, and don't worry about the people outside waiting for your signal. Once we've disposed of you, we'll lure them in, take them by surprise, and send every last one straight to hell.<end>"
KO: "그래 그래, 밖에서 네 신호를 기다리는 녀석들 말이라면 걱정 안 해도 돼. 너희를 처리한 뒤 유인해서 속여 죽여 줄게. 제대로 지옥에 보내 주지.<end>"

## 560699
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>！<line-break>逃げなきゃ！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! We have to run!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>! 도망쳐야 해!<end>"

## 560700
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "逃げてもムダだよん。<line-break>門はしーっかり閉じてるし<line-break>街中包囲してっから。<end>"
EN: "Running won't do you any good. The gates are locked up tight, and we've surrounded the whole city.<end>"
KO: "도망쳐도 소용없어. 문은 단단히 닫혀 있고 도시 전체를 포위했거든.<end>"

## 560701
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こっちの動きは<line-break>敵に完全にバレていたみたい。<line-break>どうしよう、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "It looks like the enemy knew exactly what we were planning. What do we do, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "우리 움직임은 적에게 완전히 들킨 것 같아. 어떡하지, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 560702
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど…。そうだね、<line-break>外にいる人たちに知らせないと。<line-break>急ごう、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Oh... right. We have to warn everyone outside. Hurry, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "그렇군…. 맞아, 밖에 있는 사람들에게 알려야 해. 서두르자, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 560703
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…くっ！<line-break>逃げるんだ。<end>"
EN: "...Damn! Run.<end>"
KO: "…큭! 도망쳐.<end>"

## 560704
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "逃げてもムダだよん。<line-break>門はしーっかり閉じてるし<line-break>街中包囲してっから。<end>"
EN: "Running won't do you any good. The gates are locked up tight, and we've surrounded the whole city.<end>"
KO: "도망쳐도 소용없어. 문은 단단히 닫혀 있고 도시 전체를 포위했거든.<end>"

## 560705
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なるほどな。<line-break>仕方ない、ついていってやる。<end>"
EN: "...I see. Fine, I'll come with you.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군. 어쩔 수 없지, 따라가 주마.<end>"

## 560706
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何をしているの、<value:$28>！<line-break>さっさと逃げなきゃ。<end>"
EN: "What are you doing, <value:$28>? We have to get out of here!<end>"
KO: "뭐 하는 거야, <value:$28>! 빨리 도망쳐야지.<end>"

## 560707
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "逃げてもムダだよん。<line-break>門はしーっかり閉じてるし<line-break>街中包囲してっから。<end>"
EN: "Running won't do you any good. The gates are locked up tight, and we've surrounded the whole city.<end>"
KO: "도망쳐도 소용없어. 문은 단단히 닫혀 있고 도시 전체를 포위했거든.<end>"

## 560708
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "作戦は見破られていたみたいだね。<line-break>どうしよう、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "It looks like our plan was exposed. What do we do, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "작전은 간파당한 모양이네. 어떡하지, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 560709
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…外の部隊に知らせるの？<line-break>なるほどね。急がないと！<end>"
EN: "...You're going to warn the troops outside? Right. We have to hurry!<end>"
KO: "…밖의 부대에 알리려고? 그렇구나. 서둘러야 해!<end>"

## 560710
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "逃げてもムダムダ<line-break>町中、包囲してっから。<end>"
EN: "Run all you like. We've got the whole city surrounded.<end>"
KO: "도망쳐도 소용없어. 마을 전체를 포위했으니까.<end>"

## 560711
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハハハ！　バカめ。すべての門は<line-break>我々が押さえたと言っているだろうが！<line-break>いったいどこへ逃げるつもりだ！？<end>"
EN: "Ha ha ha! Fools! I told you, we control every gate! Just where do you think you're going to run?<end>"
KO: "하하하! 바보 같으니. 모든 문은 우리가 장악했다고 했잖아! 대체 어디로 도망칠 생각이냐!?<end>"

## 560712
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わっかんないかなー<line-break><value:$28>は鐘楼に向かったのよ<line-break>門の近くにあったでしょ。デカイのが。<line-break>あの鐘を鳴らして<line-break>外にいるお仲間に<line-break>異変を伝えるつもりなのよ。<end>"
EN: "Don't you get it? <value:$28> is heading for the bell tower. You remember―the huge one near the gate? The plan is to ring that bell and warn those friends waiting outside that something's wrong.<end>"
KO: "모르겠어? <value:$28>는 종루로 향했어. 문 근처에 큰 거 있었잖아. 그 종을 울려서 밖에 있는 동료들에게 이상을 알리려는 거야.<end>"

## 560713
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、こういう絶体絶命の状況で<line-break>最善の策を思いつく機転、<line-break>あきらめの悪さ…結構スキだよ。<line-break>ヤツらに鐘を鳴らさせるな！<line-break>最高の敵には最高のもてなしを！<line-break>捕まえたら、遠慮なく斬り捨てよ！<end>"
EN: "Heh. The quick thinking to find the best move in a hopeless situation, the sheer refusal to give up... I rather like it. Don't let them ring that bell! Only the finest welcome for the finest enemy! Once you catch them, cut them down without mercy!<end>"
KO: "후후, 이런 절체절명의 상황에서 최선의 수를 떠올리는 재치, 포기하지 않는 끈기… 꽤 마음에 들어. 녀석들이 종을 울리게 하지 마! 최고의 적에게는 최고의 대접을! 붙잡으면 사양 말고 베어 버려!<end>"

## 560714
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>13…早く鳴らせ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>22着いたよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>早く鐘を鳴らして！<end><if><value:$01><equal>6鐘だよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>早く鳴らして！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>13...Ring it, quickly.<end><if><value:$01><equal>22We're here, <value:$28>. Ring the bell, quickly!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6Here's the bell, <value:$28>. Hurry and ring it!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>13…빨리 울려.<end><if><value:$01><equal>22도착했어, <value:$28>. 빨리 종을 울려!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6종이야, <value:$28>. 빨리 울려!<end>"

## 560715
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>13…これで気づかなくても、<line-break>俺たちの知ったことではない。<end><if><value:$01><equal>22外の人たち、<line-break>気づいてくれるといいんだけど…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>6外の人たち、<line-break>わかってくれればいいけど…。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>13...If they don't notice this, that's not our problem.<end><if><value:$01><equal>22I just hope the people outside notice...<end><if><value:$01><equal>6I hope the people outside understand...<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>13…이래도 눈치채지 못하면 우리 알 바 아니지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>22밖에 있는 사람들이 알아차려 주면 좋겠는데….<end><if><value:$01><equal>6밖에 있는 사람들이 알아주면 좋겠는데….<end>"

## 560716
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "街の中が急に騒がしくなってきたぞ。<line-break>どうやら、うまくいったようだ。<end>"
EN: "The city has suddenly erupted into commotion. It seems the plan worked.<end>"
KO: "도시 안이 갑자기 소란스러워졌어. 아무래도 잘된 모양이군.<end>"

## 560717
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この機会を逃してはいけません。<line-break>すぐ突入しましょう！　クリュセイス様！<end>"
EN: "We must not let this opportunity pass. We should attack at once, Lady Khryuseis!<end>"
KO: "이 기회를 놓쳐서는 안 됩니다. 당장 돌입하죠! 크류세이스 님!<end>"

## 560718
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ、あの鐘の音は？<line-break>たしか、突入の合図は…<end>"
EN: "What's that bell ringing for? I thought the signal to attack was...<end>"
KO: "뭐지, 저 종소리는? 분명 돌입 신호는….<end>"

## 560719
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…作戦は失敗です！<line-break>撤退します！！<end>"
EN: "...The operation has failed! Retreat!<end>"
KO: "…작전은 실패입니다! 철수합니다!!<end>"

## 560720
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれは、<value:$28>が<line-break>作戦の失敗を伝えているのです！<line-break>退きます！！<end>"
EN: "That is <value:$28> warning us that the operation has failed! Fall back!<end>"
KO: "저건 <value:$28>가 작전 실패를 알리는 겁니다! 후퇴합니다!!<end>"

## 560721
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイス様、なぜ、そのように？<line-break>同志<value:$28>は<line-break>緊急事態で合図を変更したのやも…。<end>"
EN: "Lady Khryuseis, what makes you say that? Comrade <value:$28> may have changed the signal because of an emergency...<end>"
KO: "크류세이스 님, 어째서 그렇게…? 동지 <value:$28>은 긴급 상황이라 신호를 바꾼 것일 수도….<end>"

## 560722
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "緊急事態ということは<line-break>敵にこちらの動きが知られてしまった<line-break>可能性が大きいわ。<line-break>今、突入しても、被害を出すだけです。<line-break>急いで引き返しましょう。おそらく、<line-break>命がけで知らせてくれたのですから。<end>"
EN: "If there is an emergency, the enemy has most likely learned of our movements. Attacking now will only cost lives. We must turn back at once. That warning was probably sent at mortal risk.<end>"
KO: "긴급 상황이라는 건 적에게 우리의 움직임이 알려졌을 가능성이 크다는 뜻이야. 지금 돌입해도 피해만 늘어날 뿐이야. 서둘러 돌아가자. 아마 목숨을 걸고 알려준 걸 테니까.<end>"

## 560723
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ならば、なおのこと見殺しにはできません！<line-break>突入して同志<value:$28>を<line-break>救い出しましょう！　クリュセイス様！<end>"
EN: "Then all the more reason not to abandon our comrade! Let us attack and rescue Comrade <value:$28>, Lady Khryuseis!<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 더더욱 보고만 있을 수 없습니다! 돌입해서 동지 <value:$28>을 구해 냅시다! 크류세이스 님!<end>"

## 560724
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…突入しても死ぬだけよ。<line-break>命令です。…退きます。<end>"
EN: "...An attack would only get us killed. That is an order. ...We are withdrawing.<end>"
KO: "…돌입해도 죽을 뿐이야. 명령입니다. …후퇴합니다.<end>"

## 560725
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…はっ、わかりました。<end>"
EN: "...Yes, ma'am. Understood.<end>"
KO: "…예, 알겠습니다.<end>"

## 560726
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…生きて…帰って。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "...Come back alive... <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…살아서… 돌아와. <value:$28>.<end>"

## 560727
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ。<line-break>連中は逃げ切れるみたいだよ。<line-break>機転きくじゃん？<end>"
EN: "Heh. Looks like that lot will get away. Quick thinking, huh?<end>"
KO: "후훗. 녀석들은 도망칠 수 있을 것 같아. 재치 있잖아?<end>"

## 560728
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、それも、あたしが追っ手を<line-break>差し向けなければ、だけどね？<line-break> <line-break>しっかし、よくここまで登ってきたよね。<line-break>その情熱であたしに<line-break>アタックしてくれればよかったのに。<end>"
EN: "Of course, that's only if I don't send anyone after them. Still, you did well to climb all the way up here. If only you'd pursued me with that much passion.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그것도 내가 추격자를 보내지 않는다면 말이지? 그런데 정말 잘도 여기까지 올라왔네. 그 열정으로 나한테 들이대 줬으면 좋았을 텐데.<end>"

## 560729
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こいつらですか、カルラ様？<end>"
EN: "Are these the intruders, Lady Karula?<end>"
KO: "이놈들입니까, 카를라 님?<end>"

## 560730
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こっちはアイリーン。メチャ強。<line-break>こりゃ、ますます絶体絶命度が<line-break>上がりましたなぁ、くせ者ちゃん。<end>"
EN: "This is Irene. She's absurdly strong. Things just got even more hopeless for you, my little rogue.<end>"
KO: "이쪽은 아이린. 엄청 강해. 이거 점점 더 절체절명의 정도가 올라가네요, 수상한 친구.<end>"

## 560732
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…元気そうね、<value:$28>。<line-break>こんな所で会うとは<line-break>思わなかったけど…<end>"
EN: "...You look well, <value:$28>. I never expected to meet you here...<end>"
KO: "…건강해 보이네, <value:$28>. 이런 곳에서 만날 줄은 몰랐지만…<end>"

## 560733
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "感動の再会って奴ぅ？<line-break>運命の女神って残酷だよね。<line-break>ちょーっと、好きになれないかな。<line-break>アイリーンの強さは知ってるよね？<line-break>絶体絶命度急上昇！　って感じかな。<line-break>どうするぅ？　くせ者ちゃん？<end>"
EN: "A touching reunion, is it? The Goddess of Fate can be so cruel. I don't think I like her very much. You know how strong Irene is, don't you? Things just got a whole lot more hopeless! So what'll you do, my little rogue?<end>"
KO: "감동의 재회라는 거야? 운명의 여신은 잔혹하네. 별로 좋아할 수 없을 것 같아. 아이린이 얼마나 강한지는 알지? 절체절명도가 급상승! 그런 느낌이려나. 어떻게 할래, 수상한 녀석?<end>"

## 560734
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…元気そうね、<value:$28>。<line-break>こんな所で会うとは<line-break>思わなかったけど…<end>"
EN: "...You look well, <value:$28>. I never expected to meet you here...<end>"
KO: "…건강해 보이네, <value:$28>. 이런 곳에서 만날 줄은 몰랐지만…<end>"

## 560735
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な～んだ、知り合いだったんだー。<line-break>あたしが紹介しようと<line-break>思ってたのになー。<line-break>アイリーン、いつもマジメだけど、<line-break>今日はいつも以上に<line-break>マジメーな目つきじゃん。<end>"
EN: "Oh, so you already know each other. And here I was going to introduce you. Irene is always so serious, but those eyes look even more serious than usual today.<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 아는 사이였구나. 내가 소개해 주려고 했는데. 아이린은 늘 진지하지만 오늘은 평소보다 더 진지한 눈빛이네.<end>"

## 560736
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こりゃ、ますます絶体絶命度が<line-break>上がっちゃいましたなぁ、<line-break>くせ者ちゃん？<end>"
EN: "Things just got even more hopeless for you, my little rogue.<end>"
KO: "이거, 점점 절체절명의 정도가 올라가 버렸네요, 수상한 분?<end>"

## 560737
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "元気そうね、<value:$28>。<line-break>こんな所で会うとは<line-break>思わなかったけど。<line-break>まさか、<line-break>あなたが解放軍なんかに<line-break>いるとはね。<end>"
EN: "You look well, <value:$28>. I never expected to meet you here. And certainly not as a member of the Liberation Army.<end>"
KO: "잘 지내는 것 같네, <value:$28>. 이런 곳에서 만날 줄은 몰랐지만. 설마 네가 해방군 같은 데 있을 줄이야.<end>"

## 560739
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こりゃ、ますます絶体絶命度が<line-break>上がっちゃいましたなぁ、<line-break>くせ者ちゃん？<end>"
EN: "Things just got even more hopeless for you, my little rogue.<end>"
KO: "이거, 점점 절체절명의 정도가 올라가 버렸네요, 수상한 분?<end>"

## 560740
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そろそろ、マジ話いこうじゃない？<line-break>あたしの部下になんない？<line-break>そしたら、命は助けてあげる。<line-break>あんたみたいな、<line-break>かわいくて度胸のあるコ<line-break>殺しちゃうなんてもったいないもんね。<end>"
EN: "How about we get serious? Why not work for me? Do that, and I'll spare your life. It'd be such a waste to kill someone as cute and daring as you.<end>"
KO: "슬슬 진지한 이야기 해 볼까? 내 부하 안 될래? 그러면 목숨은 살려 줄게. 너처럼 귀엽고 배짱 있는 애를 죽이긴 아깝잖아.<end>"

## 560741
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "外の連中も見逃してあげるわ。<line-break>あんな奴ら、あんたに比べたら<line-break>ま、カスだかんね。<line-break>答えが、いいえなら<line-break>あんたも外の連中も殺すだけ。<line-break>さて、返事はどう？　くせ者ちゃん。<end>"
EN: "I'll let the people outside go too. Compared with you, that lot's nothing but trash anyway. Say no, and I'll just kill you and all of them. So, what's your answer, my little rogue?<end>"
KO: "밖의 녀석들도 봐줄게. 걔들은 너에 비하면 뭐, 찌꺼기니까. 대답이 '아니'라면 너도 밖의 녀석들도 죽일 뿐이야. 자, 대답은? 수상한 녀석.<end>"

## 560742
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わかった。部下になろう。<end>"
EN: "...All right. I'll serve under you.<end>"
KO: "…알겠다. 부하가 되지.<end>"

## 560743
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "断る！<end>"
EN: "I refuse!<end>"
KO: "거절한다!<end>"

## 560744
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マジ？<line-break>ホントに味方になってくれんの？<end>"
EN: "Seriously? You'll really join me?<end>"
KO: "진짜? 정말 우리 편이 돼 주는 거야?<end>"

## 560746
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ様、よろしいのですか？<line-break><value:$28>が、<line-break>忠誠を誓うとは、思えないのですが？<end>"
EN: "Lady Karula, are you certain? I cannot imagine <value:$28> swearing true loyalty to you.<end>"
KO: "카를라 님, 괜찮으시겠습니까? <value:$28>이 충성을 맹세할 거라곤 생각되지 않습니다만.<end>"

## 560747
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>みたいな<line-break>優秀な人材は、<line-break>なかなか手に入らないからねー。<line-break>それぐらいは仕方ないでしょ。<end>"
EN: "Talent like <value:$28> is hard to come by. That's a risk I'll just have to take.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 같은 우수한 인재는, 좀처럼 구할 수 없으니까. 그 정도는 어쩔 수 없잖아.<end>"

## 560748
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "要は、あたしが裏切られないくらいに<line-break>大きくなればいい。<line-break>そ・れ・だ・け。<end>"
EN: "All I have to do is grow so powerful no one can betray me. That's. All.<end>"
KO: "요는 내가 배신당하지 않을 만큼 커지면 돼. 그·것·뿐.<end>"

## 560749
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ様、よろしいのですか？<line-break>こんなに簡単に裏切る者を仲間にして。<end>"
EN: "Lady Karula, are you certain? Someone who betrays allies this readily cannot be trusted among us.<end>"
KO: "카를라 님, 괜찮으시겠습니까? 이렇게 쉽게 배신하는 자를 동료로 받아들여도.<end>"

## 560750
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "要は、あたしが裏切られないくらいに<line-break>大きくなればいい。<line-break>そ・れ・だ・け。<end>"
EN: "All I have to do is grow so powerful no one can betray me. That's. All.<end>"
KO: "요는 내가 배신당하지 않을 만큼 커지면 돼. 그·것·뿐.<end>"

## 560751
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンてば、気にしすぎ。<line-break>まーったく、かわいいんだから。<line-break>んじゃ、引き上げよっか。<end>"
EN: "Irene, you worry too much. Honestly, you're adorable. Well, shall we head back?<end>"
KO: "아이린도 참, 너무 신경 쓴다니까. 정말 귀엽다니까. 그럼 철수할까.<end>"

## 560753
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍のカスどもの始末より、<line-break><value:$28>が手に入った、<line-break>その方が大収穫よ。これでよしっと<line-break>あーあ。結局、リベルダムを<line-break>軍隊で制圧しちゃうしかないんだ。<line-break>ガレキに変えちゃうしか。<end>"
EN: "Getting <value:$28> is a much greater prize than disposing of the Liberation Army's rabble. That settles that. Ah, well. I suppose we'll have to take Liberdam by force after all. Reduce it to rubble.<end>"
KO: "해방군 쓰레기들을 처리하는 것보다 <value:$28>를 손에 넣은 게 더 큰 수확이지. 이걸로 됐고. 아아, 결국 리벨덤은 군대로 제압할 수밖에 없네. 잔해로 만들어 버릴 수밖에.<end>"

## 560754
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ様！<line-break>リベルダムを<line-break>ガレキに変えるって…！<end>"
EN: "Lady Karula! Reduce Liberdam to rubble...?<end>"
KO: "칼라 님! 리벨덤을 잔해로 만들겠다고요…!?<end>"

## 560755
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はーい、今日は解散。<line-break>みんな来るべき日に備えて<line-break>腕は磨いていてね。期待してるわ。<end>"
EN: "All right, everyone, that's all for today. Keep honing your skills for the day to come. I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "자, 오늘은 해산. 다들 다가올 날을 대비해서 실력을 갈고닦아 둬. 기대하고 있을게.<end>"

## 560757
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、裏切っちゃイヤよん。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Don't betray me, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "후훗, 배신하면 싫어, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 560758
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、<line-break>潔いのか、あきらめがよすぎるのか。<line-break>義に篤いのか、カッコつけなのか。<line-break>ま、あたしに言わせれば<line-break>バカ、ってことよね。<line-break>生きのびられたのに死を選ぶなんて。<end>"
EN: "Good grief. Is that admirable resolve, or are you simply too eager to give up? Are you loyal to a fault, or merely showing off? In my book, it all amounts to one thing: stupidity. You could have lived, but chose death instead.<end>"
KO: "원 참, 결단력이 좋은 건지 포기가 너무 빠른 건지. 의리가 깊은 건지 멋 부리는 건지. 뭐, 내가 보기엔 바보지. 살아남을 수 있었는데 죽음을 택하다니.<end>"

## 560759
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>！<line-break>ロープを伝ってこい！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! Use the rope to get over here!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>! 로프를 타고 와!<end>"

## 560760
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うわー、<value:$28>もやるけど<line-break>小さな勇者様もやるよねー。<line-break>とっさにあっこまで考えて助けたんだ。<line-break>…だっけっどっ、あたしに挑むには<line-break>まだまだ実力が足りないわ。<line-break>ね、アイリーン？<end>"
EN: "Wow. <value:$28> is impressive, but our little hero did rather well too. Coming up with all that in an instant and pulling off a rescue! But you're still nowhere near strong enough to challenge me. Right, Irene?<end>"
KO: "우와, <value:$28>도 대단하지만 작은 용사님도 제법인데. 순간적으로 거기까지 생각해서 구했잖아. …그렇지만 나한테 덤비기엔 아직 실력이 모자라. 그렇지, 아이린?<end>"

## 560761
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー、わかってると思うけどぉ<line-break>これって、あんたのことね。<line-break>アイリーン？<line-break>あたしがロープを切ろうとしたらさ、<line-break>あんた、後ろからあたしに<line-break>襲いかかろうとしてたでしょ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I'm sure you've realized by now, but I mean you, Irene. When I moved to cut the rope, you were about to attack me from behind, weren't you?<end>"
KO: "아, 알겠지만 이건 네 얘기야. 아이린? 내가 밧줄을 끊으려 했을 때 너 뒤에서 날 덮치려 했잖아.<end>"

## 560762
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分が勝てると思ってはいなかった。<line-break>ただ、時間くらいなら稼げると<line-break>思ってた…くすっ、そうでしょ？<end>"
EN: "You knew you couldn't win. You only thought you could buy some time... Heh. Isn't that right?<end>"
KO: "자기가 이길 수 있을 거라곤 생각하지 않았어. 그냥 시간 정도는 벌 수 있다고 생각했지… 후후, 그렇지?<end>"

## 560763
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "甘い、甘い、甘い、甘い！<line-break>あたしの性格、理解してないっしょ？<line-break>あたしは、まず、ロープを切って<line-break>その後、呆然とするか逆上したあんたを<line-break>ゆうゆうと斬って捨てるわ。<end>"
EN: "Naive, naive, naive, naive! You really don't understand me, do you? I would have cut the rope first, then leisurely struck you down while you stood stunned or flew into a rage.<end>"
KO: "물러, 물러, 물러, 물러! 내 성격을 이해 못 하네? 난 먼저 밧줄을 끊고, 그다음 멍하니 있거나 발끈한 널 느긋하게 베어 버릴 거야.<end>"

## 560764
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "力があれば何をやってもいい。<line-break>裏返せば、力が足りないなら<line-break>黙ってろってこと。わかったぁ？<line-break>今回、あたしはあんたとあのコたちを<line-break>両方、殺すこともできた。<line-break>だけど、そうしなかった。<end>"
EN: "If you have power, you may do whatever you please. Put another way, if you lack the power, keep your mouth shut. Understand? I could have killed both you and that whole group this time. But I chose not to.<end>"
KO: "힘이 있으면 뭘 해도 돼. 뒤집어 말하면 힘이 부족하면 닥치라는 뜻이야. 알겠어? 이번에 난 너와 그 애들을 둘 다 죽일 수도 있었어. 하지만 그러지 않았지.<end>"

## 560765
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だって、もったいないんだもん。<line-break>アイリーンもあのコも、さ。<line-break> <line-break>そんなあたしの気持ち、理解してよ。<line-break>強くなってさ、あたしの寝首くらいは<line-break>かけるようになってほしいなーってさ。<end>"
EN: "Because it would've been such a waste―Irene and that one both. Try to understand how I feel. I want you to get strong enough to slit my throat in my sleep someday.<end>"
KO: "아깝잖아. 아이린도 그 애도 말이야. 그런 내 마음 좀 이해해 줘. 강해져서 내 목 정도는 노릴 수 있게 됐으면 좋겠거든.<end>"

## 560766
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あと、捨て身の攻撃っていうの？<line-break>あれ、あんた、もう禁止ね。<line-break>自棄になれば判断力が狂うわ。<line-break>それに、捨て身の攻撃って、<line-break>掛け値なしに<line-break>一生に１回だけのものよ。<end>"
EN: "And those suicidal attacks of yours? No more. Desperation clouds your judgment. Besides, a suicide attack is, quite literally, something you can only do once in your life.<end>"
KO: "그리고 목숨을 건 공격이라는 거? 그거 이제 금지야. 자포자기하면 판단력이 흐려져. 게다가 목숨을 거는 공격은 말 그대로 평생 한 번뿐인 거라고.<end>"

## 560767
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが、大切な幼なじみを<line-break>救うためでもね。<end>"
EN: "Even if it's to save a precious childhood friend.<end>"
KO: "그게 소중한 소꿉친구를 구하기 위한 일이라 해도 말이야.<end>"

## 560768
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "前に一度説教したと思うけど、<line-break>彼は彼なりに、ちゃ～んと、<line-break>自分のセキニンで動けるのよ。<end>"
EN: "I believe I lectured you about this once before, but he can make his own choices and take responsibility for them.<end>"
KO: "전에 한 번 설교한 것 같은데, 그는 그 나름대로 제대로 자기 책임으로 움직일 수 있어.<end>"

## 560769
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "命より大切なものなんてないの。<line-break>守りたいものがあるなら<line-break>なおさら、ね。<end>"
EN: "Nothing is more precious than life. Especially when there's something you want to protect.<end>"
KO: "목숨보다 소중한 건 없어. 지키고 싶은 게 있다면 더더욱, 알겠지.<end>"

## 560770
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、知らないと思ったぁ？<line-break>愛する人のことはな～んでも知りたい。<line-break>これも、アイリーンへの愛ゆえよん。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Did you think I didn't know? I want to know everything about the one I love. That's how much I love Irene.<end>"
KO: "후훗, 모를 줄 알았어? 사랑하는 사람 일이라면 뭐든 알고 싶잖아. 이것도 아이린을 향한 사랑 때문이지~.<end>"

## 560771
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "命より大切なものなんてないの。<line-break>守りたいものがあるなら<line-break>なおさら、ね。<end>"
EN: "Nothing is more precious than life. Especially when there's something you want to protect.<end>"
KO: "목숨보다 소중한 건 없어. 지키고 싶은 게 있다면 더더욱, 알겠지.<end>"

## 560773
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "命より大切なものなんてないの。<line-break>守りたいものがあるなら<line-break>なおさら、ね。<end>"
EN: "Nothing is more precious than life. Especially when there's something you want to protect.<end>"
KO: "목숨보다 소중한 건 없어. 지키고 싶은 게 있다면 더더욱, 알겠지.<end>"

## 560774
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しっかし、いー度胸してんじゃない。<line-break>このカルラ様をふっちゃうなんてさ。<end>"
EN: "Still, you've got nerve, turning down the great Karula.<end>"
KO: "그래도 배짱 하나는 좋네. 이 칼라 님을 차 버리다니.<end>"

## 560775
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さんが<line-break>ご無事でよかったです。<line-break>　<line-break>偶然<value:$28>さんたちの<line-break>動きを知って、レイヴンに<line-break>無理を聞いてもらったんです。<end>"
EN: "I'm so glad you're safe, <value:$28>. I happened to find out what <value:$28> and the others were doing, so I asked Raven to do me a rather unreasonable favor.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님이 무사하셔서 다행입니다. 우연히 <value:$28>님 일행의 움직임을 알고 레이븐에게 무리한 부탁을 했어요.<end>"

## 560776
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、ノエルの無理なお願いには<line-break>もう、ならされてるさ。<line-break>ただ、気をつけたほうがいいぜ。<line-break>俺たちにも聞こえてくるぐらいだ。<line-break>解放軍の情報はどこかで漏れてる。<line-break>内部に裏切り者がいる可能性が高い。<end>"
EN: "Heh. I'm used to Noel's unreasonable requests by now. But you'd better be careful. If word has reached even us, information about the Liberation Army is leaking somewhere. There's probably a traitor on the inside.<end>"
KO: "후후, 노엘의 무리한 부탁에는 이제 익숙해졌어. 다만 조심하는 게 좋아. 우리 귀에까지 들어올 정도니까. 해방군 정보가 어딘가에서 새고 있어. 내부에 배신자가 있을 가능성이 높아.<end>"

## 560777
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行こうぜ、ノエル。<line-break>カフィンは待たせると<line-break>ホント、うるさいからな。<end>"
EN: "Let's go, Noel. Coffin really raises a fuss if you keep her waiting.<end>"
KO: "가자, 노엘. 카핀은 기다리게 하면 정말 시끄럽거든.<end>"

## 560778
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すいません、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>みんなが待ってるんで、<line-break>わたし、行きますね。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, <value:$28>. Everyone is waiting, so I should go.<end>"
KO: "죄송해요, <value:$28>님. 모두 기다리고 있어서, 저는 갈게요.<end>"

## 560779
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱり、<value:$28>さんは<line-break>すごいです。解放軍の方たちも<line-break>無事に戻られたようですよ。<end>"
EN: "You really are amazing, <value:$28>. It seems the members of the Liberation Army made it back safely too.<end>"
KO: "역시 <value:$28>님은 대단해요. 해방군 분들도 무사히 돌아온 것 같아요.<end>"

## 560780
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おぉ、<value:$28>だ。<line-break><value:$28>が帰ってきたぞ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, it's <value:$28>! <value:$28> is back!<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>다. <value:$28>가 돌아왔다!<end>"

## 560781
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すごいな。町の中はカルラ軍が  <line-break>ぎっしり詰めていたというのに、<line-break>よく無事に帰ってこれたものだ。<end>"
EN: "Incredible. The town was packed with Karula's Army, yet you still made it back safely.<end>"
KO: "대단하군. 마을 안에 카를라군이 빽빽하게 있었는데도 무사히 돌아오다니.<end>"

## 560782
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたが鳴らしてくれた<line-break>鐘の音がなかったら、<line-break><line-break>俺たちは今頃、カルラ軍の餌食になって<line-break>全滅していたところだ。ありがとう！<end>"
EN: "If you hadn't rung that bell, Karula's Army would have wiped us all out by now. Thank you!<end>"
KO: "네가 울려 준 종소리가 없었다면 우리는 지금쯤 카를라군에게 당해 전멸했을 거야. 고맙다!<end>"

## 560783
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご苦労様でした、<value:$28>。  <line-break>あなたの機転のおかげで、被害を<line-break>最小限に食い止めることができました。<line-break>本当に、ありがとう。みんなが<line-break>あなたの帰りを待っていました。<line-break>あなたは、私たち解放軍の英雄です。<end>"
EN: "Well done, <value:$28>. Thanks to your quick thinking, we managed to keep our losses to a minimum. Thank you, truly. Everyone has been waiting for your return. You are the hero of our Liberation Army.<end>"
KO: "수고하셨습니다, <value:$28>. 당신의 기지 덕분에 피해를 최소한으로 막을 수 있었습니다. 정말 고마워요. 모두가 당신의 귀환을 기다리고 있었습니다. 당신은 우리 해방군의 영웅입니다.<end>"

## 560784
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセンの鐘の英雄、<value:$28>。<line-break>これからも、よろしく頼みますよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, hero of the Bell of Rocen. We will be counting on you in the days ahead.<end>"
KO: "로센의 종의 영웅, <value:$28>. 앞으로도 잘 부탁합니다.<end>"

## 560785
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺たちにはロセンの鐘の英雄が<line-break>ついている。<line-break>カルラ軍に目にものを見せてやるぞ！<end>"
EN: "The hero of the Bell of Rocen is with us. We'll show Karula's Army what we're made of!<end>"
KO: "우리에게는 로센의 종 영웅이 있다. 칼라군에게 본때를 보여 주자!<end>"

## 560786
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度こそは、ロセンから<line-break>ディンガルの奴らを追い出してやろうぜ！<end>"
EN: "This time, let's drive those Dyneskal bastards out of Rocen!<end>"
KO: "이번에야말로 로센에서 딘갈 놈들을 몰아내자!<end>"

## 560787
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ！<line-break>俺たちの力をやつらに思い知らせて<line-break>やろうぜ！<end>"
EN: "Yeah! Let's show them what we can do!<end>"
KO: "그래! 우리 힘을 놈들에게 똑똑히 보여 주자고!<end>"

## 560788
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、各人、持ち場に戻ってください。<line-break>事態は、いよいよ厳しくなっています。<line-break>決して、油断のないように。<end>"
EN: "Now, everyone, return to your posts. The situation is growing increasingly grave. Do not let your guard down.<end>"
KO: "그럼 각자 자리로 돌아가 주세요. 사태는 점점 심각해지고 있습니다. 절대 방심하지 마십시오.<end>"

## 560789
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当に…、よく…              <line-break>無事で、戻ってきてくれました…。<end>"
EN: "I'm truly... so glad... you made it back safely.<end>"
KO: "정말… 무사히… 돌아와 줬군요….<end>"

## 560790
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、あんな無理はさせません。  <line-break>だから、あなたも、あんなムチャは<line-break>もう、しないでください…。<end>"
EN: "I won't let you push yourself like that again. So please, don't do anything so reckless again...<end>"
KO: "이제 그런 무리는 시키지 않겠습니다. 그러니 당신도 그런 무모한 짓은 이제 하지 말아 주세요….<end>"

## 560791
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とにかく、<line-break>あなたが無事で何よりでした。        <line-break>非常に、心配していたのです。<line-break>カ、カン違いしないでいただきたいわ。<line-break>あくまで私の指揮で、大事な同志を<line-break>失いたくないから、そう思ったのです！<end>"
EN: "In any case, I'm simply glad you're safe. I was terribly worried. D-don't get the wrong idea! I only felt that way because I don't want to lose a valued comrade under my command!<end>"
KO: "아무튼 당신이 무사해서 무엇보다 다행입니다. 정말 걱정했어요. 차, 착각하지 마세요. 어디까지나 제 지휘 아래 소중한 동지를 잃고 싶지 않아서 그런 겁니다!<end>"

## 560792
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>様。<line-break>あなたをお通しする<line-break>わけにはまいりません。<end>"
EN: "…<value:$28>. I cannot allow you to pass.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28> 님. 당신을 들여보낼 수는 없습니다.<end>"

## 560793
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたがディンガル軍に<line-break>加わったことは<line-break>こちらにも伝わっています。<end>"
EN: "Word has reached us that you have joined the Dyneskal Army.<end>"
KO: "당신이 딘갈군에 가담했다는 소식은 이쪽에도 전해졌습니다.<end>"

## 560794
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "屋敷に入って、<line-break>解放軍の人に見つかったら、<line-break>必ず殺されてしまいます。<line-break>クリュセイス様は、あなたが<line-break>殺されるのを見たくはないのです。<line-break>…どうぞ、お察しください。<end>"
EN: "If you enter the mansion and are discovered by the Liberation Army, you will surely be killed. Lady Khryuseis does not wish to see that happen. Please understand.<end>"
KO: "저택에 들어갔다가 해방군 사람들에게 발각되면 반드시 살해당합니다. 크류세이스 님은 당신이 죽는 모습을 보고 싶지 않은 겁니다. …부디 헤아려 주세요.<end>"

## 560795
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part98.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "キャー！<end>"
EN: "Aah!<end>"
KO: "꺄악!<end>"

## 560797
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちっ！<end>"
EN: "Damn!<end>"
KO: "칫!<end>"

## 560798
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、まって<line-break>深追いしては…<line-break> <end>"
EN: "Wait! Don't chase the assassin too far...<end>"
KO: "아, 기다려. 너무 깊이 쫓아가면….<end>"

## 560799
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>！<line-break>どうして追っていったのです！<line-break>待ち伏せにでもあったら、いったい…！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! Why did you go after the assassin? What if you'd run into an ambush...?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>! 왜 쫓아간 겁니까! 매복이라도 당하면 어쩌려고요…!<end>"

## 560800
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なんとおっしゃったの！？<line-break>暗殺者を、おじさまの家の前で<line-break>見失った、ですって？<end>"
EN: "What did you just say?! You lost the assassin outside Uncle's house?<end>"
KO: "…뭐라고 하셨어요!? 암살자를 아저씨 집 앞에서 놓쳤다고요?<end>"

## 560801
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おじさまはお父様の親友です。<line-break>お父様が亡くなる前にも後にも<line-break>わたくしたちを助けてくださいました。<line-break>そのおじさまが解放軍を裏切る…<line-break>いえ、わたくしを襲うようなマネを<line-break>するはずがありません。<end>"
EN: "Uncle was a close friend of Father's. He helped us both before and after Father's death. He would never betray the Liberation Army―no, he would never do anything like attack me.<end>"
KO: "아저씨는 아버님의 절친한 친구입니다. 아버님이 돌아가시기 전에도 후에도 저희를 도와주셨습니다. 그런 아저씨가 해방군을 배신한다니… 아니, 저를 습격하는 짓을 할 리가 없습니다.<end>"

## 560802
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "解放軍の士気に関わります。<line-break>軽はずみな言動はつつしんで<line-break>いただきたいわ。おわかり？<end>"
EN: "This could affect the Liberation Army's morale. I must ask you to be more circumspect in what you say and do. Is that clear?<end>"
KO: "해방군의 사기에 관련된 문제입니다. 경솔한 언행은 삼가 주세요. 아시겠죠?<end>"

## 560803
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ、わたくしはあなたのことを<line-break>責めているつもりではなく、ただ…<end>"
EN: "I-I didn't mean to blame you. I only...<end>"
KO: "저, 저는 당신을 탓하려는 게 아니라, 그저….<end>"

## 560804
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助けてもらったお礼も言わないで<line-break>あんな事を言うなんて…。<end>"
EN: "How could I say such things without even thanking you for saving me...?<end>"
KO: "도움을 받고도 감사 인사도 없이 그런 말을 하다니….<end>"

## 560805
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうして…<line-break>どうして素直になれないのかしら。<end>"
EN: "Why... why can't I just be honest?<end>"
KO: "어째서… 왜 솔직해질 수 없는 걸까.<end>"

## 560806
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様。　　<line-break>どうぞ、お入りください。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. Please, come in.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님. 들어오십시오.<end>"

## 560807
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセンに潜入する作戦が失敗したのは　<line-break>残念だけど、これで、解放軍内での<line-break>あなたの立場は確立したわ。<line-break>あなたの活躍のおかげで<line-break>解放軍の結束はより堅固なものに<line-break>なるはずです。<end>"
EN: "It's a shame the operation to infiltrate Rocen failed, but it has secured your position within the Liberation Army. Thanks to your efforts, the army should be more united than ever.<end>"
KO: "로센에 잠입하는 작전이 실패한 건 아쉽지만 이걸로 해방군 안에서 당신의 입지는 확립됐어요. 당신의 활약 덕분에 해방군의 결속은 더 굳건해질 겁니다.<end>"

## 560808
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたはロセンの鐘の英雄なのだから、<line-break>これからも解放軍の支柱となって<line-break>働いてもらいます。<line-break>ロセンの復興のために。<end>"
EN: "You are the hero of the Bell of Rocen, after all. I expect you to continue serving as a mainstay of the Liberation Army―for the rebuilding of Rocen.<end>"
KO: "당신은 로센의 종의 영웅이니 앞으로도 해방군의 기둥으로 일해 주셔야 합니다. 로센의 부흥을 위해서요.<end>"

## 560809
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご苦労様。            <line-break>解放軍の居心地はどう？<end>"
EN: "Good work. How are you finding life in the Liberation Army?<end>"
KO: "수고했어. 해방군은 지낼 만해?<end>"

## 560810
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "みんな、あなたのような存在が　　　<line-break>現れるのを待っていたんだと思うわ。<end>"
EN: "I think everyone had been waiting for someone like you to come along.<end>"
KO: "모두 당신 같은 존재가 나타나길 기다렸던 것 같아.<end>"

## 560811
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わたくしにはあなたのように          <line-break>前線で働くことはできないもの。<line-break>そんなことをしても、迷惑になるわ。<line-break>みんな、どこかに不安を抱えているわ。<line-break>だから、あなたが裏切ったと思ったとき<line-break>あのような大騒ぎになったのね。<end>"
EN: "I can't serve on the front lines the way you do. If I tried, I'd only get in everyone's way. Everyone carries some measure of doubt or fear. That's why there was such an uproar when they thought you had betrayed us.<end>"
KO: "저는 당신처럼 전선에서 일할 수 없어요. 그래 봐야 방해만 되겠죠. 모두가 어딘가에 불안을 품고 있어요. 그래서 당신이 배신했다고 생각했을 때 그렇게 큰 소동이 벌어진 거겠죠.<end>"

## 560812
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、それは、みんながあなたを<line-break>頼りたかったからだと思う。<line-break>みんなが…。<end>"
EN: "But I think that was only because everyone wanted to rely on you. Everyone...<end>"
KO: "하지만 그건 모두가 당신을 의지하고 싶었기 때문이라고 생각해. 모두가….<end>"

## 560813
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "例の件ですが…。<end>"
EN: "About that matter...<end>"
KO: "그 건 말입니다….<end>"

## 560814
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…アンティノおじさまの家の前で<line-break>暗殺者が消えたとあなたが言った件。<end>"
EN: "You said the assassin disappeared outside Uncle Antino's house.<end>"
KO: "…안티노 아저씨 집 앞에서 암살자가 사라졌다고 당신이 말했던 건.<end>"

## 560815
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今から、アンティノおじさまの所へ<line-break>行って話し合いましょう。<line-break>そうすれば解決することだわ。<line-break>解放軍の有力者であるおじさまと<line-break>ロセンの鐘の英雄が対立していては<line-break>解放軍の人事上、不都合ですから。<end>"
EN: "Let's go speak with Uncle Antino now. That should settle the matter. Uncle is a leading figure in the Liberation Army, and having him at odds with the hero of the Bell of Rocen would complicate the Army's personnel affairs.<end>"
KO: "지금부터 안티노 아저씨에게 가서 이야기합시다. 그러면 해결될 일이에요. 해방군의 유력자인 아저씨와 로센의 종 영웅이 대립하고 있어서는 해방군 인사상 곤란하니까요.<end>"

## 560816
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "決して、あなたの言うことを<line-break>鵜呑みにしたわけじゃなくてよ。<line-break>そこのところ、理解してほしいわ。<end>"
EN: "That does not mean I have simply taken you at your word. I want you to understand that.<end>"
KO: "절대 당신 말을 곧이곧대로 믿은 건 아니에요. 그 점은 이해해 줬으면 해요.<end>"

## 560817
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い、一応、理解していただけたようね。<line-break>それじゃ、アンティノおじさまの<line-break>屋敷へ向かいましょう？<end>"
EN: "I-I believe you understand, at least. Shall we go to Uncle Antino's estate, then?<end>"
KO: "이, 일단 이해해 준 모양이네요. 그럼 안티노 아저씨 저택으로 가죠?<end>"

## 560818
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンティノおじさま、<line-break>突然、お訪ねして、すみません。<line-break>ひとつお尋ねしたいことがあって…。<end>"
EN: "Uncle Antino, forgive me for coming without warning. There is something I would like to ask you...<end>"
KO: "안티노 아저씨, 갑자기 찾아와서 죄송해요. 하나 여쭤볼 게 있어서요….<end>"

## 560819
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんでも答えよう。<line-break>お前は我が親友ロティの娘だからな。<line-break>どうしたのだ、クリュセイス？<end>"
EN: "Ask me anything. You are the daughter of my dear friend Lottie, after all. What is it, Khryuseis?<end>"
KO: "무엇이든 답해 주지. 넌 내 절친 로티의 딸이니까. 무슨 일이냐, 크류세이스?<end>"

## 560820
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…おじさま。<line-break>先日、わたくしは何者かに<line-break>命を狙われました。<line-break>幸いにも<value:$28>が<line-break>かけつけてくれたので、<line-break>無事でしたが…。<end>"
EN: "Uncle... Someone tried to kill me the other day. Fortunately, <value:$28> came to my aid, so I was unharmed, but...<end>"
KO: "…아저씨. 며칠 전 저는 누군가에게 목숨을 위협받았습니다. 다행히 <value:$28>이 달려와 줘서 무사했지만….<end>"

## 560821
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、<value:$28>が<line-break>助けたのか…。<end>"
EN: "I see. So <value:$28> saved you...<end>"
KO: "그렇군, <value:$28>이 구한 건가….<end>"

## 560822
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec056-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その<value:$28>が言うのです。<line-break>暗殺者のあとを追っていったが<line-break>おじさまの屋敷の前で見失った、と。<line-break>これは、どういうことなのでしょう。<line-break>おじさま、何かおっしゃってください。<line-break>このままでは…<end>"
EN: "But <value:$28> says, “I gave chase, but lost sight of the assassin outside your estate.” What am I to make of that? Please, Uncle, say something. If you do not...<end>"
KO: "그 <value:$28>가 말하길, 암살자를 뒤쫓았지만 아저씨의 저택 앞에서 놓쳤다고 했습니다. 이게 대체 무슨 일인가요. 아저씨, 뭐라도 말씀해 주세요. 이대로라면…<end>"
